### [1] [A Geometry-Informed Computer Vision Method for Detecting and Examining Overtaking Vehicles From A Bicycle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23699)
*Gandhimathi Padmanaban,Rayane Moustafa,Fred Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于几何约束的计算机视觉流程，仅用单个自行车车载摄像头即可自动检测超车事件，无需多传感器或显式标定，在315个真实事件中达到97.8%召回率和零误报，并实现了无标定的横向距离估计。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统从自行车后视视频中提取超车事件依赖人工逐帧标注，效率低下，严重制约了样本量和自然场景下的骑行安全研究。

Method: 结合RT-DETR目标检测与ByteTrack多目标跟踪，设计了三阶段几何验证模块（方位角趋势、表观尺寸增长、空间确认），基于透视投影原理自动识别超车事件，并利用边界框几何特征实现无标定横向距离估计。

Result: 在315个真实超车事件上达到97.8%召回率和零误报；事件识别平均领先车辆通过2.44秒，84.1%超过1.5秒人类反应阈值；横向距离测量中33.3%低于5英尺安全阈值；无标定距离估计平均绝对误差13-14厘米。

Conclusion: 该流程消除了仪器自行车研究中事件提取的标注瓶颈，为大规模城市环境中车辆-自行车交互分析提供了可扩展的基础，并具备主动骑行预警的可行性。

Original Abstract: Instrumented bicycle studies have produced direct field evidence on vehicle passing behavior, but extracting overtaking events from continuous rear-facing video has remained dependent on manual, frame-by-frame annotation. This bottleneck constrains sample sizes and limits naturalistic cycling safety research. We present a geometry-informed computer vision pipeline that automates overtaking event detection from a single bicycle-mounted camera without multi-sensor configurations or explicit camera calibration. The system combines RT-DETR object detection with ByteTrack multi-object tracking through a three-stage geometric validation module enforcing bearing angle trend, apparent size growth, and spatial confirmation criteria derived from perspective projection principles. Validated on 315 manually annotated real-world overtaking events from urban roads in Ann Arbor, Michigan, the pipeline achieved 97.8% recall with zero false positives. The system identified overtaking intentions a mean of 2.44 seconds before vehicle passage, with 84.1% of events exceeding the 1.5-second human reaction time threshold, demonstrating feasibility for active cyclist warning. Lateral passing distance measurements from 96 events revealed 33.3% of passes below the 5-foot (152.4 cm) threshold, consistent with non-compliance rates in prior field and self-reported studies. A preliminary calibration-free lateral distance estimation approach using bounding box geometric features achieved mean absolute errors of 13-14 cm under leave-one-out cross-validation, sufficient to distinguish close passes from standard passes for safety categorization. By automating event isolation from consumer-grade footage, the system removes the primary annotation bottleneck of instrumented bicycle research and provides a scalable foundation for vehicle-bicycle interaction analysis across larger datasets and diverse urban environments.

中文摘要翻译: 仪器自行车研究已为车辆超车行为提供了直接的现场证据，但从连续后视视频中提取超车事件仍依赖于人工逐帧标注。这一瓶颈制约了样本量，限制了自然场景下的骑行安全研究。我们提出了一种基于几何约束的计算机视觉流程，可在无需多传感器配置或显式相机标定的情况下，仅凭单个自行车车载摄像头自动检测超车事件。该系统结合RT-DETR目标检测与ByteTrack多目标跟踪，通过一个三阶段几何验证模块施加方位角趋势、表观尺寸增长和空间确认准则——这些准则均源自透视投影原理。在密歇根州安娜堡市城市道路上315个手动标注的真实超车事件上进行验证，该流程实现了97.8%的召回率且零误报。系统在车辆通过前平均2.44秒识别到超车意图，其中84.1%的事件超过1.5秒的人类反应时间阈值，证明了主动骑行预警的可行性。对96个事件的横向通过距离测量显示，33.3%的通过距离低于5英尺（152.4厘米）的安全阈值，与先前实地研究和自我报告研究中的不达标率一致。一种利用边界框几何特征的初步无标定横向距离估计方法在留一交叉验证下实现了13-14厘米的平均绝对误差，足以区分近距离超车与标准超车以进行安全分类。通过从消费级视频素材中自动提取事件，该系统消除了仪器自行车研究的主要标注瓶颈，为更大数据集和多样城市环境中的车辆-自行车交互分析提供了可扩展的基础。

</details>


### [2] [Sol Video Inference Engine: Agent-Native Full-Stack Acceleration Framework for Efficient Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23743)
*Yitong Li,Junsong Chen,Haopeng Li,Haozhe Liu,Jincheng Yu,Ligeng Zhu,Ping Luo,Song Han,Enze Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sol Video Inference Engine：一个无需训练、智能体驱动的视频扩散模型加速框架，通过并行技能智能体对缓存、稀疏注意力、令牌剪枝、量化和内核融合五种技术进行实例特定优化，在多种模型和硬件上实现2倍以上端到端加速且质量无损。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现代视频扩散模型随规模扩大推理成本急剧增加，而最有效的加速策略高度依赖于具体实例（模型架构、数值敏感性、注意力模式差异；推理配置的空间/时间分辨率差异；硬件的内存层次、数值格式和吞吐量差异），手动性能优化成本高昂且难以迁移。

Method: 提出Sol Video Inference Engine，一个智能体驱动的原生无训练加速框架。将五种技术（缓存、稀疏注意力、令牌剪枝、量化、内核融合）组织为加速栈，通过并行技能智能体分别优化每种技术的实现，再由智能体集成器组合成全局加速栈，最后由人类验证器提供生成质量反馈，实现面向具体部署目标的实例特定优化。

Result: 在三种不同规模和架构的视频模型（64B Cosmos3-Super、22B LTX-2.3、2B SANA-Video）上，以极少人工成本实现超过2倍端到端加速，同时保持接近无损的VBench质量。

Conclusion: 该智能体框架有效解决了视频扩散模型加速策略的实例特定性问题，展示了在多种模型和硬件上实现高效、质量无损加速的通用能力。

Original Abstract: Modern video diffusion models achieve higher generation quality through scaling, but this also increases inference cost. Although many acceleration methods have been proposed, a central challenge is that the most effective acceleration strategy is highly instance-specific: a recipe that works well for one combination of model, hardware, and inference configuration often does not transfer to another. Different models vary in architecture, numerical sensitivity, and attention concentration patterns. Inference settings differ in spatial and temporal resolution and video duration, while hardware platforms differ in memory hierarchy, supported numerical formats, and kernel throughput. These factors create a large tuning space, making manual performance engineering costly. We present Sol Video Inference Engine, an agentic, native, training-free acceleration framework for video diffusion models. It organizes five broadly applicable techniques, cache, sparse attention, token pruning, quantization, and kernel fusion, into an agentic acceleration stack for instance-specific optimization. For a concrete deployment target defined by a model, hardware platform, and serving configuration, parallel skill agents optimize the implementation of each technique, an agent integrator composes them into a global acceleration stack, and a human validator provides feedback on generation quality. We instantiate this workflow on three video models with different sizes and architectures: 64B Cosmos3-Super, 22B LTX-2.3, and 2B SANA-Video. With little human effort, the full stack achieves more than 2x end-to-end acceleration while maintaining near-lossless VBench quality, demonstrating the effectiveness of the agent framework for video diffusion acceleration.

中文摘要翻译: 现代视频扩散模型通过扩大规模实现了更高的生成质量，但这也增加了推理成本。尽管已提出许多加速方法，但一个核心挑战在于最有效的加速策略高度实例特定：适用于某一模型、硬件和推理配置组合的方案往往难以迁移到其他组合。不同模型在架构、数值敏感性和注意力集中模式上存在差异；推理设置在空间和时间分辨率以及视频时长上有所不同；而硬件平台在内存层次结构、支持的数值格式和内核吞吐量方面也各不相同。这些因素构成了巨大的调优空间，使手动性能工程成本高昂。我们提出了Sol Video Inference Engine，一个智能体驱动的、原生的、无需训练的扩散模型加速框架。它将五种广泛适用的技术——缓存、稀疏注意力、令牌剪枝、量化和内核融合——组织成一个智能体加速栈，用于实例特定优化。针对由模型、硬件平台和服务配置定义的具体部署目标，并行技能智能体优化每种技术的实现，智能体集成器将它们组合成全局加速栈，人类验证器提供生成质量反馈。我们在三种不同规模和架构的视频模型上实例化此工作流：64B Cosmos3-Super、22B LTX-2.3和2B SANA-Video。在极少人工投入下，完整加速栈实现了超过2倍的端到端加速，同时保持接近无损的VBench质量，展示了该智能体框架在视频扩散加速方面的有效性。

</details>


### [3] [Listening makes Vision Clear for VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23763)
*Yiyang Chen,Yixin Tan,Binrui Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对视觉-语言一致性评估中答案侧注意力分布存在的解码漂移和结构标记干扰问题，提出基于提示侧语义的PV-TAM方法，通过注意力峰值分布衡量提示与视觉区域的对齐程度，在多个数据集上显著优于传统方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法使用答案侧（answer-side）标记的注意力分布评估视觉-语言一致性，但存在两个问题：(1) 解码漂移（decoding drift）：先前生成答案 token 的语言先验累积，导致最高注意力区域与预期语义 token 不匹配；(2) 结构标记（如模态边界标记）会囊括整个上下文，对无关区域产生高注意力。因此需要避免这些扭曲，为大视觉语言模型提供一致性评估。

Method: 提出提示-视觉 Token 激活图（PV-TAM），核心包括：(1) 采用提示侧（prompt-side）语义代替答案侧语义；(2) 引入过滤器去除模态边界标记引起的系统偏差；(3) 利用注意力峰值分布（而非传统仅通过掩码评估重叠而忽略激活强度）来衡量提示与视觉区域之间的对齐程度。

Result: 在多个数据集上，PV-TAM在基于注意力的定位指标和IoU风格的定位指标上均一致地优于答案侧基线方法。

Conclusion: PV-TAM通过使用提示侧语义和去除模态边界标记偏差，有效避免了答案侧注意力分布中的解码漂移和结构标记干扰，为大型视觉语言模型提供了更可靠的视觉-语言一致性评估方法。

Original Abstract: Recent work typically assesses vision--language consistency using attention distributions of answer-side tokens. However, we observe that highest attention regions are not always consistent with the intended semantic token. This probably stems from decoding drift, where language priors from previously generated answer tokens accumulate and mismatch with visual attention. Besides the priors from previous answer tokens, we find that structural tokens, e.g., modality boundary markers, may encompass the entire context and generate high attention to areas unrelated to the target. To avoid these distortions and provide consistency evaluation for large VLMs, we adopt prompt-side semantics and propose Prompt-Vision Token Activation Map (PV-TAM). PV-TAM further incorporates a filter to remove systematic bias induced by modality boundary markers. Unlike traditional methods that evaluate overlap solely through masks while ignoring activation intensity, our metrics leverage the peak distribution of attention to measure the alignment between prompts and visual regions. In experiments, PV-TAM consistently improves both attention-based and IoU-style localization metrics over answer-side baselines on various datasets.

中文摘要翻译: 近期工作通常使用答案侧（answer-side）标记的注意力分布来评估视觉-语言一致性。然而，我们观察到最高注意力区域并不总是与预期的语义标记一致。这很可能源于解码漂移（decoding drift），即先前生成的答案 token 的语言先验累积，与视觉注意力不匹配。除了先前答案 token 的先验之外，我们发现结构标记（如模态边界标记）可能囊括整个上下文，并对与目标无关的区域产生高注意力。为避免这些扭曲并为大型视觉语言模型（VLMs）提供一致性评估，我们采用提示侧（prompt-side）语义，并提出提示-视觉 Token 激活图（Prompt-Vision Token Activation Map, PV-TAM）。PV-TAM进一步引入过滤器，以消除由模态边界标记引起的系统性偏差。与仅通过掩码评估重叠而忽略激活强度的传统方法不同，我们的指标利用注意力的峰值分布来衡量提示与视觉区域之间的对齐程度。在实验中，PV-TAM在多个数据集上相较于答案侧基线方法，在基于注意力的定位指标和IoU风格的定位指标上均取得了一致性提升。

</details>


### [4] [From Spatial to Spectral: An Efficient, Frequency-Guided Feature Representation Learner for Small Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23825)
*Yuhan Rui,Shihan Qiao,Yibin Lou,Mingxi Yu,Yutong Wan,Yanqiao Chen,Dongsheng Hou,Zhen Cao,Athena Zhuoming Zhong,Qi Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出从空间域转向频谱域的范式，通过频率引导的特征表示框架和轻量级DER算子（WDG、LGE、FDHead），在不增加参数量的情况下有效恢复小目标的高频细节，DERNet以1/6参数超越YOLOv11。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 小目标检测受限于目标本身的特征稀疏性，而空间域检测器会不可分辨地丢弃关键高频细节。在空间域恢复这些脆弱线索通常需要昂贵的架构放大，反而会放大背景噪声。

Method: 提出频率引导特征表示框架，包含三个即插即用的轻量模块：小波差分门控(WDG)、对数Gabor增强器(LGE)和频率驱动检测头(FDHead)，分别注入骨干、颈部和头部，通过分解-增强-重构(DER)算子实现频率感知调制。

Result: 在VisDrone2019、UAVDT、TinyPerson、DOTAv1等多领域基准上取得一致提升；DERNet系列在相同尺度下仅需YOLOv11的1/6参数量即实现超越。

Conclusion: 将特征处理从空间域转向频谱域是小目标检测的有效范式，轻量级频率感知模块能以极低参数代价显著提升检测性能。

Original Abstract: Efficient small object detection is bottlenecked by the inherent feature scarcity of tiny targets, which is further aggravated by operations of spatial-domain detectors that indiscriminately discard critical high-frequency details. Recovering these fragile cues within the spatial domain is notoriously difficult, as it often requires computationally expensive architectural upscaling that inadvertently amplifies background noise. To bridge this gap, we propose a paradigm \textbf{shift from spatial to spectral} feature processing, introducing a holistic solution with the following novelty: (1) A versatile \textbf{Frequency-Guided Feature Representation framework} that generalizes across diverse detector architectures (both CNN and Transformer-based), offering a robust alternative to spatial-only feature extraction; (2) The unified \textbf{Decompose--Enhance--Reconstruct (DER)} operator, instantiated via three \textbf{lightweight, plug-and-play} modules -- Wavelet-Difference Gate (WDG), Log-Gabor Enhancer (LGE), and Frequency-Driven Head (FDHead) -- to systematically inject frequency-aware modulation into the backbone, neck, and head. This mechanism decouples feature modeling from resolution reduction, capturing discriminative high-frequency components to enable accurate localization with significantly reduced parameter redundancy; (3) Extensive validation on multi-domain benchmarks (VisDrone2019, UAVDT, TinyPerson, DOTAv1) demonstrating consistent gains. Notably, our proposed \textbf{DERNet} series outperforms YOLOv11 models under the same scale while requiring \textbf{only 1/6 of the parameters}, backed by rigorous spectral diagnostics and error decomposition analysis.

中文摘要翻译: 高效的小目标检测受困于小目标固有的特征稀疏性，而空间域检测器的操作会不可分辨地丢弃关键的高频细节，进一步加剧了这一瓶颈。在空间域内恢复这些脆弱线索极为困难，因为这通常需要计算昂贵的架构放大，反而会无意中放大背景噪声。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了一种从空间域转向频谱域的特征处理范式，并引入了一个整体解决方案，其创新点如下：(1) 一种通用的频率引导特征表示框架，可推广至多种检测器架构（包括CNN和基于Transformer的模型），为纯空间特征提取提供了鲁棒替代方案；(2) 统一的分解-增强-重构(DER)算子，通过三个轻量级即插即用模块——小波差分门控(WDG)、对数Gabor增强器(LGE)和频率驱动检测头(FDHead)来实例化，系统性地将频率感知调制注入骨干网络、颈部和检测头。该机制将特征建模与分辨率降低解耦，捕获判别性的高频分量，从而实现精准定位且参数冗余显著降低；(3) 在多领域基准（VisDrone2019、UAVDT、TinyPerson、DOTAv1）上的广泛验证表明了一致的性能提升。值得注意的是，我们提出的DERNet系列在相同尺度下优于YOLOv11模型，且仅需其1/6的参数量，该结论得到了严格的频谱诊断和误差分解分析的支持。

</details>


### [5] [ABACUS: Adapting Unified Foundation Model for Bridging Image Count Understanding and Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23835)
*Anindya Mondal,Sauradip Nag,Anjan Dutta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ABACUS是一个统一的视觉-语言模型，无需基准特定训练即可处理物体计数、人群计数、指代表达计数和计数忠实图像生成，通过密度感知自适应缩放、边界感知计数策略和循环一致性GRPO三项创新，在七个基准上达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有统一视觉-语言模型在计数任务上表现不佳，且不同计数任务（物体计数、人群计数、指代表达计数）通常需要专门的模型和训练。此外，理解和生成任务之间存在差距，缺乏一个统一的框架能够同时处理计数相关理解和生成任务。

Method: 基于现有3B参数统一基础模型，采用三项关键创新：1) 密度感知自适应缩放与物体性图（objectness maps）实现空间定位；2) 基于GRPO的边界感知计数策略消除裁剪边界错误；3) 循环一致性GRPO策略，让理解分支自我批判生成输出，在无需外部标注的情况下弥合理解-生成差距。

Result: 在七个基准数据集上取得了最先进的结果，超越了任务特定专家模型和更大的通用模型。

Conclusion: ABACUS证明了统一模型可以在无需基准特定训练的情况下，同时胜任多种计数相关任务，并且通过循环一致性策略有效弥合了理解与生成之间的差距。

Original Abstract: ABACUS is a unified vision-language model that handles object counting, crowd counting, referring-expression counting, and count-faithful image generation without any benchmark-specific training required. Our model is built on existing 3B-parameter unified foundation model and is adapted for object localization tasks using three key innovations: density-aware adaptive zooming with objectness maps for spatial grounding; a boundary-aware count policy via GRPO to eliminate crop-boundary errors; and a cycle-consistent GRPO strategy where the understanding branch self-critiques generated outputs, closing the understanding-generation gap without any external annotations. ABACUS achieves state-of-the-art results across seven benchmarks, outperforming both task-specific specialists and larger generalist models.

中文摘要翻译: ABACUS是一个统一的视觉-语言模型，能够处理物体计数、人群计数、指代表达计数和计数忠实图像生成，无需任何基准特定的训练。我们的模型基于现有的30亿参数统一基础模型构建，并通过三项关键创新适配物体定位任务：利用物体性图进行密度感知自适应缩放以实现空间定位；通过GRPO实现边界感知计数策略以消除裁剪边界误差；以及一种循环一致性GRPO策略，其中理解分支对生成的输出进行自我批判，无需任何外部标注即可弥合理解与生成之间的差距。ABACUS在七个基准测试中取得了最先进的结果，同时超越了任务特定专家模型和更大的通用模型。

</details>


### [6] [Flood Mapping from RGB imagery using a Vision Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24120)
*Vladyslav Polushko,Tilman Bucher,Ronald Rösch,Thomas März,Markus Rauhut,Andreas Weinmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了如何将卫星预训练的Earth Observation foundation model（Prithvi-EO-2.0-600M Vision Transformer + UPerNet解码器）通过微调适应到厘米级RGB航空影像的洪水制图任务，实验表明该方法在域内任务上达到SOTA，在零样本跨域迁移上优于基线，且仅需少量目标域数据即可快速收敛到接近全量训练的性能水平。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 洪水应急响应需要高分辨率、时效性强的洪水范围地图。航空RGB影像成本低、可快速获取，但现有基于CNN和小型Vision Transformer的水体分割模型在新场景（新洪水事件）下需要大量标注数据进行适配，缺乏跨域泛化能力。而卫星预训练的Earth Observation foundation model虽然泛化能力强，但其训练数据（卫星影像）在空间分辨率、视角几何和辐射度量上与近距RGB航空影像存在显著差异，因此需要研究如何有效适配。

Method: 采用Prithvi-EO-2.0-600M Vision Transformer作为骨干网络，结合UPerNet解码器构建二值水体分割模型（Prithvi-2.0-UPN），在BlessemFlood21和NeuenahrFlood两个RGB洪水数据集上进行微调。设计了三个实验：1）域内训练测试，评估全监督性能；2）零样本跨域迁移（BlessemFlood21训练→NeuenahrFlood测试）；3）使用少量NeuenahrFlood数据进一步微调，评估迁移学习效率。

Result: 1）Prithvi-2.0-UPN在BlessemFlood21和NeuenahrFlood上达到SOTA；2）零样本跨域迁移中优于现有基线模型；3）仅使用少量NeuenahrFlood训练数据进一步微调时，Prithvi-2.0-UPN性能提升最快，且能接近全量训练的性能水平，展现出良好的迁移能力。

Conclusion: 卫星预训练的Earth Observation foundation model通过适当微调可以有效适应厘米级RGB航空影像的洪水分割任务，在域内达到最优性能，在跨域迁移中优于基线模型，且具备高效的小样本迁移能力。但零样本迁移性能仍有提升空间，值得进一步研究。

Original Abstract: Timely, high-resolution maps of flood extent around settlements are essential for emergency response and damage assessment. We consider airborne RGB imagery for flood mapping as it can be collected rapidly at low cost. To produce flood maps, deep learning models for water segmentation are often used. CNN based and small vision transformer models are used. However, they need much data for adaptation to a change of scenery, i.e., another flooding event. Vision foundation models or large vision transformers are known to generalize across domains. Recently, foundation models for Earth observation became available. They are pretrained on satellite data, whose spatial resolution, viewing geometry, and radiometry differ from nadir RGB imagery. Thus, adaptation is required. We investigate how a satellite-pretrained Earth observation foundation model can be adapted to centimeter-scale floodwater mapping from RGB imagery. Specifically, we fine-tune a model we call Prithvi-2.0-UPN consisting of the Prithvi-EO-2.0-600M Vision Transformer combined with a UPerNet decoder for binary water segmentation on two RGB datasets (BlessemFlood21, NeuenahrFlood). In a first experiment we observe that Prithvi-2.0-UPN reaches state-of-the-art results on BlessemFlood21 and NeuenahrFlood, when trained on their datasets. In a second experiment we show that Prithvi-2.0-UPN performs better than state-of-the-art baseline models for transfer to a new flood event (trained on BlessemFlood21, tested on NeuenahrFlood) in a zero-shot setting. However, the performance indicates room for improvement. In this respect, we investigate in a third experiment how performance improves when further fine-tuning the models with small shares of NeuenahrFlood training data: Prithvi-2.0-UPN improves the fastest and reaches almost the performance level when fully trained on NeuenahrFlood, indicating transfer capabilities.

中文摘要翻译: 及时、高分辨率的聚落周边洪水范围地图对于应急响应和灾害评估至关重要。我们考虑使用航空RGB影像进行洪水制图，因其可低成本快速获取。为生成洪水地图，常采用深度学习模型进行水体分割，包括基于CNN和小型Vision Transformer的模型。然而，这些模型在场景变化（即另一次洪水事件）时需要大量数据进行适配。Vision foundation model（即大型Vision Transformer）被认为具有跨域泛化能力。近期，面向地球观测的foundation model问世，它们基于卫星数据预训练，而卫星数据在空间分辨率、视角几何和辐射度量上与近距RGB影像存在差异，因此需要适配。本文研究如何将卫星预训练的Earth Observation foundation model适配到基于RGB影像的厘米级洪水制图任务。具体地，我们对一个名为Prithvi-2.0-UPN的模型进行微调，该模型由Prithvi-EO-2.0-600M Vision Transformer与UPerNet解码器组成，用于在两个RGB数据集（BlessemFlood21、NeuenahrFlood）上进行二值水体分割。在第一个实验中，我们观察到Prithvi-2.0-UPN在BlessemFlood21和NeuenahrFlood上达到最先进水平（在其各自数据集上训练后）。在第二个实验中，我们证明在零样本设置下，Prithvi-2.0-UPN在迁移到新洪水事件时（在BlessemFlood21上训练，在NeuenahrFlood上测试）优于最先进的基线模型。然而，性能表明仍有改进空间。为此，我们在第三个实验中探究了使用少量NeuenahrFlood训练数据进一步微调时性能的提升情况：Prithvi-2.0-UPN提升最快，且几乎达到在NeuenahrFlood上全量训练时的性能水平，这表明其具有良好的迁移能力。

</details>


### [7] [HANCLIP: A Family of Hyperbolic Angular Negation Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23843)
*Hoang-Bao Le,Aiden Durrant,Thai Son Mai,Binh T. Nguyen,Liting Zhou,Cathal Gurrin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HANCLIP通过双曲空间建模和角度三元组损失，仅用2万张图像-文本四元组数据微调，在不破坏预训练知识的前提下显著提升视觉语言模型对否定语义的理解能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 视觉语言模型（VLM）对否定语义（negation）非常脆弱，容易依赖浅层词汇共现而被误导；直接微调否定数据又会干扰已有知识，导致标准基准性能下降。

Method: 提出HANCLIP系列模型，结合双曲公式（建模层次语义关系与非对称性）和角度三元组目标（驱动否定描述与对应正向描述之间的系统性分离），在2万组图像-文本四元组上训练，对嵌入空间进行几何重构。

Result: 在NegBench基准上取得一致提升，同时在标准分类和图像-文本检索基准上保持竞争或更优性能；框架可即插即用于CLIP、LongCLIP、SmartCLIP、HiMo-CLIP等主流VLM，无需大规模重训练。

Conclusion: 精心设计的几何目标函数仅用少量额外数据就能显著扩展现有VLM的推理能力，且模型无关、训练高效。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are typically pre-trained on large-scale image-text datasets to capture semantic correspondences between visual content and natural language. However, they remain surprisingly brittle to negation: models often rely on shallow word co-occurrence and are easily distracted by misleading or irrelevant textual cues, even when their overall retrieval or classification performance is strong. Moreover, directly finetuning on negation data can interfere with previously acquired knowledge, causing noticeable degradation on standard vision-language benchmarks. To tackle these issues, this work introduces HANCLIP (Hyperbolic + Angular + Negation), a family of VLMs that explicitly restructures the embedding space to encode "what an image is not" alongside "what it is." HANCLIP is trained on a compact set of 20,000 image-text quadruplets and combines a hyperbolic formulation, which models hierarchical semantic relations and asymmetries, with an angular triplet objective that drives systematic separation between negated descriptions and their corresponding positives. This geometry-aware design strengthens negation sensitivity while preserving the global structure of pretrained representations, rather than overwriting them. Extensive experiments across multiple vision-language tasks show that HANCLIP delivers consistent gains on the negation-focused NegBench benchmark, while maintaining competitive or improved performance on standard classification and image-text retrieval benchmarks. The framework is model-agnostic and can be plugged into CLIP, LongCLIP, SmartCLIP, and HiMo-CLIP without large-scale retraining, demonstrating that a carefully designed geometric objective can substantially extend the reasoning capabilities of existing VLMs using only modest additional data.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉语言模型（VLM）通常在大规模图像-文本数据集上进行预训练，以捕获视觉内容与自然语言之间的语义对应关系。然而，它们对否定语义仍然出奇地脆弱：即使模型的整体检索或分类性能很强，模型也常常依赖浅层的词汇共现，容易被误导性或无关的文本线索干扰。此外，直接在否定数据上微调会干扰先前习得的知识，导致标准视觉语言基准性能明显下降。为了解决这些问题，本文引入了HANCLIP（双曲+角度+否定），这是一个VLM系列，它明确重构了嵌入空间，以编码"图像不是什么"以及"图像是什么"。HANCLIP在紧凑的2万组图像-文本四元组数据集上训练，结合了双曲公式（用于建模层次语义关系和非对称性）和角度三元组目标（用于驱动否定描述与其对应正向描述之间的系统性分离）。这种几何感知设计在增强否定敏感性的同时，保留了预训练表示的整体结构，而非覆盖它们。跨多个视觉语言任务的大量实验表明，HANCLIP在专注于否定的NegBench基准上取得了一致提升，同时在标准分类和图像-文本检索基准上保持了竞争性或更优性能。该框架与模型无关，可无缝接入CLIP、LongCLIP、SmartCLIP和HiMo-CLIP而无需大规模重训练，证明了精心设计的几何目标函数仅使用少量额外数据就能显著扩展现有VLM的推理能力。

</details>


### [8] [High-Fidelity Synthetic Transmission Electron Microscopy Image Generation Using Diffusion Probabilistic Models for Data-Limited Semiconductor Metrology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24817)
*Johannes Boehm,Bappaditya Dey*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）的框架，在极端数据稀缺条件下（仅15张样本）合成透射电子显微镜（TEM）图像，并通过渐进式块训练、数据增强、域迁移、分类器引导和修复技术，生成符合FAB计量要求的高保真全尺寸图像，同时利用DDPM特征进行分割，在缺陷检测、分割和计量等下游ML任务中表现出色。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 先进半导体节点的需求急剧增加了透射电子显微镜（TEM）的使用，但破坏性样品制备、缓慢成像和高昂成本严重限制了用于机器学习（ML）的大规模多样化数据集的获取，亟需生成高质量的合成TEM图像以支持下游任务。

Method: 提出基于DDPM的合成TEM图像生成框架：采用渐进式块训练策略从低分辨率块扩展到全尺寸图像；集成自定义TrivialAugment自适应、跨进程域迁移、分类器引导和RePaint风格修复；并重新利用DDPM编码器特征图进行分割以获取连贯区域掩码。

Result: 合成图像MS-SSIM > 0.98，专家定性评估与结构相似性结果一致，在缺陷检测、分割和计量等下游ML任务中有效保持统计与物理真实性。

Conclusion: 该DDPM框架能够在极端数据稀缺条件下生成高质量、物理真实的合成TEM图像，有效支持下游机器学习任务，为半导体领域的合成数据生成提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Advanced semiconductor nodes drastically increased demand for Transmission Electron Microscopy (TEM), yet destructive sample preparation, slow imaging and high costs severely limit the availability of diverse datasets needed for downstream machine learning (ML). Synthetic data generation is becoming essential, but current generative models often miss TEM-specific noise, structural detail, and stochastic variability crucial for evaluation. We present a Denoising Diffusion Probabilistic Model (DDPM) framework for synthetic TEM image generation under extreme data scarcity. A progressive patch-based training strategy scales from low-resolution patches to full images, enabling from-scratch training with only 15 samples. We integrate a custom TrivialAugment adaptation, cross-process domain transfer, classifier guidance, and RePaint-style inpainting, culminating in full-image generation that preserves global structural and spatial relationships in compliance with FAB metrology requirements. Beyond synthesis, we repurpose DDPM feature representations for segmentation, partitioning encoder feature maps to obtain coherent region masks. Our synthetic images achieve up to MS-SSIM > 0.98 and qualitative expert assessment consistent with structural similarity results, facilitating downstream ML training for defect detection, segmentation, and metrology while preserving statistical and physical realism.

中文摘要翻译: 先进半导体节点的需求急剧增加了透射电子显微镜（TEM）的使用，然而破坏性样品制备、缓慢成像和高昂成本严重限制了用于下游机器学习（ML）的多样化数据集的可用性。合成数据生成正变得至关重要，但当前的生成模型往往缺少TEM特有的噪声、结构细节以及对评估至关重要的随机变异性。我们提出了一种去噪扩散概率模型（DDPM）框架，用于在极端数据稀缺条件下合成TEM图像生成。渐进式块训练策略从低分辨率块扩展到全尺寸图像，使得仅用15个样本即可从头训练。我们集成了自定义的TrivialAugment自适应、跨进程域迁移、分类器引导以及RePaint风格的图像修复，最终实现全尺寸图像生成，该生成保留了全局结构和空间关系，符合FAB计量要求。在合成之外，我们还重新利用DDPM特征表示进行分割，对编码器特征图进行分区以获得连贯的区域掩码。我们的合成图像达到了MS-SSIM > 0.98，专家定性评估与结构相似性结果一致，促进了缺陷检测、分割和计量等下游ML训练，同时保持了统计与物理真实性。

</details>


### [9] [Mind the Heads: Topological Representation Alignment for Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23885)
*Davide Caffagni,Alberto Compagnoni,Federico Melis,Sara Sarto,Pier Luigi Dovesi,Mark Granroth-Wilding,Marcella Cornia,Lorenzo Baraldi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为HeRA（Head-Wise Representation Alignment）的方法，在多头注意力层面对齐多模态大语言模型（MLLM）的内部表示与外部视觉编码器的表示，通过对比学习目标保持局部邻域拓扑结构，并发现对齐最不对齐的头能带来最大收益，有效缓解视觉幻觉并提升视觉任务性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有表示对齐方法通常对齐语言骨干网络的固定层，忽略了Transformer模型中细粒度的注意力头结构，未能充分利用多头注意力机制中的潜在对齐空间。

Method: 基于Platonic表示假设，利用互K近邻（MKNN）对齐度量，设计了一个可微分的对比学习目标来匹配跨模态的局部拓扑结构；在多模态训练过程中，选择MKNN得分最低（即最不对齐）的注意力头进行对齐，从而最大化对齐效益。

Result: 在多个MLLM和18个基准测试上的实验表明，HeRA在视觉中心任务上持续提升性能；同时，通过自然抑制对语言先验的过度依赖，有效充当了针对视觉幻觉的正则化器。

Conclusion: 细粒度地在注意力头级别进行表示对齐是提升多模态大语言模型性能的有效策略，特别是对齐最不对齐的头能够带来最大的改进，并为视觉幻觉问题提供了一种有效的正则化手段。

Original Abstract: Representation alignment has emerged as an effective approach to improve Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) by regularizing their internal representations toward those of an external vision encoder. However, existing methods typically align a fixed layer of the language backbone, overlooking the fine-grained structure of Transformer models. In this work, we propose Head-Wise Representation Alignment (HeRA), a method that enforces cross-modal alignment at the level of individual attention heads. Our approach is grounded in the Platonic Representation Hypothesis, focusing on preserving the topological structure of representations (i.e., their local neighborhood relationships) across modalities. Following the Mutual K-Nearest Neighbor (MKNN) alignment metric, we introduce a contrastive objective that acts as a differentiable proxy for matching local structures. HeRA applies this objective during multimodal training to specific attention heads in the LLM, selected by their alignment score according to the MKNN metric. Counterintuitively, we find that aligning the least aligned heads yields the largest gains. Extensive evaluations across multiple MLLMs and 18 benchmarks demonstrate that HeRA consistently improves performance on challenging vision-centric tasks and serves as an effective regularizer against visual hallucinations by naturally curbing the over-reliance on linguistic priors. Our code is publicly released.

中文摘要翻译: 表示对齐已成为一种有效的多模态大语言模型（MLLM）改进方法，它通过将模型内部表示正则化到外部视觉编码器的表示空间来实现。然而，现有方法通常对齐语言骨干网络的固定层，忽略了Transformer模型的细粒度结构。本文提出了一种名为逐头表示对齐（HeRA）的方法，该方法在单个注意力头级别强制进行跨模态对齐。我们的方法基于Platonic表示假设，专注于保留跨模态表示的拓扑结构（即局部邻域关系）。遵循互K近邻（MKNN）对齐度量，我们引入了一个对比学习目标，作为匹配局部结构的可微分代理。HeRA在多模态训练期间将此目标应用于LLM中特定的注意力头，这些注意力头根据MKNN度量的对齐得分进行选择。与直觉相反，我们发现对齐最不对齐的头能带来最大的收益。在多个MLLM和18个基准测试上的广泛评估表明，HeRA在具有挑战性的视觉中心任务上持续提升性能，并通过自然抑制对语言先验的过度依赖，有效地作为对抗视觉幻觉的正则化器。我们的代码已公开发布。

</details>


### [10] [REALM: A Unified Red-Teaming Benchmark for Physical-World VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23892)
*Yifei Zhao,Qian Lou,Mengxin Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了REALM，首个面向物理世界视觉-语言模型的统一红队评测基准，整合12种攻击方法、3种防御和13个模型，在统一协议下公平比较各类红队攻击方法，发现文本与排版注入攻击效果最强，多模态联合优化生成最具迁移性的视觉扰动。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有红队评测方法分散在不同数据集、指标和威胁模型下，难以直接比较，且缺乏针对物理世界VLM功能故障的系统性评测标准。

Method: 构建统一红队基准REALM，集成12种攻击方法、3种模型无关防御和13个VLM；提出智能体目标生成流水线，为每个场景构造共享、场景特定且物理可实现的攻击目标，实现对不同攻击家族在统一对抗目标下的公平比较。

Result: 文本和排版注入攻击导致最多故障；多模态联合优化产生最强视觉扰动迁移性；单次攻击以更低成本接近迭代方法效果；模型规模本身并不赋予对抗鲁棒性。

Conclusion: REALM提供了首个统一的物理世界VLM红队评测框架，能够公平比较不同攻击方法，揭示了现有VLM对抗鲁棒性的关键洞见。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used as perception-reasoning backbones for embodied intelligence in safety-critical physical systems, where perception or reasoning errors can lead to unsafe decisions or actions. Although many red-teaming methods have been developed to probe VLM vulnerabilities, their evaluation remains fragmented across datasets, metrics, and threat models, making direct comparison difficult and obscuring whether observed differences arise from stronger attacks, more vulnerable models, or incompatible evaluation settings. Existing chatbot-centric red-teaming benchmarks mainly standardize jailbreak and content-safety evaluation, but they do not systematically capture physically grounded functional failures or cover red-teaming methods that target physical-world VLMs. This raises the key challenge of comparing diverse attack methods under a unified protocol while targeting the same scenario-specific failures. We introduce REALM, to our knowledge the first unified red-teaming benchmark for physical-world VLMs. REALM integrates 12 red-teaming methods, 3 model-agnostic defenses, and 13 VLMs under a practical black-box threat model with shared datasets and metrics. To align adversarial objectives across attack families, REALM introduces an agentic target-generation pipeline that constructs shared, scenario-specific, and physically grounded attack objectives for each scene, enabling fair comparison of diverse red-teaming methods under aligned adversarial goals. Our evaluation shows that text and typographic injection attacks induce the most failures, multimodal co-optimization yields the strongest visual-perturbation transfer, single-pass attacks approach iterative methods at much lower cost, and model scale alone does not confer adversarial robustness. Code is available at https://github.com/UCF-ML-Research/REALM.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）越来越多地被用作安全关键物理系统中具身智能的感知-推理骨干，而感知或推理错误可能导致不安全的决策或行动。尽管已有许多红队评测方法用于探查VLM的脆弱性，但这些方法在数据集、指标和威胁模型上的评测是分散的，导致直接比较困难，且难以区分观察到的差异是源于更强的攻击、更脆弱的模型，还是不相容的评测设置。现有以聊天机器人为中心的红队基准主要标准化了越狱和内容安全评测，但未能系统地捕获物理世界中的功能性故障，也未覆盖针对物理世界VLM的红队评测方法。这提出了一个关键挑战：如何在统一协议下比较不同的攻击方法，同时针对相同的场景特定故障。我们提出REALM，据我们所知这是首个面向物理世界VLM的统一红队评测基准。REALM在共享数据集和指标的实际黑盒威胁模型下，整合了12种红队攻击方法、3种模型无关防御和13个VLM。为了在不同攻击家族间对齐对抗目标，REALM引入了一种智能体目标生成流水线，为每个场景构造共享的、场景特定的且物理可实现的攻击目标，从而在统一对抗目标下实现对不同红队攻击方法的公平比较。我们的评测表明，文本和排版注入攻击诱发最多故障，多模态联合优化产生最强的视觉扰动迁移性，单次攻击以更低成本接近迭代方法的效果，而模型规模本身并不能赋予对抗鲁棒性。代码已开源：https://github.com/UCF-ML-Research/REALM。

</details>


### [11] [The Professor: Multi-Teacher Unsupervised Prompt Distillation for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23897)
*Ahmad Algadhi,Ahmed Alzuhair,Omar Alkhulaif,Muzammil Behzad*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TheProfessor，一种多教师提示蒸馏方法，通过集成一个领域微调的PromptSRC ViT-L/14教师和一个零样本EVA-CLIP-L/14教师，在四个数据集上相比单教师PromptKD平均HM提升1.77点，在域偏移场景下提升最为显著（EuroSAT +5.78）。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的提示蒸馏方法（如PromptKD）仅使用单一教师模型，未能充分利用不同教师模型在域偏移场景下提供的互补监督信号。

Method: 使用固定双教师集成：一个领域微调的PromptSRC ViT-L/14教师和一个零样本EVA-CLIP-L/14教师，后者的logit可针对每个数据集预计算；采用置信度加权集成策略进行知识蒸馏到学生模型。

Result: 在Caltech-101、DTD、UCF101和EuroSAT四个数据集上，置信度加权集成平均HM从87.52提升至89.28（+1.77），等概率集成提升至88.88（+1.37）；增益与数据集相关，在Caltech-101上仅+0.16，在EuroSAT上高达+5.78。

Conclusion: 多教师提示蒸馏在域偏移场景下最为有效，此时第二个教师能够提供互补的监督信号，显著提升学生模型的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Prompt distillation compresses large vision-language models (VLMs) such as CLIP into lightweight student models by matching teacher predictions on unlabeled domain images. PromptKD (CVPR 2024) established this paradigm with a single PromptSRC-finetuned ViT-L/14 teacher and a ViT-B/16 student. We propose TheProfessor, a multi-teacher extension that distills from a fixed two-teacher ensemble: a domain-finetuned PromptSRC ViT-L/14 teacher and a zero-shot EVA-CLIP-L/14 teacher whose logits are pre-computed per dataset. We evaluate single-teacher PromptKD, equal-probability ensembling, and confidence-weighted ensembling on four base-to-novel datasets: Caltech-101, DTD, UCF101, and EuroSAT. In a 12-run single-seed sweep, confidence-weighted ensembling improves average HM from 87.52 to 89.28 (+1.77 points), while equal averaging improves average HM to 88.88 (+1.37 points). Gains are dataset dependent: they are negligible on Caltech-101 (+0.16 HM for confidence weighting), modest on UCF101 (+0.62), and largest on domain-shifted EuroSAT (+5.78). These results update our earlier Caltech-only analysis and show that multi-teacher prompt distillation is most useful when the second teacher contributes complementary supervision under domain shift.

中文摘要翻译: 提示蒸馏通过匹配教师在无标签领域图像上的预测，将CLIP等大型视觉语言模型（VLM）压缩为轻量级学生模型。PromptKD（CVPR 2024）使用单个PromptSRC微调后的ViT-L/14教师和ViT-B/16学生建立了这一范式。我们提出TheProfessor，一种多教师扩展方法，从固定的双教师集成中进行蒸馏：一个领域微调的PromptSRC ViT-L/14教师和一个零样本EVA-CLIP-L/14教师，后者的logit可针对每个数据集预计算。我们在四个基础到新类（base-to-novel）数据集——Caltech-101、DTD、UCF101和EuroSAT上评估了单教师PromptKD、等概率集成和置信度加权集成。在12次运行的单种子扫描中，置信度加权集成将平均调和均值（HM）从87.52提升至89.28（+1.77个点），而等平均集成将平均HM提升至88.88（+1.37个点）。增益依赖于数据集：在Caltech-101上可忽略不计（置信度加权+0.16 HM），在UCF101上适中（+0.62），在域偏移的EuroSAT上最大（+5.78）。这些结果更新了我们先前仅基于Caltech-101的分析，并表明当第二个教师在域偏移下提供互补监督时，多教师提示蒸馏最为有效。

</details>


### [12] [Trustworthy Image Authentication using Forensic Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23917)
*Tai D. Nguyen,Matthew C. Stamm*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出法医知识图谱（FKG）统一框架，融合证据提取、结构化推理与可解释性，在检测、伪造识别定位及法医解释上均优于现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有伪造检测器可针对特定伪造类型但缺乏可解释性，而视觉-语言模型（VLM）虽能提供解释但无法利用法医痕迹进行可靠检测，因此需要一种既能检测伪造又能提供可解释性证据的统一框架。

Method: 提出法医知识图谱（FKG）框架，该结构编码法医痕迹及其因果依赖关系并与场景内容关联；引入新型法医认证网络和迭代上下文精炼策略，引导VLM生成忠实且基于证据的解释；同时构建了包含5万张真实伪造图像及对应FKG标注的数据集FKG-50K。

Result: 实验表明，FKG在检测、伪造识别与定位、以及法医解释方面均优于现有的伪造检测器和视觉-语言模型。

Conclusion: 法医知识图谱通过结构化推理和可解释性统一了伪造检测任务，为高真实感图像伪造提供了可信赖的认证方案。

Original Abstract: Advances in generative AI have made image falsification highly realistic, demanding trustworthy authentication systems. Existing forensic detectors can target certain forgery types but lack interpretability, while vision-language models (VLMs) provide explanations but cannot exploit forensic traces for reliable detection. We propose Forensic Knowledge Graphs (FKGs), a unified framework that integrates forensic evidence extraction, structured reasoning, and human-interpretable explanation. Our FKG structure encodes forensic traces along with their causal dependencies and links to scene content. To generate accurate FKGs, we introduce a novel forensic authentication network and an Iterative Context Refinement strategy that guides VLMs to produce faithful, grounded explanations. We also present FKG-50K, a dataset of 50,000 realistic forgeries with ground-truth FKGs. Experiments demonstrate that FKG outperforms both forensic detectors and VLMs in detection, forgery identification and localization, and forensic justification.

中文摘要翻译: 生成式AI的进步使得图像伪造变得极为逼真，从而迫切需要可信赖的认证系统。现有的伪造检测器可针对特定伪造类型，但缺乏可解释性；而视觉-语言模型（VLM）虽能提供解释，却无法利用法医痕迹进行可靠检测。本文提出法医知识图谱（FKG），一个统一框架，集成了法医证据提取、结构化推理和人类可理解的解释。我们的FKG结构编码了法医痕迹及其因果依赖关系，并将其与场景内容关联。为生成准确的FKG，我们引入了一种新型的法医认证网络和迭代上下文精炼策略，引导VLM产生忠实且基于证据的解释。我们还提出了FKG-50K数据集，包含5万张带有真实FKG标注的真实感伪造图像。实验表明，FKG在检测、伪造识别与定位以及法医解释方面均优于现有的伪造检测器和视觉-语言模型。

</details>


### [13] [DivRL: Disentangled Self-Similarity Rewards for Diverse Subject-Driven Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23950)
*Qian Wang,Zhenyu Li,Abdelrahman Eldesokey,Peter Wonka*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出DivRL框架，通过解耦视觉特征和门控优化策略，解决主体驱动图像生成中身份保持与结构多样性之间的矛盾。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有主体驱动图像生成面临"身份-多样性悖论"：强身份保持往往导致输出僵化、多样性低，亟需一种能同时优化身份一致性和结构多样性的方法。

Method: 提出DivRL后训练框架，包含：1) 负自相似性度量(nSSM)量化结构多样性；2) 视觉语义匹配(VSM)评估身份一致性；3) "探索-抑制"策略：VSM作为门控约束，模型自由探索多样结构，仅对违反身份阈值的样本通过二次铰链损失进行惩罚，将身份保持从竞争目标转为可行性约束。

Result: 实验表明，DivRL能有效推动模型生成既一致又多样的图像，在保持相当身份一致性的前提下，通过门控优化公式显著提升结构多样性。

Conclusion: 通过门控优化将身份保持转化为可行性约束，使得负自相似性度量和视觉语义匹配能够 jointly improve，是解决身份-多样性悖论的有效框架。

Original Abstract: Subject-driven image generation faces an "Identity-Diversity Paradox", where strong identity preservation often leads to rigid and low-diversity outputs. We propose a post-training framework called DivRL that jointly optimizes identity consistency and structural diversity simultaneously by leveraging disentangled visual features from a robust similarity model. Specifically, we introduce a Negative Self-Similarity Measure (nSSM) to quantify structural diversity, and Visual Semantic Matching (VSM) to evaluate identity consistency. We propose an "Explore-and-Suppress" strategy that treats VSM as a gated constraint: the model freely explores structurally diverse configurations, and only samples that violate the identity threshold are penalized via a quadratic hinge loss. This converts identity preservation from a competing objective into a feasibility constraint, allowing nSSM and VSM to improve jointly. Experiments demonstrate that our method effectively pushes the model to generate both consistent and diverse images and improves structural diversity while maintaining comparable identity consistency through a gated optimization formulation.

中文摘要翻译: 主体驱动的图像生成面临"身份-多样性悖论"：强身份保持往往导致输出僵化且多样性低。我们提出一种名为DivRL的后训练框架，通过利用来自鲁棒相似性模型的解耦视觉特征，同时联合优化身份一致性和结构多样性。具体而言，我们引入负自相似性度量(nSSM)来量化结构多样性，以及视觉语义匹配(VSM)来评估身份一致性。我们提出一种"探索-抑制"策略，将VSM作为门控约束：模型自由探索结构多样性的配置，仅对违反身份阈值的样本通过二次铰链损失进行惩罚。这将身份保持从竞争目标转化为可行性约束，使得nSSM和VSM能够共同改进。实验表明，我们的方法有效推动模型生成既一致又多样的图像，并通过门控优化公式，在保持相当身份一致性的同时提升结构多样性。

</details>


### [14] [Token-to-Token Alignment of Text Embeddings for Semantic Blending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24021)
*Saar Huberman,Ron Mokady,Or Patashnik,Daniel Cohen-Or*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Token-to-Token对齐框架，通过结构对齐和嵌入对齐两个阶段，将文本提示中的token映射到一致的语义位置，使线性插值能够产生平滑的语义过渡，从而支持图像融合和连续编辑等应用。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前文本到图像生成模型中，token序列空间缺乏一致的可访问结构：语义相似的图像可能对应措辞、顺序和概念位置不同的token序列，而相似的token序列可能编码截然不同的语义。这种结构缺失使得在该空间中进行平滑过渡变得困难，阻碍了图像融合和连续编辑控制等应用。

Method: 提出Token-to-Token对齐框架，包含两个阶段：1) 结构对齐：将不同提示重写为共享的结构化形式，使语义对应的概念映射到一致的位置；2) 嵌入级对齐：基于语义相似性匹配不同提示间的token嵌入表示。

Result: 经过对齐后，简单的线性插值成为有意义的操作，能够产生平滑且连贯的语义过渡，支持图像融合和连续编辑等应用。实验表明文本到图像模型中的文本嵌入空间隐式编码了连续的语义结构，只需通过适当的对齐即可访问。

Conclusion: 语义控制可以通过组织现有表示来实现，而无需修改生成模型本身。文本嵌入空间本身具有连续语义结构，只是需要对齐才能显现。

Original Abstract: In modern generative models, images are specified and controlled through text prompts. In practice, images are generated from sequences of tokens derived from these prompts. However, the space of token sequences lacks a consistent accessible structure: semantically similar images may correspond to sequences that differ in wording, ordering, and placement of concepts, while similar token sequences may encode very different semantics. This apparent lack of structure makes it difficult to perform smooth transitions in this space, hindering applications such as image blending and continuous control of edits. We argue that this limitation stems not from the absence of semantic structure, but from misalignment between representations. To address this misalignment, we introduce Token-to-Token alignment, a framework that establishes explicit semantic correspondence between tokens across prompts. Our approach transforms prompts into a structured representation in which semantically corresponding concepts are mapped to consistent positions across prompts, and then aligns their token embeddings based on semantic similarity. Concretely, the method consists of two stages: a structural alignment that rephrases prompts into a shared structured form, followed by an embedding-level alignment that matches token representations across prompts. With this alignment in place, simple linear interpolation becomes a meaningful operation, producing smooth and coherent semantic transitions and enabling applications such as blending and continuous editing. Our results show that text embedding spaces in text-to-image models implicitly encode a continuous semantic structure that becomes accessible once representations are properly aligned, suggesting that semantic control can be achieved by organizing existing representations rather than modifying the generative model.

中文摘要翻译: 在现代生成模型中，图像通过文本提示进行指定和控制。在实践中，图像是从这些提示导出的token序列生成的。然而，token序列空间缺乏一致的可访问结构：语义相似的图像可能对应措辞、顺序和概念位置不同的序列，而相似的token序列可能编码截然不同的语义。这种结构缺失使得在该空间中进行平滑过渡变得困难，阻碍了图像融合和编辑的连续控制等应用。我们认为，这一限制并非源于语义结构的缺失，而是表示之间的未对齐。为了解决这一未对齐问题，我们引入了Token-to-Token对齐框架，该框架在不同提示的token之间建立显式的语义对应关系。我们的方法将提示转换为一种结构化表示，其中语义对应的概念被映射到不同提示中的一致位置，然后基于语义相似性对齐它们的token嵌入。具体而言，该方法包含两个阶段：结构对齐将提示重写为共享的结构化形式，随后是嵌入级对齐，匹配不同提示间的token表示。经过这种对齐后，简单的线性插值成为有意义的操作，能够产生平滑且连贯的语义过渡，从而实现融合和连续编辑等应用。我们的结果表明，文本到图像模型中的文本嵌入空间隐式编码了连续的语义结构，一旦表示被适当对齐即可访问，这表明语义控制可以通过组织现有表示来实现，而无需修改生成模型。

</details>


### [15] [DriveStack-VLA: Render-Teacher Alignment for BEV-Based DeepStack Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24051)
*Jingke Wang,Zhenru Zhao,Shuangming Lei,Hao Su,Yuehao Huang,Yijia Xie,Kai Tang,Guanglin Xu,AiXue Ye,Yukai Ma,Yong Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriveStack-VLA 通过双视觉建模模块（鸟瞰图注入和渲染-教师对齐）以及自批判模块，增强了视觉-语言-动作驾驶模型的空间感知能力，在多个自动驾驶基准上取得了领先性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有VLA驾驶模型缺乏面向驾驶的空间智能，其策略主要基于透视图像令牌和语言先验，缺乏度量几何、自上而下的场景结构以及对安全关键感知线索的关注，导致在弱视觉几何建模和专家演示的感知覆盖方面表现脆弱。

Method: 1) 双视觉建模组件：通过DeepStack式连接将鸟瞰图表示注入大语言模型解码器，并提出Render-Teacher Alignment对齐真实图像与栅格化图像的感知焦点；2) 基于head的自批判模块，用于对采样轨迹进行排序并条件性地优化最佳轨迹。

Result: NAVSIMv1上达91.6 PDMS，NAVSIMv2上达91.0 EPDMS（启用人类惩罚过滤器），闭环Bench2Drive上驾驶分数79.49、成功率56.36%。

Conclusion: DriveStack-VLA通过增强空间接地和多模态轨迹选择，有效提升了VLA模型的驾驶性能，在多项基准上达到先进水平。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language-Action driving models convert a pretrained Vision-Language Model into a driving policy, allowing them to use world knowledge and follow language guidances. However, existing VLA driving models still lack driving-oriented spatial intelligence: their policies are mainly grounded on perspective image tokens and language priors, while precise motion planning requires metric geometry, top-down scene structure, and attention to safety-critical perceptual cues. This limitation makes current models vulnerable to weak visual geometry modeling and perceptual coverage in expert demonstrations. In this paper, we present DriveStack-VLA, a framework built upon a large VLM backbone. To strengthen the spatial grounding of VLA driving, we develop dual visual modeling components. We inject a Bird-Eye-View representation into the Large Language Model decoder through a DeepStack-style connection, and propose Render-Teacher Alignment to align the perceptual focus of real images with that of rasterized images. Furthermore, to bridge the gap in multimodal trajectory selection, we introduce a head-based self-critique module that ranks sampled trajectories and conditionally refines the best one. DriveStack-VLA achieves 91.6 PDMS on NAVSIMv1, 91.0 EPDMS on NAVSIMv2 (with the human penalty filter enabled), and a driving score of 79.49 with a success rate of 56.36\% on the closed-loop Bench2Drive. More visualizations are available on our project page: https://anonymous.4open.science/w/drivestack-vla/.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言-动作驾驶模型将预训练的视觉-语言模型转换为驾驶策略，使其能够利用世界知识并遵循语言指导。然而，现有VLA驾驶模型仍然缺乏面向驾驶的空间智能：它们的策略主要基于透视图像令牌和语言先验，而精确的运动规划需要度量几何、自上而下的场景结构以及对安全关键感知线索的关注。这一限制使得当前模型在专家演示中容易受到弱视觉几何建模和感知覆盖不足的影响。在本文中，我们提出了DriveStack-VLA，一个基于大型VLM骨干网络构建的框架。为了增强VLA驾驶的空间接地能力，我们开发了双视觉建模组件。我们通过DeepStack式连接将鸟瞰图表示注入大语言模型解码器，并提出渲染-教师对齐（Render-Teacher Alignment）来对齐真实图像与栅格化图像的感知焦点。此外，为了弥合多模态轨迹选择中的差距，我们引入了一个基于head的自批判模块，该模块对采样轨迹进行排序并有条件地优化最佳轨迹。DriveStack-VLA在NAVSIMv1上达到91.6 PDMS，在NAVSIMv2上达到91.0 EPDMS（启用人类惩罚过滤器），在闭环Bench2Drive上获得79.49的驾驶分数和56.36%的成功率。更多可视化内容请访问我们的项目页面：https://anonymous.4open.science/w/drivestack-vla/。

</details>


### [16] [EPEdit: Redefining Image Editing with Generative AI and User-Centric Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24057)
*Hoang-Phuc Nguyen,Dinh-Khoi Vo,Trong-Le Do,Hai-Dang Nguyen,Tan-Cong Nguyen,Vinh-Tiep Nguyen,Tam V. Nguyen,Khanh-Duy Le,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EPEdit是一个基于Stable Diffusion零样本图像编辑算法的应用，通过集成后端框架和前端界面，支持多种图像编辑任务，无需微调即可实现高效、低成本的图像处理。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统图像编辑工具（如Photoshop）需要专业经验，而现有生成式AI方案（如Stable Diffusion）需要大量重新训练和微调，导致用户成本高昂。为降低门槛和成本，作者提出了EPEdit。

Method: EPEdit集成了基于Stable Diffusion的零样本图像编辑算法，无需额外微调，用户通过文本命令或标记区域即可驱动图像生成、替换、移除、背景修改、姿态变化、区域编辑等任务。

Result: 用户在图像编辑、主题设计和系统整体性能评估中表明，EPEdit优于现有解决方案，提供了用户友好且成本效益高的综合图像编辑体验。

Conclusion: EPEdit通过零样本方法消除了对模型重新训练和微调的需求，降低了使用成本，同时保持了强大的编辑能力，是一种高效、易用的图像编辑解决方案。

Original Abstract: The demand for image manipulation has seen a significant increase recently. Traditional tools like Photoshop and Capture One, while powerful, require considerable expertise to use effectively. Generative AI has introduced alternative platforms, such as Luminar Neo, Pixlr X, and Canva. However, many of these solutions, including resource-heavy models like Stable Diffusion, often require substantial retraining and fine-tuning, leading to high costs for users. To address these challenges, we introduce Efficient Photo Editor (EPEdit), an application that integrates a robust backend framework with a user-friendly front-end interface. EPEdit supports a wide range of creative image editing tasks, including image generation, object replacement, object removal, background modification, changes in object pose or perspective, region-specific editing, and thematic collection design, all guided by masks and prompts. Users can interact with the system through simple text commands or by marking areas for precise adjustments, making it accessible even to those without technical expertise. At its core, EPEdit leverages zero-shot image editing algorithms based on Stable Diffusion model, removing the need for additional fine-tuning. This approach enables efficient image manipulation and thematic collection creation. User evaluations for tasks of image editing, thematic design, and overall system performance demonstrate that EPEdit outperforms existing solutions, offering a user-friendly, cost-effective solution for comprehensive image editing.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，图像处理的需求显著增长。Photoshop和Capture One等传统工具虽然功能强大，但需要相当的专业知识才能有效使用。生成式AI引入了替代平台，如Luminar Neo、Pixlr X和Canva。然而，许多这类解决方案，包括像Stable Diffusion这样资源密集型的模型，通常需要大量的重新训练和微调，导致用户成本高昂。为了解决这些挑战，我们提出了高效图像编辑器（EPEdit），一个集成了强大后端框架与用户友好前端界面的应用。EPEdit支持广泛的创意图像编辑任务，包括图像生成、对象替换、对象移除、背景修改、对象姿态或视角变化、区域特定编辑以及主题化集合设计，所有操作均通过掩码和提示引导。用户可以通过简单的文本命令或标记区域进行精确调整，即使没有技术背景也能轻松使用。EPEdit的核心利用基于Stable Diffusion模型（SD）的零样本图像编辑算法，无需额外微调。这种方法能够高效地实现图像处理和主题化集合的创建。用户在图像编辑、主题设计及系统整体性能评估中的结果表明，EPEdit优于现有解决方案，提供了一种用户友好且成本效益高的全面图像编辑方案。

</details>


### [17] [VisChronos: Revolutionizing Image Captioning Through Real-Life Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24058)
*Phuc-Tan Nguyen,Hieu Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VisChronos框架，利用大语言模型和密集字幕模型从单张图像中识别和描述真实事件，并构建了EventCap数据集，以弥合视觉内容与自然语言理解之间的语义鸿沟，生成上下文感知的事件描述。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统图像描述方法在捕捉上下文叙事方面存在局限，难以生成能够反映真实世界事件背景的详细描述。本文旨在弥合视觉内容与自然语言理解之间的语义鸿沟，通过利用真实世界的历史事件作为知识源来增强字幕生成。

Method: 提出VisChronos框架，结合大语言模型和密集字幕模型，从单张输入图像中自动识别并描述真实事件，生成详细且上下文感知的事件描述。

Result: 用户研究证明了该方法在生成准确、连贯且以事件为核心的描述方面的有效性。

Conclusion: VisChronos框架能够生成准确、连贯且以事件为中心的描述，为未来以事件为中心的图像理解研究奠定了基础。

Original Abstract: This paper aims to bridge the semantic gap between visual content and natural language understanding by leveraging historical events in the real world as a source of knowledge for caption generation. We propose VisChronos, a novel framework that utilizes large language models and dense captioning models to identify and describe real-life events from a single input image. Our framework can automatically generate detailed and context-aware event descriptions, enhancing the descriptive quality and contextual relevance of generated captions to address the limitations of traditional methods in capturing contextual narratives. Furthermore, we introduce a new dataset, EventCap (https://zenodo.org/records/14004909), specifically constructed using the proposed framework, designed to enhance the model's ability to identify and understand complex events. The user study demonstrates the efficacy of our solution in generating accurate, coherent, and event-focused descriptions, paving the way for future research in event-centric image understanding.

中文摘要翻译: 本文旨在通过利用真实世界中的历史事件作为字幕生成的知识源，弥合视觉内容与自然语言理解之间的语义鸿沟。我们提出了VisChronos，一个新颖的框架，利用大语言模型和密集字幕模型从单张输入图像中识别和描述真实事件。我们的框架能够自动生成详细且上下文感知的事件描述，增强生成字幕的描述质量和上下文相关性，从而解决传统方法在捕捉上下文叙事方面的局限性。此外，我们引入了一个新的数据集EventCap（https://zenodo.org/records/14004909），该数据集使用所提出的框架专门构建，旨在增强模型识别和理解复杂事件的能力。用户研究表明，我们的解决方案在生成准确、连贯且以事件为核心的描述方面具有有效性，为未来以事件为中心的图像理解研究铺平了道路。

</details>


### [18] [Ingredient-Level Food Image Segmentation for Nutrition Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24059)
*Jonesh Shrestha*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了食品图像中食材级别的语义分割任务，在FoodSeg103数据集上微调了两个SegFormer变体（B0和B1），B1在像素准确率（0.7929）和平均IoU（0.3204）上均取得更优结果，并将预测掩码转换为食材面积百分比供营养感知参考。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 食品图像常包含多种可见食材，仅分配一个菜品标签会丢失重要的视觉结构信息，因此需要研究像素级别的食材语义分割，以捕捉更精细的视觉信息。

Method: 在FoodSeg103数据集上微调两个SegFormer变体：SegFormer-B0（较小基线模型）和SegFormer-B1（较大最终模型），两者均使用ImageNet预训练的MiT主干网络，并新初始化了104类输出层。

Result: 在2135张测试图像上，B0获得0.7709像素准确率和0.2521平均IoU；B1获得0.7929像素准确率和0.3204平均IoU，B1在所有测试指标上均有提升，平均IoU绝对增益为+0.0683。

Conclusion: 该系统可将预测掩码转换为可见食材面积百分比，提供简单的视觉成分摘要，可作为初步的营养感知提示，但不能直接估算卡路里、宏量营养素、食物质量、体积、密度或真实份量大小。

Original Abstract: Food images often contain several visible ingredients, so assigning one dish label to an entire image hides important visual structure. This work studies ingredient-level semantic segmentation on FoodSeg103, where the model predicts an ingredient class for each pixel. Two SegFormer variants were fine-tuned and evaluated under a controlled setup: SegFormer-B0 as the smaller baseline model and SegFormer-B1 as the larger final model. Both models use ImageNet-pretrained MiT backbones with newly initialized 104-class output layers. On the held-out FoodSeg103 test split of 2,135 images, B0 achieved 0.7709 pixel accuracy and 0.2521 mean IoU, while B1 achieved 0.7929 pixel accuracy and 0.3204 mean IoU. B1 improved every saved test metric, including a +0.0683 absolute gain in mean IoU. The system also converts predicted masks into visible ingredient-area percentages, giving a simple visual composition summary of the predicted meal. This summary can serve as a first-pass nutrition-awareness cue by providing a visual alternative to detailed food tracking similar to plate-based meal guidance, but it is not a direct estimate of calories, macronutrients, food mass, volume, density, or true portion size.

中文摘要翻译: 食品图像通常包含多种可见食材，因此为整张图像分配一个菜品标签会丢失重要的视觉结构。本文研究FoodSeg103数据集上的食材级语义分割，即模型为每个像素预测一个食材类别。在受控设置下微调并评估了两种SegFormer变体：较小的基线模型SegFormer-B0和较大的最终模型SegFormer-B1。两个模型均使用ImageNet预训练的MiT主干网络，并新初始化了104类输出层。在包含2,135张图像的FoodSeg103测试集上，B0达到了0.7709的像素准确率和0.2521的平均IoU，而B1达到了0.7929的像素准确率和0.3204的平均IoU。B1在所有保存的测试指标上均有改进，包括平均IoU的+0.0683绝对增益。该系统还将预测掩码转换为可见食材面积百分比，提供预测餐食的简单视觉成分摘要。该摘要可作为初步的营养感知提示，提供一种类似于基于餐盘膳食指导的、替代详细食物追踪的视觉方案，但它不能直接估算卡路里、宏量营养素、食物质量、体积、密度或真实份量大小。

</details>


### [19] [ObsGraph: Hierarchical Observation Representation for Embodied Reasoning and Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24068)
*Taekbeom Lee,Youngseok Jang,Jeonghwa Heo,Jeongjun Choi,H. Jin Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出ObsGraph，一种以观察为中心的层级场景图，统一了场景表示、检索与探索。通过房间-视图-对象三层结构，在有限预算下进行粗到细的层级检索，并利用检索结果结构化探索候选空间，实现表示、检索与自适应多尺度探索的紧密耦合，在具身推理与探索基准上提升了成功率和效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 在复杂陌生环境中，机器人需要识别并获取任务所需信息，但现有方法在场景表示、信息检索与探索决策之间缺乏紧密耦合，导致探索效率低下。本文旨在通过统一的层级场景图结构，将场景表示、检索与探索三者有机结合，提升具身推理与探索的效果。

Method: 提出ObsGraph，一种以观察为中心的层级场景图，包含房间层（粗粒度语义锚点）、视图层（保留上下文物体共视性）和对象层（存储细粒度细节）。在此基础上，在有限预算下进行粗到细的层级检索，并利用检索结果结构化探索候选空间——激活房间级探索、视图细化或前沿探索，从而实现表示、检索与自适应多尺度探索的紧密耦合。

Result: 在具身推理与探索基准上的实验表明，ObsGraph在任务成功率和探索效率方面均有提升，验证了结构化场景表示以及由信息差驱动的针对性信息收集的有效性。

Conclusion: 结构化场景表示（如ObsGraph的层级场景图）以及基于识别到的信息差进行针对性信息收集，能够显著提升具身智能体在复杂陌生环境中的推理与探索能力。

Original Abstract: Embodied reasoning and exploration are increasingly considered crucial abilities for robots operating in complex and unfamiliar environments. To accomplish tasks in such settings, an agent must identify and acquire the information necessary for the task through exploration. We propose ObsGraph, an observation-centric hierarchical scene graph that unifies scene representation, retrieval, and exploration. It retains visual evidence and organizes it into room-view-object layers: rooms provide coarse semantic anchors, views preserve contextual object covisibility, and objects store fine-grained details. On top of this representation, we perform coarse-to-fine hierarchical retrieval under a bounded budget, and crucially use retrieval outcomes to structure the exploration candidate space--activating room-level exploration, view refinement, or frontier exploration--thereby tightly coupling representation, retrieval, and adaptive multi-scale exploration. Experiments across embodied reasoning and exploration benchmarks demonstrate improved success and efficiency, highlighting the benefits of structured scene representation and more targeted information gathering driven by identified evidence gaps.

中文摘要翻译: 具身推理与探索越来越被认为是机器人在复杂陌生环境中运行的关键能力。为了在此类环境中完成任务，智能体必须通过探索来识别并获取任务所需的信息。我们提出ObsGraph，一种以观察为中心的层级场景图，它统一了场景表示、检索与探索。它保留视觉证据并将其组织为房间-视图-对象三层结构：房间提供粗粒度的语义锚点，视图保留上下文物体的共视性，对象存储细粒度细节。在此表示之上，我们在有限预算下执行从粗到细的层级检索，并关键地利用检索结果来结构化探索候选空间——激活房间级探索、视图细化或前沿探索——从而将表示、检索与自适应多尺度探索紧密耦合。在具身推理与探索基准上的实验表明，ObsGraph在成功率和效率上均有提升，凸显了结构化场景表示以及由识别到的信息差驱动的更针对性信息收集的优势。

</details>


### [20] [Fabric Image Demoiréing Benchmark from Synthesis to Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24072)
*Pengchao Wei,Xiaojie Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个织物图像去摩尔纹的标准化基准，通过物理驱动的合成框架构建大规模多分辨率数据集，并定制了基线模型，解决了织物摩尔纹因宽带半周期特性导致的去摩尔纹难题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 织物摩尔纹由精细纺织图案与相机传感器网格交互产生，其宽带半周期特性导致频谱严重重叠，使去摩尔纹问题高度病态。现有基于屏幕摩尔纹数据集训练的模型泛化能力差，而织物图像去摩尔纹缺乏标准化基准，亟需建立专用基准与数据集。

Method: 提出物理驱动的合成框架，构建包含16,050对多分辨率织物图像的配对数据集，支持可控混叠严重程度。在此基础上定制基线模型用于基准评估。

Result: 构建了首个大规模织物去摩尔纹基准数据集（16,050对），基线模型在该基准上取得了有前景的性能并展现出强泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该基准为织物图像去摩尔纹研究提供了标准化平台，推动了该方向的研究进展。

Original Abstract: Fabric moiré is a sampling-induced aliasing artifact caused by the interaction between fine textile patterns and camera sensor grids, producing structured interference that severely degrades image quality. Unlike screen-induced moiré, which stems from strictly periodic display lattices, fabric moiré is intrinsically more challenging due to the broadband and semi-periodic nature of textile weaves. The heavy spectral overlap between intrinsic texture and aliasing components renders fabric demoiréing substantially more ill-posed. Consequently, existing models trained on screen moiré datasets generalize poorly to these complex textile patterns. Despite its practical importance, fabric image demoiréing remains underexplored and lacks standardized benchmarks. We present the first comprehensive benchmark for fabric image demoiréing. To address the difficulty of acquiring pixel-aligned real-world pairs, we develop a physically motivated synthesis framework and construct a large-scale dataset comprising 16,050 paired multi-resolution fabric images with controllable aliasing severity. Furthermore, we customize a baseline model, which establishes promising performance on the proposed benchmark dataset with strong generalization ability. Our benchmark provides a standardized platform for advancing research in fabric image demoiréing.

中文摘要翻译: 织物摩尔纹是一种由精细纺织图案与相机传感器网格交互产生的采样诱导混叠伪影，其结构化干扰会严重降低图像质量。与源于严格周期性显示网格的屏幕摩尔纹不同，织物摩尔纹因纺织品的宽带和半周期特性而本质上更具挑战性。固有纹理与混叠分量之间的严重频谱重叠使得织物去摩尔纹问题高度病态。因此，在屏幕摩尔纹数据集上训练的现有模型难以泛化到这些复杂的纺织图案。尽管具有重要的实际意义，织物图像去摩尔纹仍缺乏探索且缺少标准化基准。本文提出了首个织物图像去摩尔纹的综合性基准。为解决获取像素级对齐的真实世界配对数据的困难，我们开发了一个物理驱动的合成框架，并构建了一个包含16,050对多分辨率织物图像的大规模数据集，支持可控的混叠严重程度。此外，我们定制了一个基线模型，在所提出的基准数据集上取得了有前景的性能，并展现出强泛化能力。我们的基准为推进织物图像去摩尔纹研究提供了标准化平台。

</details>


### [21] [End-to-End Radar and Communication Modulation Recognition with Neuromorphic Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24075)
*Xiaohu Li,Chongxiao Qu,Caiyong Lin,Chenxiao Dou,Wei Hua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于脉冲驱动Transformer的SNN架构EMRFormer，用于资源受限平台上的自动调制识别，在保持高精度的同时能耗降低超90%。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度学习AMR方法计算成本高，难以在精度与功耗间取得平衡，限制了在资源受限平台上的应用。

Method: 提出EMRFormer，一种端到端脉冲神经网络（SNN）架构：1）自适应脉冲编码器与整数LIF神经元缓解信息退化；2）将脉冲可分离CNN（SSCNN）集成到脉冲驱动Transformer（SpikeFormer）中，提取多尺度时序特征。

Result: 在多个主流数据集上达到SOTA精度；低SNR环境下保持强性能；理论能耗降低超90%；在KA200神经形态芯片上功耗比3090 GPU或Orin NX降低最多5倍。

Conclusion: 这项工作为资源受限设备上的AMR提供了一条有前景的路径，证明了SNN架构在兼顾精度与能效方面的潜力。

Original Abstract: Although deep learning-based methods can achieve high accuracy in automatic modulation recognition (AMR) tasks, their high computational cost makes it difficult to strike a balance between accuracy and power consumption, thereby limiting their application on resource-constrained platforms. Neuromorphic architectures that perform spike-driven inference with modest energy budgets have recently been explored for vision and timeseries tasks. Motivated by these works, we propose EMRFormer, a novel end-to-end spiking nerural network (SNN) architecture that applies spike-driven transformer to the constraints of neuromorphic hardware for AMR. The model incorporates an adaptive spike encoder and Integer Leaky Integrate-and-Fire neurons to mitigate the degradation of effective information and enhance SNN representational capacity. By integrating spike-separable Convolution Neural Networks (SSCNN) into Spike-Driven Transformers (SpikeFormer), EMRFormer effectively extracts multi-scale temporal features from the raw IQ waveforms. We validate our approach across various mainstream datasets, the experimental results show that EMRFormer achieves state-of-the-art interms of accuracy, outperforming all the baselines. Furthermore, the model maintains strong performance in low signal-to-noise(SNR) environments and reduces theoretical energy consumption by over 90%. Finally, we evaluate our model on a KA200 neuromorphic chip. The results show that our model achieves up to 5 times reduction in power compared to running on a 3090 GPU or an Orin NX. This work demonstrates a promising pathway for AMR on resource-constrained devices.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管基于深度学习的方法在自动调制识别（AMR）任务中能够实现高精度，但其高昂的计算成本使其难以在精度与功耗之间取得平衡，从而限制了其在资源受限平台上的应用。近年来，神经形态架构因其在适度能耗下执行脉冲驱动推理的能力，已在视觉和时间序列任务中得到探索。受这些工作的启发，我们提出了EMRFormer——一种新颖的端到端脉冲神经网络（SNN）架构，它将脉冲驱动Transformer应用于神经形态硬件约束下的AMR任务。该模型结合了自适应脉冲编码器和整数漏 integrate-and-fire（LIF）神经元，以缓解有效信息的退化并增强SNN的表征能力。通过将脉冲可分离卷积神经网络（SSCNN）集成到脉冲驱动Transformer（SpikeFormer）中，EMRFormer能够从原始IQ波形中有效地提取多尺度时序特征。我们在多个主流数据集上验证了该方法，实验结果表明，EMRFormer在精度上达到了最先进的水平，优于所有基线模型。此外，该模型在低信噪比（SNR）环境下仍保持良好的性能，并将理论能耗降低了90%以上。最后，我们在KA200神经形态芯片上评估了该模型。结果显示，与在3090 GPU或Orin NX上运行相比，我们的模型功耗最多降低了5倍。这项工作为资源受限设备上的AMR展示了一条有前景的路径。

</details>


### [22] [Progressive Pixel-Neighborhood Deformable Cross-Attention for Multispectral Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24092)
*Tian Qiu,Jifeng Shen,Xin Zuo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出渐进式像素邻域可变形交叉注意力机制（PNAFusion）用于多光谱特征融合，通过局部邻域先验和可变形对齐解决跨模态特征错位与非线性空间映射问题，在多个数据集上取得领先性能，同时降低计算开销。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有全局交叉注意力虽具有强长程建模能力，但其计算复杂度随特征尺寸呈二次增长，难以部署在资源受限平台。同时，可见光与热成像图像间的弱错位通常集中在局部邻域，且跨模态语义对应往往遵循非线性空间映射，固定感受野难以有效建模。

Method: 提出PNAFusion框架，包含两个核心模块：(1) 像素邻域交叉注意力（PNCA）模块，避免冗余全局特征匹配并抑制背景噪声；(2) 自适应可变形对齐（ADA）模块，通过学习逐像素偏移捕获非线性空间对应关系。两个模块通过迭代反馈机制渐进式地优化跨模态特征对齐。

Result: 在FLIR、M3FD和DroneVehicle数据集上，基于YOLOv5检测器分别达到84.2、90.5和85.5 mAP@0.5；迁移至Co-DETR后在FLIR和M3FD上分别达到86.8和90.8 mAP@0.5。相比ICAFusion，GPU显存降低33.0%，理论FLOPs从194.8G降至156.4G，但可变形采样和迭代优化带来了额外延迟。

Conclusion: 通过引入局部空间先验和可变形对齐，PNAFusion在实现高效跨模态特征融合的同时显著降低了计算资源需求，验证了在资源受限平台上进行多光谱目标检测的可行性。

Original Abstract: Effective cross-modal feature alignment and interaction are central challenges in multispectral object detection. Although global cross-attention provides strong long-range modeling ability, its quadratic complexity with respect to feature size limits deployment on resource-constrained platforms. We therefore propose Progressive Pixel-Neighborhood Deformable Cross-Attention for multispectral feature fusion, termed PNAFusion. The proposed framework is motivated by two observations: weak misalignment between visible and thermal images is usually concentrated around local neighborhoods, and semantic correspondence across modalities often follows non-linear spatial mappings that fixed receptive fields cannot model well. To address these issues, PNAFusion incorporates local spatial priors into its architectural design to concentrate feature interaction and alignment on the most relevant neighborhoods. Specifically, a Pixel-Neighborhood Cross-Attention (PNCA) module is introduced to avoid redundant global feature matching and suppress background noise. Meanwhile, an Adaptive Deformable Alignment (ADA) module captures non-linear spatial correspondences through learned pixel-wise offsets. These components are further integrated through an iterative feedback mechanism to progressively refine cross-modal feature alignment. Experiments on FLIR, M3FD, and DroneVehicle show that PNAFusion achieves 84.2, 90.5, and 85.5 mAP@0.5, respectively, under the YOLOv5 detector, and further reaches 86.8 mAP@0.5 on FLIR and 90.8 mAP@0.5 on M3FD when transferred to Co-DETR. Efficiency analysis indicates that PNAFusion reduces allocated GPU memory by 33.0\% compared with ICAFusion and reduces theoretical FLOPs from 194.8 G to 156.4 G, although the deformable sampling and iterative refinement introduce additional latency. Our code will be available at https://github.com/DanielQiuTian/PNAFusion.

中文摘要翻译: 有效的跨模态特征对齐与交互是多光谱目标检测的核心挑战。尽管全局交叉注意力具有强大的长程建模能力，但其计算复杂度随特征尺寸呈二次增长，限制了其在资源受限平台上的部署。为此，我们提出渐进式像素邻域可变形交叉注意力用于多光谱特征融合，命名为PNAFusion。该框架的动机源于两个观测结果：可见光与热红外图像之间的弱错位通常集中在局部邻域附近，而跨模态的语义对应往往遵循非线性空间映射，固定感受野难以有效建模。为解决这些问题，PNAFusion将局部空间先验融入架构设计，将特征交互与对齐集中到最相关的邻域上。具体而言，引入像素邻域交叉注意力（PNCA）模块以避免冗余的全局特征匹配并抑制背景噪声；同时，自适应可变形对齐（ADA）模块通过学习逐像素偏移来捕获非线性空间对应关系。这些组件通过迭代反馈机制进一步集成，以渐进式地优化跨模态特征对齐。在FLIR、M3FD和DroneVehicle上的实验表明，基于YOLOv5检测器，PNAFusion分别达到84.2、90.5和85.5的mAP@0.5；当迁移至Co-DETR时，在FLIR和M3FD上进一步达到86.8和90.8 mAP@0.5。效率分析表明，与ICAFusion相比，PNAFusion分配的GPU显存降低33.0%，理论FLOPs从194.8 G降至156.4 G，尽管可变形采样和迭代优化引入了额外延迟。我们的代码将在https://github.com/DanielQiuTian/PNAFusion开源。

</details>


### [23] [Universal Guideline-Driven Image Clustering via a Hybrid LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24094)
*Wenliang Zhong,Rob Barton,Lucas Goncalves,Kushal Kumar,Feng Jiang,Hehuan Ma,Yuzhi Guo,Vidit Bansal,Karim Bouyarmane,Junzhou Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个基于文本指导的统一图像聚类框架（GDC Agent），通过生成式概念代理建模和LLM最小生成树遍历，无需任务特定训练即可泛化到多种聚类场景，性能超越专门方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 不同图像聚类任务（如粗粒度/细粒度、全局/局部标准、平衡/长尾分布）之间存在根本性差距，现有方法往往针对特定场景设计，缺乏能够统一处理多种聚类场景的通用框架。

Method: 1) Guideline-Driven Image Clustering Agent：通过文本指导桥接不同聚类场景的统一框架。2) Generative Concept Proxy Modeling：通过概念代理提取生成受指导感知的嵌入，无需任务特定训练。3) LLM Traversal based on Minimum Spanning Tree：基于最小生成树的LLM遍历，选择性地应用LLM推理处理复杂语义判断，实现自动聚类发现。

Result: 在从一般到细粒度分类、全局到局部标准、平衡到长尾分布的多种聚类场景中，该方法一致地优于各类专门方法。

Conclusion: 提出的统一框架通过文本指导有效弥合了不同图像聚类场景之间的差距，展示了在多种聚类任务上的通用性和优越性能。

Original Abstract: Unifying image clustering across different clustering scenarios remains challenging due to fundamental gaps among tasks. We introduce a Guideline-Driven Image Clustering Agent, the first universal framework that bridges these gaps through textual guidelines. To incorporate complex guidelines without task-specific training, we propose Generative Concept Proxy Modeling, which generates guideline-aware embeddings via concept proxy extraction. For scenarios requiring automatic cluster discovery, we introduce LLM Traversal based on Minimum Spanning Tree that selectively applies LLM reasoning for complex semantic judgments. Our method generalizes across diverse clustering scenarios spanning from general to fine-grained categorization, from global to local criteria, and from balanced to long-tail distributions. Our framework consistently outperforms specialized methods across diverse clustering tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 由于不同聚类任务之间存在根本性差距，统一不同聚类场景下的图像聚类仍然是具有挑战性的问题。我们提出了一种基于指导驱动的图像聚类代理（Guideline-Driven Image Clustering Agent），这是首个通过文本指导桥接这些差距的通用框架。为了在不进行任务特定训练的情况下融入复杂指导，我们提出了生成式概念代理建模（Generative Concept Proxy Modeling），该方法通过概念代理提取生成受指导感知的嵌入。对于需要自动聚类发现的场景，我们引入了基于最小生成树的LLM遍历（LLM Traversal based on Minimum Spanning Tree），该方法选择性地应用LLM推理来处理复杂语义判断。我们的方法可泛化到多种聚类场景，涵盖从一般到细粒度分类、从全局到局部标准、以及从平衡到长尾分布。该框架在多种聚类任务上一致优于专门方法。

</details>


### [24] [Beyond Bayer: Task-Optimal Sensor Co-Design for Robust Autonomous-Driving Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24096)
*Reeshad Khan,John Gauch*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探讨自动驾驶感知中传感器本身（相机）的测量方式对任务性能的影响，通过可微的RAW到任务流程，发现学习彩色滤波阵列（CFA）权重是提升性能的关键，而光学系统（点扩散函数）的联合设计反而有害，并给出了简单有效的传感器设计建议。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有自动驾驶感知研究主要关注扩大模型规模（更大的骨干网络、基础模型、多智能体融合），但忽略了上游问题：相机传感器本身应该如何测量？本文旨在从传感器层面挖掘哪些自由度对密集预测任务有益。

Method: 构建了一个可微的RAW到任务（RAW-to-task）流程，将传感器参数（CFA权重、点扩散函数PSF、噪声）纳入端到端优化，分解每个传感器自由度对密集预测任务（语义分割）的贡献。

Result: 1）学习CFA权重是最大的增益杠杆，在KITTI-360上mIoU提升+0.017，在ACDC上提升+0.023；2）点扩散函数联合设计反而带来负收益（KITTI-360上-0.020 mIoU），由数据处理不等式约束；3）噪声联合优化效果微弱；4）扩大CFA图块超过2x2反而持续损害性能，因为滤波器被限制在秩为3的sRGB输入中；5）增益与下游模型无关，在雾、夜、雨、雪等恶劣天气下均表现鲁棒。

Conclusion: 最佳方案是：学习2x2的CFA权重，保持单位点扩散函数（identity PSF）。这提供了简单且模型无关的传感器级改进策略。

Original Abstract: Robust perception underpins autonomous driving, and most recent progress comes from scaling the model-larger backbones, foundation models, and cooperative multi-agent fusion. We pursue a complementary, upstream question: what should the camera itself measure? Using a differentiable RAW-to-task pipeline, we decompose which sensor degrees of freedom benefit dense prediction. Learning the spectral colour-filter-array (CFA) weights is the dominant lever, improving mIoU by +0.017 (KITTI-360) and +0.023 (ACDC) over a fixed camera. In contrast, point-spread-function (optics) co-design is net-negative (-0.020 mIoU on KITTI-360) - a consequence of the data-processing inequality, which also bounds the task information that any downstream model, however large or cooperative, can recover. Noise co-optimisation is marginal, and counter to intuition enlarging the CFA tile beyond 2x2 consistently hurts, as the filters are confined to the rank three sRGB input. Because the intervention is at the sensor, the gains are model-agnostic; we validate robustness on ACDC's fog, night, rain, and snow, and conclude with a simple recipe: learn the 2x2 CFA weights and keep an identity PSF.

中文摘要翻译: 鲁棒的感知是自动驾驶的基础，而最近的大多数进展来自于扩展模型——更大的骨干网络、基础模型以及多智能体协同融合。我们探索了一个互补的上游问题：相机本身应该测量什么？通过一个可微的RAW到任务流程，我们分解了哪些传感器自由度有益于密集预测。学习光谱彩色滤波阵列（CFA）权重是主要的性能杠杆，相较于固定相机，在KITTI-360上mIoU提升0.017，在ACDC上提升0.023。相比之下，点扩散函数（光学系统）的联合设计为净负收益（在KITTI-360上mIoU下降0.020）——这是数据处理不等式的后果，该不等式也限制了任何下游模型（无论多大或多协同）能够恢复的任务信息。噪声联合优化效果微弱，且与直觉相反，将CFA图块扩大到2x2以上持续带来性能损害，因为滤波器被限制在秩为3的sRGB输入中。由于干预在传感器层面，因此增益与模型无关；我们在ACDC的雾、夜、雨、雪场景下验证了鲁棒性，并最终得出一个简单的方案：学习2x2的CFA权重，保持单位点扩散函数。

</details>


### [25] [DramaDirector: Geometry-Guided Short Drama Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24107)
*Hengji Zhou,Sijie Liu,Jianrun Chen,Xingchen Zou,Lianghao Xia,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DramaDirector是一个基于几何约束的框架，通过从真实短剧镜头库中检索深度和姿态信息来引导第一帧生成和图像到视频的合成，从而将全局情节和局部上下文转化为视觉上连贯的多镜头短剧视频。同时提出了包含35部真人剧、2.8K集、81K镜头的DramaBoard基准数据集。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 短剧具有快节奏镜头切换、对话驱动的焦点转移和苛刻的摄影基础要求，现有的基于提示词或纯文本的视频生成流程难以满足这些挑战，因此需要一种能够将情节转化为视觉上扎实的多镜头视频的方法。

Method: DramaDirector将每个镜头解耦为静态视觉条件和动态叙事条件，通过模式约束的SFT和GRPO结合文本-视觉对齐奖励来训练规划器，并从真实短剧镜头库中检索深度-姿态参考来引导第一帧生成和图像到视频的合成。

Result: 实验表明DramaDirector在忠实度、一致性和可控性上优于代表性的多智能体和视频生成基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过引入摄影几何约束和结构化情节-镜头映射，DramaDirector有效解决了短剧生成中对摄影构图和镜头连贯性的需求，为情节到短剧的自动生成提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Short dramas, with their rapid shot rhythms, dialogue-driven focus shifts, and demanding cinematographic grounding, pose challenges that prompt-level or text-only video generation pipelines struggle to meet. We study plot-to-short-drama generation, where a global plot and local context are transformed into visually grounded multi-shot videos. We propose DramaDirector, a geometry-grounded framework that lets the planner borrow cinematographic geometry from a gallery of real short-drama shots indexed by depth and pose. DramaDirector decouples each shot into static visual and dynamic narrative conditions, trains the planner with schema-constrained SFT and GRPO under a learned text-visual alignment reward, and retrieves depth-pose references to guide first-frame generation and image-to-video synthesis. We also introduce DramaBoard, a benchmark built from 35 live-action dramas, 2.8K episodes, and 81K shots, with structured storyboards and multi-dimensional evaluation protocols. Experiments show that DramaDirector improves over representative multi-agent and video generation baselines on faithfulness, consistency, and controllability. Our code is released at: https://github.com/iLearn-Lab/DramaDirector

中文摘要翻译: 短剧因其快速的镜头节奏、对话驱动的焦点转移以及对摄影基础的苛刻要求，给基于提示词或纯文本的视频生成流程带来了难以克服的挑战。本文研究情节到短剧的生成任务，即将全局情节和局部上下文转化为视觉上扎实的多镜头视频。我们提出DramaDirector，一个基于几何约束的框架，使规划器能够从以深度和姿态索引的真实短剧镜头库中借用摄影几何信息。DramaDirector将每个镜头解耦为静态视觉条件和动态叙事条件，通过模式约束的SFT和GRPO结合学习到的文本-视觉对齐奖励来训练规划器，并检索深度-姿态参考以引导第一帧生成和图像到视频的合成。我们还引入了DramaBoard基准数据集，该数据集由35部真人剧、2.8K集和81K个镜头构成，配有结构化的故事板和多维评估协议。实验表明，DramaDirector在忠实度、一致性和可控性上优于代表性的多智能体和视频生成基线方法。我们的代码已开源：https://github.com/iLearn-Lab/DramaDirector

</details>


### [26] [A Benchmark for Hallucination Detection in VLMs for Gastrointestinal Endoscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24115)
*Aminu Lawal,Niyoj Oli,Sachin Acharya,Prashnna Gyawali,Maria Carmen Romano,Binod Bhattarai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在胃肠道内镜VQA数据集Gut-VLM上系统评估了9种幻觉检测方法，涵盖黑盒、灰盒和白盒方法。结果表明，白盒方法ReXTrust在所有5个VLM上表现最优，AUC最高达93.0；白盒隐状态访问平均带来19.5 AUC点的优势。此外，论文识别了"自信幻觉"这一系统性失败模式。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法主要在放射学基准（如MIMIC-CXR、VQA-RAD）上评估，而胃肠道内镜领域的幻觉检测研究严重不足。本文旨在填补这一空白，系统评估多种幻觉检测方法在胃肠道内镜VQA任务上的表现。

Method: 在Gut-VLM数据集（4,392个测试VQA对）上，对5个VLM（MedGemma-4B/27B、LLaVA-Med-7B、LLaVA-v1.6-7B、Lingshu-32B）评估9种幻觉检测方法，分为三类：黑盒方法（RadFlag、SelfCheckGPT-NLI）、灰盒方法（AvgProb、AvgEnt、MaxProb、MaxEnt、Semantic Entropy、VASE）和白盒方法（ReXTrust）。

Result: 1) 白盒方法ReXTrust在所有5个模型上AUC最高，峰值达93.0（MedGemma-4B），显著优于其他方法（p<0.001）；2) 白盒隐状态访问平均带来19.5 AUC点优势（范围9.5-33.5）；3) 在LLaVA-v1.6-7B上，黑盒和聚类灰盒方法接近随机水平，而ReXTrust仍达AUC 79.9；4) 非白盒方法中，token级灰盒统计（MaxEnt、MaxProb）最优；5) 识别了"自信幻觉"这一系统性失败模式。

Conclusion: 白盒方法（利用模型隐状态）在胃肠道内镜VLM幻觉检测中显著优于黑盒和灰盒方法，但"自信幻觉"（模型以高一致性或高概率产生幻觉）对所有基于一致性和不确定性的方法构成系统性挑战。

Original Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are prone to hallucination, which remains a major barrier to their safe deployment in clinical practice. To date, most hallucination detection methods have been evaluated on radiology benchmarks such as MIMIC-CXR and VQA-RAD, while gastrointestinal (GI) endoscopy remains largely underexplored. In this paper, we benchmark nine hallucination detection methods on the Gut-VLM dataset, a GI diagnostic Visual Question Answering (VQA) dataset with 4,392 test VQA pairs, across five VLMs (MedGemma-4B, MedGemma-27B, LLaVA-Med-7B, LLaVA-v1.6-7B, and Lingshu-32B). The methods span three categories: black-box methods (RadFlag, SelfCheckGPT-NLI), gray-box methods (AvgProb, AvgEnt, MaxProb, MaxEnt, Semantic Entropy, and VASE), and a white-box method (ReXTrust). Our results show that ReXTrust, a white-box method, achieves the highest AUC across all five models, outperforming the strongest alternative method on each VLM by a statistically significant margin (paired permutation test, p < 0.001 in all cases), reaching a peak AUC of 93.0 on MedGemma-4B. White-box hidden-state access provides a consistent advantage of 19.5 AUC points on average (range: 9.5--33.5), with ReXTrust maintaining strong performance even on LLaVA-v1.6-7B (AUC 79.9), where black-box methods and clustering-based gray-box methods collapse to near-chance performance. Among non-white-box methods, token-level gray-box statistics (MaxEnt, MaxProb) are the strongest alternatives, outperforming both clustering-based gray-box methods (Semantic Entropy, VASE) and black-box approaches on average. We further identify confident confabulation, a failure mode in which models hallucinate with high inter-sample consistency or high token-level probability, as a systemic failure for both consistency and uncertainty-based methods.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型（VLM）容易产生幻觉，这仍然是其在临床实践中安全部署的主要障碍。迄今为止，大多数幻觉检测方法都在放射学基准（如MIMIC-CXR和VQA-RAD）上评估，而胃肠道内镜检查在很大程度上仍未得到探索。本文在Gut-VLM数据集上对九种幻觉检测方法进行了基准测试，该数据集是一个胃肠道诊断视觉问答（VQA）数据集，包含4,392个测试VQA对，涉及五个VLM（MedGemma-4B、MedGemma-27B、LLaVA-Med-7B、LLaVA-v1.6-7B和Lingshu-32B）。这些方法涵盖三类：黑盒方法（RadFlag、SelfCheckGPT-NLI）、灰盒方法（AvgProb、AvgEnt、MaxProb、MaxEnt、Semantic Entropy和VASE）以及白盒方法（ReXTrust）。我们的结果表明，白盒方法ReXTrust在所有五个模型上取得了最高的AUC，以统计显著的优势（配对置换检验，所有情况下p<0.001）超过了每个VLM上最强的替代方法，在MedGemma-4B上达到了93.0的峰值AUC。白盒隐状态访问平均带来19.5 AUC点的持续优势（范围：9.5-33.5），ReXTrust即使在LLaVA-v1.6-7B上也保持了强劲性能（AUC 79.9），而在该模型上黑盒方法和基于聚类的灰盒方法性能跌至接近随机水平。在非白盒方法中，token级灰盒统计量（MaxEnt、MaxProb）是最强的替代方案，平均优于基于聚类的灰盒方法（Semantic Entropy、VASE）和黑盒方法。我们进一步识别了自信幻觉（confident confabulation）——一种模型以高样本间一致性或高token级概率产生幻觉的失败模式，这是基于一致性和基于不确定性的方法共同面临的系统性失败。

</details>


### [27] [An LMM for Precisely Grounding Elements in Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24118)
*Yijian Lu,Chuangxin Zhao,Kai Sun,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li,Ji Qi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PreciseDoc提出了一种专门用于文档图像中精确元素定位的大多模态模型，通过合成高质量文档数据与强化学习联合训练定位与推理能力，显著提升了文档空间定位和文档理解的精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的大多模态模型在文本密集型文档图像中定位精度差，难以准确找到关键文档元素以支持可靠推理。

Method: 构建了两种高质量合成文档数据管道以生成海量带细粒度坐标标注的训练数据（包括含相机效果的合成手填文档）；引入视觉定位推理训练范式，通过强化学习联合监督定位与推理，提升定位证据对推理的贡献。

Result: 在多个基准上综合评估表明，所提出的数据与方法在文档空间定位和文档理解方面具有优势。

Conclusion: PreciseDoc通过高质量合成数据与强化学习联合训练，有效提升了文档图像中元素定位的精度，并能够优化应用于文档VQA任务。

Original Abstract: Visual grounding in documents is a crucial ability for Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) in areas such as document understanding, deep research and document error detection. However, existing approaches exhibit poor grounding precision in text-rich document images, often failing to accurately locate the critical document elements needed for reliable reasoning. To address this gap, we introduce PreciseDoc, an LMM specifically designed for precise element grounding and can be further optimized for Document VQA tasks. Specifically, to enhance the basic localization capability, we construct challenging training data by two pipelines capable of mass-producing high-quality documents with paired metadata of fine-grained coordinates, including synthetic hand-filled documents with camera effects. The model develops more real-world functions beyond straightforward localization of single text, such as locating personal information from CVs. Furthermore, we introduce a training paradigm for visual grounded reasoning where the grounding and reasoning are supervised jointly with reinforcement learning to improve the contribution of the grounded evidence. A comprehensive evaluation on various benchmarks demonstrates the advantage of the proposed data and methods in document spatial grounding and document understanding.

中文摘要翻译: 文档中的视觉定位是大规模多模态模型在文档理解、深度研究和文档错误检测等领域的关键能力。然而，现有方法在文本密集型文档图像中的定位精度较差，常常无法准确定位可靠推理所需的关键文档元素。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了PreciseDoc，这是一种专为精确元素定位设计的大多模态模型，并可进一步优化用于文档VQA任务。具体而言，为了增强基本的定位能力，我们通过两种能够大规模生产高质量文档及其细粒度坐标配对元数据的管道构建了具有挑战性的训练数据，包括具有相机效果的合成手填文档。该模型还开发了超越简单单文本定位的真实世界功能，例如从简历中定位个人信息。此外，我们引入了一种视觉定位推理训练范式，其中定位和推理通过强化学习联合监督，以提高定位证据对推理的贡献。对多种基准的综合评估证明了所提出的数据和方法在文档空间定位和文档理解方面的优势。

</details>


### [28] [Bengal-HP_RU: A Dataset of Bengal People For Head Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24122)
*Md. Ahanaf Arif Khan,Md. Tawhidur Rahman,Sangeeta Biswas,Md. Iqbal Aziz Khan,Subrata Pramanik,Sanjoy Kumar Chakravarty,Bimal Kumar Pramanik*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Bengal-HP_RU，首个面向孟加拉人群的公开头部姿态数据集，包含12,894张标注了连续俯仰角、偏航角和滚转角的头部图像，旨在填补南亚人群在头部姿态估计研究中的数据空白。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的头部姿态数据集主要包含西方或东亚受试者，南亚人群（尤其是孟加拉人）严重缺乏代表性，因此需要构建一个面向孟加拉人群的专用数据集以促进相关研究的公平性和多样性。

Method: 从维基共享资源（Wikimedia Commons）收集免费许可图片，经过自动化流水线处理后再进行人工标签校正；按维基上传者身份划分数据集以避免数据污染，最终得到来自296个独立上传者的10,494张训练图像和2,400张测试图像。

Result: 构建了包含12,894张标注图像的数据集，训练/测试集按上传者身份严格划分（10,494/2,400），数据集在年龄、性别、遮挡、光照和背景等方面展现出丰富的多样性，反映了真实的野外环境条件。

Conclusion: Bengal-HP_RU作为首个面向孟加拉人群的公开头部姿态数据集，填补了该人群在头部姿态估计研究中的空白，为促进南亚人群相关计算机视觉研究提供了重要资源。

Original Abstract: Existing head pose datasets predominantly feature subjects of Western or East Asian origin, leaving South Asian populations, particularly Bengali individuals, largely underrepresented. We introduce Bengal-HP_RU, the first publicly available head pose dataset centred on Bengali subjects, comprising 12,894 labelled head images annotated with continuous yaw, pitch, and roll values. Images were collected from Wikimedia Commons under free licences and processed through an automated pipeline followed by manual label correction. The dataset is partitioned by Wikimedia uploader identity to prevent data contamination, yielding 10,494 training and 2,400 test images across 296 unique uploaders. Bengal-HP_RU exhibits substantial diversity in subject age, gender, occlusion, illumination, and background, reflecting realistic in-the-wild conditions. The dataset is publicly available at https://doi.org/10.17632/xbw9kr37jb.2.

中文摘要翻译: 现有的头部姿态数据集主要包含西方或东亚血统的受试者，而南亚人群（尤其是孟加拉人）在很大程度上缺乏代表性。我们提出了Bengal-HP_RU，这是首个面向孟加拉受试者的公开头部姿态数据集，包含12,894张标注的头部图像，并附有连续的偏航角、俯仰角和滚转角标注。图像从维基共享资源（Wikimedia Commons）中依据免费许可收集，经过自动化流水线处理，随后进行人工标签校正。数据集按维基上传者身份进行划分以防止数据污染，最终得到来自296个独立上传者的10,494张训练图像和2,400张测试图像。Bengal-HP_RU在受试者年龄、性别、遮挡、光照和背景方面表现出显著的多样性，反映了真实的野外环境条件。该数据集已在https://doi.org/10.17632/xbw9kr37jb.2公开发布。

</details>


### [29] [Sat2City v2: Native 3D City Asset Generation from a Single Satellite Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24138)
*Tongyan Hua,Dongli Wu,Jinjing Zhu,Yinrui Ren,Zhongcheng Hong,Ying-Cong Chen,Hui Xiong,Wufan Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Sat2City v2 是一个从单张卫星图像生成显式3D城市资产（带纹理网格）的框架，通过适配预训练的3D基础模型，在真实世界卫星-网格配对数据集上实现了可控外观的几何与纹理生成，性能优于现有基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有卫星图像到3D城市生成方法（如Sat2City）存在外观随机、训练数据为合成数据、任务专用VAE难以扩展到真实噪声重建等问题。此外，许多方法仅生成适合渲染的3D代理而非可复用的显式纹理网格。因此需要一种能生成具有合理几何结构和可控外观的显式纹理网格资产的方法。

Method: Sat2City v2 适配预训练的本地结构潜空间3D基础模型到弱对齐的卫星图像和纹理网格。首先构建包含9个城市24个区域共16,241个卫星-网格配对数据的真实世界数据集。将每个网格编码到预训练的本地3D潜空间，微调卫星条件几何流，然后使用解码后的形状锚定卫星条件纹理生成，保持Sat2City的几何到外观级联架构。

Result: 在度量尺度DSM重建和生成式城市资产基准（几何与外观）上的实验表明，Sat2City v2 在所有评估基线中取得了最佳综合性能。

Conclusion: Sat2City v2 将卫星到城市生成从面向渲染的3D代理推进到显式纹理网格资产，并提供了首个针对该资产级任务从地理匹配裁剪中收集的卫星-网格配对真实数据集。

Original Abstract: Generating explicit 3D city assets from a single satellite image is important for digital twins, urban simulation, and geospatial intelligence. Unlike satellite-to-street-view synthesis, the task requires a reusable textured mesh with plausible geometry and controllable appearance rather than a 3D proxy optimized only for rendering a small set of images or videos. The ICCV Sat2City framework made a first step by conditioning cascaded sparse-voxel latent diffusion on satellite-derived height maps, but its appearance was random, its training data were synthetic, and its task-specific VAE did not scale well to noisy real-world reconstructions. We present Sat2City v2, a journal extension that adapts a pretrained native structured-latent 3D foundation model to weakly aligned satellite images and textured meshes. We build a real-world dataset with 16,241 satellite-mesh pairs across 24 regions in 9 cities. Instead of learning a 3D representation from noisy city meshes, Sat2City v2 encodes each mesh into a pretrained native 3D latent space, fine-tunes a satellite-conditioned geometry flow, and uses the decoded shape to anchor satellite-conditioned texturing. This retains Sat2City's geometry-to-appearance cascade while enabling appearance-controllable generation from the satellite input. Experiments on metric-scale DSM reconstruction and generative city-asset benchmarks for geometry and appearance show that Sat2City v2 achieves the best overall performance among evaluated baselines. Overall, Sat2City v2 advances satellite-to-city generation from rendering-oriented 3D proxies to explicit textured mesh assets, supported by, to the best of our knowledge, the first documented satellite-mesh paired dataset collected from matched geographic crops for this asset-level task. Project page: https://ai4city-hkust.github.io/Sat2City-v2/
