  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法面临两难困境：数据驱动方法需低效地扩大参数量来隐式学习旋转，而严格等变网络缺乏现代VFM的语义能力。当前框架通常冻结VFM，将旋转泛化的全部负担推给下游解码器，形成架构瓶颈。

Method: 提出旋转等变蒸馏（REDI）范式：将VFM的非等变语义表示蒸馏到轻量级严格旋转等变编码器中，利用等变几何架构约束高维语义。解码器配备熵驱动空间对齐模块，通过评估离散旋转假设显式锁定规范坐标系，消除全局歧义后再进行连续精细优化。

Result: 在多个基准上达到新SOTA，SatAst数据集上姿态精度绝对提升13.89%，速度比RoMa v2快1.9倍，在单张RTX 4090上实现约41 FPS的实时推理。

Conclusion: REDI-Match通过旋转等变蒸馏有效解耦了语义表达与旋转泛化的学习，打破了现有架构瓶颈，实现了精度与效率的双重提升。

Original Abstract: Vision Foundation Models (VFMs) have significantly advanced dense feature matching, yet severe in-plane rotation remains a critical challenge. Existing solutions face a fundamental dilemma: data-driven methods require inefficient parameter scaling to implicitly learn rotations, whereas strictly equivariant networks lack the semantic capacity of modern VFMs. Consequently, current frameworks typically freeze VFMs and shift the entire burden of rotation generalization to the downstream decoder. To break this architectural bottleneck, we propose REDI-Match, an efficient framework driven by a novel Rotation-Equivariant Distillation (REDI) paradigm. Instead of relying on rotation data augmentation to establish rotational correspondences, REDI distills the non-equivariant semantic representations of a VFM into a lightweight, strictly rotation-equivariant encoder, leveraging an equivariant geometric architecture to constrain robust high-dimensional semantics. To fully exploit these features, we equip the decoder with an entropy-driven spatial alignment module. By evaluating discrete rotation hypotheses, this mechanism explicitly locks onto the canonical coordinate system, eliminating global ambiguity before continuous refinement. Extensive experiments demonstrate that REDI-Match establishes a new state-of-the-art (SOTA) across multiple benchmarks. Notably, it achieves a 13.89% absolute pose accuracy improvement on the highly challenging SatAst dataset while operating 1.9x faster than the current SOTA (RoMa v2), enabling real-time inference (~41 FPS) on a single RTX 4090 GPU. Code: https://github.com/YinjiGe/REDI-Match.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉基础模型（VFM）显著推动了密集特征匹配的发展，但严重的平面内旋转仍然是一个关键挑战。现有方法面临一个根本性困境：数据驱动方法需要低效的参数扩展来隐式学习旋转，而严格等变网络缺乏现代VFM的语义能力。因此，当前框架通常冻结VFM，将旋转泛化的全部负担转移到下游解码器。为了打破这一架构瓶颈，我们提出了REDI-Match，一个由新颖的旋转等变蒸馏（REDI）范式驱动的高效框架。REDI不依赖旋转数据增强来建立旋转对应关系，而是将VFM的非等变语义表示蒸馏到一个轻量级的严格旋转等变编码器中，利用等变几何架构来约束鲁棒的高维语义。为了充分利用这些特征，我们为解码器配备了一个熵驱动的空间对齐模块。通过评估离散旋转假设，该机制显式锁定规范坐标系，在连续精细优化之前消除全局模糊性。大量实验表明，REDI-Match在多个基准上建立了新的最先进水平（SOTA）。值得注意的是，在极具挑战性的SatAst数据集上，它实现了13.89%的绝对姿态精度提升，同时运行速度比当前SOTA（RoMa v2）快1.9倍，在单张RTX 4090 GPU上实现了实时推理（~41 FPS）。代码：https://github.com/YinjiGe/REDI-Match。

</details>


### [60] [UniTranslator: A Unified Multi-modal Framework for End-to-end In-Image Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24333)
*Jiahao Lyu,Pei Fu,Zhenhang Li,Shaojie Zhang,Jiahui Yang,Yu Zhou,Can Ma,Zhenbo Luo,Jian Luan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: UniTranslator是一个统一的图像内机器翻译（IIMT）多模态框架，通过理解-生成对齐模块（UGAM）和空间掩码解码器（SMD）解决理解-生成冲突和空间位置错位问题，在多个基准测试上达到最先进性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的统一多模态模型直接应用于图像内机器翻译时存在两个关键问题：1）理解-生成冲突——翻译推理结果与生成阶段使用的文本监督不一致；2）空间位置错位——渲染的文本无法准确匹配目标文本区域。为解决这些问题，需要一种能紧密耦合翻译理解和文本编辑的统一框架。

Method: 提出UniTranslator统一多模态框架，包含两个核心模块：1）理解-生成对齐模块（UGAM），通过桥接理解与生成之间的表示间隙，促进翻译内容预测与文本渲染之间的语义一致性；2）空间掩码解码器（SMD），通过对文本区域进行像素级监督，提升生成过程中的空间定位、几何对齐和布局可控性。

Result: 在多个基准测试上的大量实验表明，UniTranslator在多种语言方向和复杂的真实世界布局下均达到了最先进的性能。实验结果还揭示了翻译理解与图像生成之间存在强烈的相互促进效应。

Conclusion: UniTranslator通过统一的多模态翻译学习框架，有效解决了IIMT中的理解-生成冲突和空间错位问题，验证了翻译理解与图像生成之间的相互增强效果，凸显了统一翻译多模态学习的优势。

Original Abstract: In-Image Machine Translation (IIMT) aims to translate scene text in an image and render the translated text back into the original regions while preserving the overall visual appearance. Recent unified multimodal models provide a promising solution by combining visual-text understanding and image generation within a single framework. However, directly adapting such models to IIMT remains challenging. In particular, they often suffer from understanding-generation conflicts, where the translation inferred during understanding is inconsistent with the text supervision used in generation, and spatial position misalignment, where the rendered text does not accurately match the target text regions. To address these issues, we present UniTranslator, a unified multimodal framework for IIMT that tightly couples translation understanding and text editing. Specifically, we introduce an Understand-Generation Alignment Module (UGAM) to bridge the representation gap between understanding and generation, encouraging semantic consistency between translated content prediction and text rendering. We further propose a Spatial Mask Decoder (SMD) with pixel-level supervision over text regions to improve spatial grounding, geometric alignment, and layout controllability during generation. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks demonstrate that UniTranslator achieves state-of-the-art performance across diverse language directions and complex real-world layouts. Moreover, our results reveal a strong mutual reinforcement effect between translation understanding and image generation, highlighting the advantage of unified translation multimodal learning. Code is available at https://github.com/SeerRay-Lab/Unitranslator.

中文摘要翻译: 图像内机器翻译（IIMT）旨在翻译图像中的场景文本，并在保留整体视觉外观的同时将翻译后的文本渲染回原始区域。最近统一的跨模态模型通过将视觉-文本理解和图像生成结合在单一框架内，提供了一种有前景的解决方案。然而，直接将此类模型应用于IIMT仍然具有挑战性。特别是，它们常常面临理解-生成冲突（翻译推理结果与生成过程中使用的文本监督不一致）以及空间位置错位（渲染的文本未能精确匹配目标文本区域）的问题。为了解决这些问题，我们提出了UniTranslator，一个用于IIMT的统一多模态框架，该框架紧密耦合了翻译理解与文本编辑。具体来说，我们引入了理解-生成对齐模块（UGAM），以弥合理解与生成之间的表示差距，促进翻译内容预测与文本渲染之间的语义一致性。我们进一步提出了空间掩码解码器（SMD），通过对文本区域进行像素级监督，以提升生成过程中的空间定位、几何对齐和布局可控性。在多个基准测试上的大量实验表明，UniTranslator在多种语言方向和复杂的真实世界布局下均达到了最先进的性能。此外，我们的结果揭示了翻译理解与图像生成之间存在强烈的相互促进效应，凸显了统一翻译多模态学习的优势。代码已开源：https://github.com/SeerRay-Lab/Unitranslator。

</details>


### [61] [Ill-Posed by Design: Probing Evidence Use in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24335)
*Boaz Meivar,Shaked Perek,Shani Shvartzman,Eli Schwartz,Shai Avidan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将单目度量物体尺寸估计作为反事实分析的新诊断场景，通过构建Metric VQA数据集并评估12个视觉语言模型，发现大模型在利用场景几何线索方面存在显著不足，即使经过LoRA微调也无法有效学习利用几何信息。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有反事实分析在良态任务（well-posed tasks）上诊断价值有限，因为多个线索独立支持同一答案时，移除一个线索不改变预测。需要一种病态（ill-posed）诊断设置来更有效地评估视觉语言模型中的证据选择能力。

Method: 1) 提出单目度量物体尺寸估计作为病态诊断任务（物理尺寸无法从单张未标定图像确定）；2) 构建Metric VQA数据集（10,813个来自Objectron的尺寸查询+331个实地测量场景）；3) 评估12个开源视觉语言模型（3B-397B参数）；4) 采用反事实分析分解六种视觉和语言证据通道；5) 进行LoRA微调干预实验。

Result: 1) 最大VLM（Qwen3-VL-235B等）在野外场景上落后于纯文本前沿LLM；2) 目标身份是最重要的线索，目标像素和局部上下文仅对部分模型有帮助；3) 外观尺寸能改变预测但缺乏方向性读取；4) 全局场景几何基本未被利用；5) LoRA微调虽可学习任务，但模型未能学会利用场景几何。

Conclusion: 当前视觉语言模型在度量估计任务中严重依赖目标身份等简单线索，而无法有效利用场景几何信息；LoRA微调作为干预手段仍不足以让模型学会利用几何线索。

Original Abstract: Counterfactual analysis is widely used to study evidence use in vision-language models, but its diagnostic value is limited on well-posed tasks: when several cues independently support the same answer, removing one may not change the prediction. We propose monocular metric object-size estimation as an ill-posed diagnostic setting for evidence selection: because physical size cannot be determined from a single uncalibrated image, models must rely on imperfect cues category priors, target appearance, local context, apparent image size, and scene geometry. We assemble Metric VQA ($10{,}813$ dimension queries from Objectron and $331$ tape-measured in-the-wild scenes) and evaluate $12$ open-weight VLMs ($3$--$397$\,B parameters) with counterfactual analysis decomposing six visual and language evidence channels. Even the largest VLMs tested (Qwen3-VL-235B, Qwen3.5-397B, InternVL3.5-241B) trail a text-only frontier LLM on the in-the-wild split. The diagnostic analysis shows: target identity is the most load-bearing cue, target pixels and local context help only some models, apparent size shifts predictions without a directional readout, and global scene geometry is largely unused. We analyze LoRA fine-tuning as an actionable intervention specific to metric estimation: while the task is learnable, the models do not learn to leverage scene geometry.

中文摘要翻译: 反事实分析被广泛用于研究视觉语言模型中的证据使用，但在良态任务中其诊断价值有限：当多个线索独立支持同一答案时，移除一个线索可能不会改变预测。我们提出将单目度量物体尺寸估计作为证据选择的病态诊断场景：因为物理尺寸无法从单张未标定图像确定，模型必须依赖不完美线索——类别先验、目标外观、局部上下文、图像表观尺寸和场景几何。我们构建了Metric VQA数据集（来自Objectron的10,813个尺寸查询和331个实地卷尺测量场景），并通过反事实分析分解六种视觉和语言证据通道，评估了12个开源VLM（3B–397B参数）。即使是测试的最大VLM（Qwen3-VL-235B、Qwen3.5-397B、InternVL3.5-241B）在野外场景分割上仍落后于纯文本前沿LLM。诊断分析表明：目标身份是最具承重能力的线索，目标像素和局部上下文仅对部分模型有帮助，表观尺寸能改变预测但缺乏方向性读取能力，全局场景几何基本未被利用。我们分析了LoRA微调作为针对度量估计的可操作干预手段：虽然任务是可学习的，但模型并未学会利用场景几何信息。

</details>


### [62] [TIGER: Taming Identity, Geometry, and Generative Priors for High-Quality Face Video Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24336)
*Yang Zhou,Wenxue Li,Peng Zhang,Yifei Chen,Fei Wang,Daiguo Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出TIGER三先验融合框架，通过身份先验、几何先验和生成先验解决人脸视频修复中的身份偏移、视角纠缠引导和感知真实性问题，实现SOTA性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法难以同时解决人脸视频修复中的三个关键挑战：身份偏移（identity shift）、视角纠缠引导（viewpoint-entangled guidance）和感知真实性（perceptual realism）。

Method: 提出TIGER框架，包含：1) 身份先验：将主体判别性嵌入注入潜空间以锚定身份；2) 几何先验：将2D参考线索提升到解耦的3D参数空间，通过跨源参数融合构建几何锚点；3) 生成先验：利用一步校正流（one-step rectified flow）高效利用视频生成模型；并设计了渐进式三阶段训练优化策略。

Result: 在身份保真度和时间稳定性上达到SOTA性能，实现了高质量、高效率且身份一致的人脸视频修复。

Conclusion: TIGER通过结构化三先验融合有效解决了FVR中的关键挑战，在身份保真度和时间稳定性方面均达到最优表现。

Original Abstract: Face Video Restoration (FVR) aims to recover high-fidelity facial videos from degraded input while preserving identity and semantic consistency across frames. Existing methods often struggle to simultaneously address three key challenges: identity shift, viewpoint-entangled guidance, and perceptual realism. To tackle these issues, we propose TIGER, a structured tri-prior fusion framework that Tames Identity, Geometry, and gEnerative pRiors for high-quality FVR. Specifically, an Identity Prior is first established by injecting subject-discriminative embeddings into the latent space, effectively anchoring the subject's identity against severe degradations. Then, to provide temporally consistent structural guidance for dynamic videos, TIGER constructs a Geometry Prior by lifting 2D reference cues into a disentangled 3D parameter space, creating a geometric anchor through cross-source parameter fusion. Moreover, to achieve maximum efficiency without compromising realism, we harness the video generation model's Generative Prior through a one-step rectified flow. We further design a progressive three-stage training optimization strategy that refines structural fidelity, textural reconstruction, and distribution-level realism to ensure robust optimization. We also construct a large-scale FVR dataset to facilitate robust training and standardized evaluation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that TIGER achieves state-of-the-art performance in both identity fidelity and temporal stability, delivering a high-quality, efficient and identity-consistent FVR. Project page: https://yzhoulv.github.io/Tiger/.

中文摘要翻译: 人脸视频修复（FVR）旨在从退化的输入中恢复高保真的人脸视频，同时保持帧间的身份一致性和语义一致性。现有方法往往难以同时应对三个关键挑战：身份偏移、视角纠缠引导以及感知真实性。为了解决这些问题，我们提出TIGER，一个结构化三先验融合框架，用于驯服身份先验、几何先验和生成先验以实现高质量FVR。具体而言，首先通过将主体判别性嵌入注入潜空间来建立身份先验，有效锚定主体身份以对抗严重退化。然后，为动态视频提供时间一致的结构性引导，TIGER通过将2D参考线索提升到解耦的3D参数空间来构建几何先验，通过跨源参数融合创建几何锚点。此外，为了在不牺牲真实性的前提下实现最大效率，我们通过一步校正流利用视频生成模型的生成先验。我们还设计了渐进式三阶段训练优化策略，以细化结构保真度、纹理重建和分布级真实性，确保鲁棒优化。我们还构建了一个大规模FVR数据集，以促进稳健训练和标准化评估。大量实验表明，TIGER在身份保真度和时间稳定性方面均达到最先进水平，实现了高质量、高效且身份一致的人脸视频修复。项目页面：https://yzhoulv.github.io/Tiger/。

</details>


### [63] [Open-Vocabulary BEV Segmentation with 3D-Aware Geometric Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24353)
*Hojun Choi,Seulbin Hwang,Dae Jung Kim,Kisung Kim,Hyunjung Shim,Jinhan Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OVBEVSeg，一种几何感知的开放词汇BEV分割框架，通过高斯泼溅反投影和三级几何约束，在nuScenes上实现SOTA性能，未见类别mIoU超越闭集方法15.3，推理速度比投影法快2.5倍，显存仅0.22倍。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有BEV感知方法局限于闭集场景，无法应对开放世界中的未知类别；同时将2D VLM语义提升到BEV时存在3D几何不一致性问题。

Method: 基于高效高斯泼溅（GS）反投影的OVBS框架，包含三个渐进阶段：(1) 2D到BEV伪标签生成（可靠3D投影实现开放词汇泛化）；(2) 联合2D-BEV逐场景优化（BEV结构约束保证3D几何一致性）；(3) 3D几何蒸馏（实现在线推理效率）。

Result: 在nuScenes数据集上，OVBEVSeg未见类别mIoU超越闭集方法15.3；无需新类标注即可与使用40%标注的自/半监督基线竞争；推理速度2.5倍于投影法，显存仅0.22倍。

Conclusion: 所提几何感知的OVBEVSeg框架有效解决了开放词汇BEV分割中2D到3D几何不一致的关键挑战，在开放泛化能力和效率上均取得了显著优势。

Original Abstract: Bird's-eye view (BEV) perception fuses multi-camera images into a unified top-down representation for autonomous driving. Despite recent progress, state-of-the-art methods remain confined to closed-set scenarios, making them vulnerable to unpredictable real-world environments. In this work, we introduce open-vocabulary BEV segmentation (OVBS), which leverages vision-language models (VLMs) to recognize categories beyond the training set while maintaining precise BEV perception and real-time efficiency. A key challenge in OVBS lies in the 3D geometric inconsistency inherent in the ill-posed lifting of 2D VLM semantics into BEV. To address this, we propose OVBEVSeg, a geometry-aware OVBS framework that enhances efficient Gaussian splatting (GS)-based unprojection by leveraging robust 3D geometric constraints across three progressive stages: (1) 2D-to-BEV pseudo-labeling via reliable 3D projection for OV generalization; (2) joint 2D-BEV per-scene optimization with BEV structural constraints for 3D geometric consistency; and (3) 3D geometric distillation for online efficiency. On the nuScenes dataset, OVBEVSeg achieves state-of-the-art performance, outperforming closed-set methods by 15.3 mIoU on unseen categories. Remarkably, even with no novel-class ground-truth labels, it remains competitive with self- and semi-supervised baselines trained with up to 40% of ground-truth annotations. Furthermore, it achieves 2.5x faster inference with only 0.22x the memory consumption of projection-based methods. Project page: https://hchoi256.github.io/projects/ovbevseg/.

中文摘要翻译: 鸟瞰图（BEV）感知将多摄像头图像融合为统一的俯视表示用于自动驾驶。尽管近期取得了进展，最先进的方法仍局限于闭集场景，使其在不可预测的现实环境中表现脆弱。本文引入开放词汇BEV分割（OVBS），利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）识别训练集之外的类别，同时保持精确的BEV感知和实时效率。OVBS的一个关键挑战在于将2D VLM语义提升到BEV时固有的3D几何不一致性。为解决此问题，我们提出OVBEVSeg，一种几何感知的OVBS框架，该框架通过利用三个渐进阶段的鲁棒3D几何约束来增强基于高效高斯泼溅（GS）的反投影：(1) 通过可靠3D投影的2D到BEV伪标签生成，实现开放词汇泛化；(2) 联合2D-BEV逐场景优化，配合BEV结构约束保证3D几何一致性；(3) 3D几何蒸馏实现在线效率。在nuScenes数据集上，OVBEVSeg取得了最先进的性能，在未见类别上超越闭集方法15.3 mIoU。值得注意的是，即使没有新类别的真实标注，它与使用高达40%真实标注训练的自监督和半监督基线仍具有竞争力。此外，其推理速度比基于投影的方法快2.5倍，显存消耗仅为后者的0.22倍。项目页面：https://hchoi256.github.io/projects/ovbevseg/。

</details>


### [64] [SignNet-1M: Large-Scale Multilingual Sign Language Video Dataset with Downstream Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24361)
*Zhewen He,Junyi Hu,Haomian Huang,Zhenhua Li,Yu-Shen Liu,Yi Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SignNet-1M，一个大规模增强手语数据集，通过3D高斯溅射（新视角渲染）、扩散模型（场景/身份编辑）和渲染后增强（模拟采集压缩伪影）三种方式合成多样化的真实分布变化，显著提升了手语模型在跨视角、跨背景、跨身份和渲染后偏移下的泛化能力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有手语模型通常在受限条件下采集的数据集上训练，缺乏视角、背景和手语者身份的多样性，导致在真实世界分布变化下鲁棒性差。作者旨在构建一个大规模增强数据集来弥合这一差距。

Method: (i) 通过3D高斯溅射（3DGS）进行新视角渲染（旋转和缩放）；(ii) 利用扩散模型进行背景替换和手语者替换，同时保留手语动作和语言内容；(iii) 渲染后增强，模拟采集和压缩伪影（如姿态/时间扰动和视频级损坏）。数据集覆盖ASL、CSL和DGS三种手语。

Result: 实验表明，使用SignNet-1M训练的模型在跨视角、跨背景、跨身份和渲染后偏移下均能持续提升泛化性能，同时保持强分布内性能。提供了统一基准套件和消融实验，验证了每种增强组件的有效性。

Conclusion: SignNet-1M及其完整的增强流水线和基准测试可有效提升手语模型在真实世界分布变化下的鲁棒性，数据集和代码已开源。

Original Abstract: Sign language models are typically trained on datasets captured under constrained conditions, with limited viewpoint, background, and signer-identity diversity, leading to poor robustness under real-world distribution shifts. We introduce SignNet-1M, a large-scale augmented dataset spanning ASL, CSL, and German Sign Language (DGS). SignNet-1M synthesizes realistic variations along three axes: (i) novel-view rendering (rotation and zoom) via 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), (ii) scene/identity editing via diffusion models for background replacement and signer substitution while preserving sign motion and linguistic content, and (iii) post-rendering augmentations that emulate capture and compression artifacts (e.g., pose/temporal perturbations and video-level corruptions) to better match in-the-wild recordings. Beyond data release, we provide a unified benchmark suite across downstream tasks (e.g., translation and recognition) and ablations that isolate each augmentation component. Experiments across backbones show that training with SignNet-1M consistently improves generalization under cross-view, cross-background, cross-identity, and post-rendering shifts, while maintaining strong in-distribution performance. The dataset, full augmentation pipeline, and benchmark are available at https://signnet.chatsign.ai/.

中文摘要翻译: 手语模型通常在受限条件下采集的数据集上训练，视角、背景和手语者身份多样性有限，导致在真实世界分布变化下鲁棒性差。我们引入了SignNet-1M，一个大规模增强数据集，覆盖美国手语（ASL）、中国手语（CSL）和德国手语（DGS）。SignNet-1M通过三种方式合成逼真的变化：(i) 通过3D高斯溅射（3DGS）进行新视角渲染（旋转和缩放）；(ii) 通过扩散模型进行场景/身份编辑，实现背景替换和手语者替换，同时保留手语动作和语言内容；(iii) 渲染后增强，模拟采集和压缩伪影（例如姿态/时间扰动和视频级损坏），以更好地匹配野外录制视频。除数据发布外，我们还提供了跨下游任务（如翻译和识别）的统一基准套件以及隔离每种增强组件的消融实验。跨多种骨干网络的实验表明，使用SignNet-1M训练的模型在跨视角、跨背景、跨身份和渲染后偏移下均能持续提升泛化性能，同时保持强分布内性能。数据集、完整增强流水线和基准测试可在https://signnet.chatsign.ai/获取。

</details>


### [65] [Structural Kolmogorov-Arnold Convolutions: Learnable Function on the Values or the Filter Shape as Parameter-Efficient Alternative to Per-Edge Convolutional KANs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24371)
*Stefano Mereu,Oleksandr Kuznetsov,Gabriele Marchello,Alessandro Galdelli,Emanuele Frontoni,Adriano Mancini,Ferdinando Cannella*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将KAN中的可学习函数置于卷积的结构层面（作用于像素值或滤波器形状），而非每个边缘，设计了SV-KAN、AG-KAN、RF-KAN三种变体。实验表明，在同等参数量（~0.4M）下，RF-KAN（形状模型）和SV-KAN（值模型）在CIFAR-10/100上超越普通卷积和逐边KAN，其中RF-KAN依赖局部振荡基和内容自适应，而去除形状学习会导致精度暴跌40+点，证明形状是核心贡献者。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有卷积KAN将可学习函数附加到每个卷积核条目上，虽表达能力强但参数量大且易过拟合。本文认为应将可学习函数置于卷积的结构层面而非每条边上，从而在保持性能的同时大幅降低参数量。

Method: 沿单一轴组织设计空间：函数作用于像素值（值模型）还是滤波器形状（形状模型）。提出三种实现：SV-KAN（共享单变量函数作用于值，空间滤波器自由静态）、AG-KAN（共享值函数+内容自适应高斯门控）、RF-KAN（将可学习函数移到滤波器形状上，用局部Morlet小波基展开每个滤波器，带内容自适应振幅）。

Result: 四层协议下，RF-KAN在CIFAR-10达88.47±0.10%，SV-KAN达88.20±0.31%；CIFAR-100上分别为64.40±0.19%和64.57±0.30%，均约0.4M参数。形状模型和最简单值模型表现相当，均优于普通卷积和所有逐边KAN（含官方Gram变体），且参数量约为后者的1/5。消融实验表明去除形状学习精度下降超40点。

Conclusion: 在同等小规模参数量下，将可学习函数置于卷积的结构层面（无论是值模型还是形状模型）比逐边KAN更有效；形状模型（RF-KAN）的增益来自局部振荡小波基和内容自适应，且在该规模下形状学习是精度提升的核心支撑。

Original Abstract: Convolutional Kolmogorov--Arnold Networks (KANs) replace the fixed weights of a convolutional kernel with learnable univariate functions. The dominant formulation attaches one such function to every kernel entry and lets it act on pixel values, expressive but parameter-heavy and prone to overfitting. We argue that the learnable functions are better placed in the \emph{structure} of the convolution than on each edge, and we organise the design space along a single axis: whether the function acts on the pixel \emph{values} or on the filter \emph{shape}. We study three realisations. SV-KAN applies one shared univariate function to the values and leaves the spatial filter free and static, aa classical convolution with a single learnable shared activation. AG-KAN keeps the shared value function but supplies the spatial structure through a content-adaptive Gaussian gate. RF-KAN instead moves the learnable functions onto the filter shape, building each filter from oriented ridge profiles expanded in a localised oscillatory (Morlet) wavelet basis with content-adaptive amplitudes. Under a matched four-layer protocol with in-run references and three seeds, RF-KAN and SV-KAN reach $88.47\pm0.10\%$ and $88.20\pm0.31\%$ on CIFAR-10 and $64.40\pm0.19\%$ and $64.57\pm0.30\%$ on CIFAR-100, at about $0.4$M parameters. At this matched scale the shape model and the simplest value model meet at the top, both above a plain convolution and every per-edge KAN we tested, including the official Gram variant, at roughly a fifth of the parameters. A controlled study attributes the RF-KAN gain to an intrinsically localised oscillatory basis and to content adaptivity, and an ablation that removes the learned shape entirely, leaving only the shared value function, collapses accuracy by over forty points, identifying the learned shape as the load-bearing ingredient at this scale.

中文摘要翻译: 卷积Kolmogorov–Arnold网络（KAN）将卷积核的固定权重替换为可学习的单变量函数。当前主流方法为每个核条目附加一个这样的函数，使其作用于像素值——这种方式表达能力强，但参数量大且易过拟合。我们认为，可学习函数更适合置于卷积的\emph{结构}层面而非每条边上，并沿单一轴组织设计空间：函数作用于像素\emph{值}还是滤波器\emph{形状}。我们研究了三种实现：SV-KAN将共享的单变量函数应用于像素值，空间滤波器保持自由静态，本质上是带有单个可学习共享激活的经典卷积；AG-KAN保留共享值函数，但通过内容自适应高斯门控提供空间结构；RF-KAN则将可学习函数移至滤波器形状上，利用局部振荡（Morlet）小波基展开形成方向脊状轮廓的滤波器，并带内容自适应振幅。在匹配的四层协议下，配合运行内参考和三组随机种子，RF-KAN和SV-KAN在CIFAR-10上分别达到88.47±0.10%和88.20±0.31%，在CIFAR-100上分别达到64.40±0.19%和64.57±0.30%，参数量约0.4M。在此匹配规模下，形状模型与最简单的值模型并列最优，两者均优于普通卷积以及我们测试的所有逐边KAN（包括官方Gram变体），而参数量仅约为后者的五分之一。控制实验将RF-KAN的性能增益归因于内在局部化的振荡基和内容自适应性；消融实验移除整个可学习的形状，仅保留共享值函数后，准确率暴跌超过四十个百分点，表明在该规模下可学习形状是承重性关键成分。

</details>


### [66] [MATCH: Flow Matching for Multi-View Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24375)
*Mathis Kruse,Melissa Schween,Bodo Rosenhahn*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MATCH是首个基于Flow Matching的多视角异常检测方法，通过ODE公式估计似然并计算异常分数，在Real-IAD和MANTA-Tiny数据集上达到SOTA性能，且可在消费级硬件上实时运行。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 工业物体复杂度提高需要从多个视角分析以检测异常，现有多视角异常检测方法性能有限，亟需一种高效、准确且可在消费级硬件上实时运行的多视角异常检测方法。

Method: 基于Flow Matching（FM）的ODE公式进行似然估计，从而推导出异常分数用于多视角图像数据在物体级、图像级和像素级的异常检测；利用FM模型的架构灵活性高效地将不同空间尺寸的特征变换为标准正态分布；通过省略代价高昂的散度项确保实时可用性。

Result: 在Real-IAD数据集上进行了全面评估，并在MANTA-Tiny数据集上首次提供了主流异常检测方法的综合评估；在异常检测和分割任务上均达到SOTA性能；在消费级硬件上运行；消融研究验证了方法选择的有效性。

Conclusion: MATCH方法在工业多视角异常检测中取得了最先进的性能，兼具高精度和实时性，且硬件要求低，具有实际生产场景的应用价值。

Original Abstract: Detecting anomalies in industrial objects is an important topic for increasing production efficiency. More complex objects often require the analysis of several view points, which has led to the field of multi-view anomaly detection. We present MATCH, the first multi-view anomaly detection method based on Flow Matching (FM). With the ODE formulation of Flow Matching, we can estimate likelihoods and thereby derive an anomaly score to detect anomalies in multi-view image data at object, image, and pixel-level. The architectural flexibility of FM models allows us to efficiently transform features of different spatial sizes to the normal distribution. We evaluate thoroughly on the already established Real-IAD data set and are also the first to provide a comprehensive evaluation of popular anomaly detection methods for the MANTA-Tiny data set. MATCH achieves state-of-the-art performance in both anomaly detection and segmentation, all while running on consumer-level hardware. By omitting the costly divergence term needed for likelihood estimation, we ensure that MATCH is usable in real-time production scenarios. Lastly, several ablation studies are conducted to validate the methodological choices.

中文摘要翻译: 检测工业物体中的异常是提高生产效率的重要课题。更复杂的物体通常需要从多个视角进行分析，这催生了多视角异常检测领域。我们提出了MATCH，这是首个基于Flow Matching（FM）的多视角异常检测方法。利用Flow Matching的ODE公式，我们可以估计似然，从而推导出异常分数，用于在多视角图像数据中进行物体级、图像级和像素级的异常检测。FM模型的架构灵活性使我们能够高效地将不同空间尺寸的特征变换为标准正态分布。我们在已建立的Real-IAD数据集上进行了全面评估，并且也是首个在MANTA-Tiny数据集上对主流异常检测方法进行综合评估的工作。MATCH在异常检测和分割任务中均达到了最先进的性能，且可在消费级硬件上运行。通过省略用于似然估计的成本高昂的散度项，我们确保MATCH可在实时生产场景中使用。最后，我们进行了多项消融研究以验证方法论选择的有效性。

</details>


### [67] [MedPCFM: Improving Medical Point Cloud Completion by Integrating Point Transformers and Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24433)
*Kamil Kwarciak,Marek Wodzinski*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PCFM，一种基于PTv3的流匹配方法，用于医疗点云补全，在SkullFix、SkullBreak和下颌骨缺损数据集上实现了最先进的生成性能，同时显著减少了采样步数，并带来高达7倍的吞吐量提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 医疗点云补全对解剖结构重建和下游临床工作流至关重要，但该领域的生成式建模研究尚不充分。

Method: 提出PCFM（Point Cloud Flow Matching），这是一种基于PTv3（Point Transformer v3）的连续时间生成式流匹配方法，用于医疗点云补全。同时构建了强基线：基于PTv3的确定性编码器-解码器补全模型，以及基于PVCNN和PTv3去噪器的扩散补全模型（PCDiff）。

Result: PCFM与确定性PTv3基线性能相当，在所有数据集上实现了最先进的生成性能；与扩散模型相比所需的采样步数大幅减少；在最佳操作点，PCFM相比PVCNN骨干网络带来高达7倍的速度提升；模型规模和点数量的缩放研究显示，更高点分辨率带来一致增益，不同模型规模间存在信息丰富的权衡。

Conclusion: PCFM结合PTv3在医疗点云补全中兼具生成质量与计算效率优势，流匹配方法相比扩散生成在采样效率上具有显著优势，且PTv3相比PVCNN在吞吐量上有大幅提升。

Original Abstract: Medical point cloud completion is important for anatomical reconstruction and downstream clinical workflows, yet generative modeling in this setting remains insufficiently studied. We investigate completion through continuous-time generative modeling and introduce PCFM, a PTv3-backed flow matching approach for medical point cloud completion. We evaluate on SkullFix and SkullBreak, and additionally on the more recent Mandibular Defect dataset. We build strong baselines by adapting PTv3 to a deterministic encoder-decoder completion model and by instantiating diffusion completion (PCDiff) with both PVCNN and PTv3 denoisers. PCFM with PTv3 is competitive with the deterministic PTv3 baseline and achieves state-of-the-art generative performance across datasets, while requiring substantially fewer sampling steps than diffusion. At the best operating points, PTv3 also yields clear throughput gains, providing up to a 7$\times$ speed-up for PCFM compared to a PVCNN backbone. Finally, we study empirical scaling trends by varying model size and point cardinality, showing consistent gains with higher point resolution and informative trade-offs across model scales.

中文摘要翻译: 医疗点云补全对于解剖结构重建和下游临床工作流程至关重要，但该场景下的生成式建模研究仍不充分。我们通过连续时间生成式建模研究补全问题，并引入PCFM——一种基于PTv3的流匹配方法，用于医疗点云补全。我们在SkullFix和SkullBreak数据集，以及更新的下颌骨缺损数据集上进行了评估。通过将PTv3适配为确定性编码器-解码器补全模型，并分别使用PVCNN和PTv3去噪器实例化扩散补全模型（PCDiff），我们构建了强基线。基于PTv3的PCFM与确定性PTv3基线性能相当，并在所有数据集上实现了最先进的生成性能，同时所需的采样步数远少于扩散模型。在最佳操作点，PTv3还带来了显著的吞吐量提升，与PVCNN骨干网络相比，PCFM的速度提升高达7倍。最后，我们通过改变模型规模和点数量研究了经验性缩放趋势，结果表明更高点分辨率带来一致的性能增益，且不同模型规模间存在信息丰富的权衡。

</details>


### [68] [Modality-Aware Out-of-Distribution Detection for Multi-Modal Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24404)
*Lars Doorenbos,Duc Manh Vu,Serdar Ozsoy,Juergen Gall*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 为解决多模态动作识别中的跨模态分布外（OOD）检测问题，提出一种后验混合检测器，利用多模态与单模态预测之间的关系以及特征空间得分，在推理时显式考虑多模态信息，显著提升OOD检测性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有方法在训练时虽考虑OOD检测进行多模态正则化，但推理时仍使用适用于单模态的现成OOD检测器，丢弃了多模态间的重要信息。需要设计一种专门针对多模态场景、能在推理时充分利用多模态信息的OOD检测方法。

Method: 1) 发现多模态与单模态预测之间存在有趣关系，利用该信号构建后验检测器；2) 结合特征空间评分（检测多模态空间中的离流形样本）与多模态logits归一化；3) 提出混合检测器，兼容现有的训练时方法。

Result: 在MultiOOD基准的多个数据集上，所提方法平均性能优于现有最先进方法，表明在推理时显式考虑不同模态对多模态OOD检测至关重要。

Conclusion: 推理时显式考虑多模态信息对多模态OOD检测非常重要，所提混合检测器能有效提升性能且兼容现有训练方法。

Original Abstract: The incorporation of additional modalities into action recognition models increases their performance across a wide range of settings. However, how this additional information can contribute to making the models more robust remains underexplored, particularly for the case of multi-modal out-of-distribution (OOD) detection. While methods exist that regularize the multi-modal training process with OOD detection in mind, they still apply off-the-shelf OOD detectors designed for the uni-modal case during inference, discarding important information. Based on an interesting relationship we find between the multi-modal and uni-modal predictions, we propose to use this signal to build a post-hoc detector explicitly designed for the multi-modal scenario. We combine this new source of information with a feature-space score, which detects off-manifold samples in the multi-modal space, and normalize them by the multi-modal logits. In doing so, the proposed hybrid detector is compatible with existing training-time approaches and consistently improves performance. Experiments on a wide range of established datasets from the MultiOOD benchmark show that, on average, our approach outperforms the state of the art. Our results show the importance of explicitly considering the different modalities at inference time for multi-modal OOD detection.

中文摘要翻译: 将额外的模态信息融入动作识别模型可在广泛设置下提升其性能。然而，这些额外信息如何有助于增强模型的鲁棒性仍未被充分探索，尤其是在多模态分布外（OOD）检测场景中。虽然已有方法在训练过程中针对OOD检测对多模态训练进行正则化，但在推理阶段它们仍直接使用为单模态场景设计的现成OOD检测器，从而丢弃了重要信息。基于我们发现的多模态与单模态预测之间的有趣关系，本文提出利用这一信号构建一个专门针对多模态场景的后验检测器。我们将这一新信息源与特征空间评分（用于检测多模态空间中的离流形样本）相结合，并通过多模态logits进行归一化处理。由此提出的混合检测器与现有的训练时方法兼容，并能持续提升性能。在MultiOOD基准中多个已建立数据集上的实验表明，我们的方法平均性能优于现有最先进方法。实验结果凸显了在多模态OOD检测的推理阶段显式考虑不同模态信息的重要性。

</details>


### [69] [EERLoss: A Novel Loss Function for Training Deep Biometric Models. A Case Study in Keystroke Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24586)
*Nahuel Gonzalez,Marta Robledo-Moreno,Ivan DeAndres-Tame,Ruben Vera-Rodriguez,Ruben Tolosana*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EERLoss，一种可微分的EER近似损失函数，用于直接优化生物特征验证任务的等错误率，在击键动态验证基准上实现约30%的相对EER降低。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有深度学习生物特征验证方法通常优化间接目标函数，导致优化过程与主要评估指标（等错误率EER）之间不一致，需要一种直接对齐训练目标与评估指标的损失函数。

Method: 提出EERLoss，一种对EER的次可微、任意精度近似损失函数，可直接用于训练深度生物特征模型；该框架还可扩展到优化DET曲线上的任意特定工作点。

Result: 在包含超过18.5万被试的大规模KVC-onGoing击键动态验证基准上，消融实验证明EERLoss优于现有最先进损失函数，收敛速度更快；重新训练KVC获奖架构后，相对EER降低约30%。

Conclusion: EERLoss作为一种任务对齐的训练目标，在高方差生物特征（如击键动态）上显著优于现有方法，验证了其有效性。

Original Abstract: Deep learning approaches to biometric verification are commonly trained by optimizing indirect objectives, creating a misalignment between the optimization process and the primary evaluation metric, typically the Equal Error Rate (EER). This paper introduces EERLoss: a subdifferentiable, arbitrarily accurate approximation to EER for training deep biometric models. Furthermore, this framework has the potential to be adapted to optimize any specific operating point on the DET curve, enhancing its generalizability. To validate this approach, EERLoss is evaluated on a particularly demanding behavioral biometric modality: keystroke dynamics verification. This task is characterized by its high intra-class and low inter-class variability. Experiments are conducted on the large-scale KVC-onGoing benchmark, incorporating data from over 185,000 subjects across different scenarios. A comprehensive ablation study initially demonstrates the superiority of EERLoss in comparison to existing state-of-the-art loss functions. It also converges substantially faster compared to other losses, reducing the overall training cost. Additionally, a comparison is made between the proposed loss and the KVC-winning architecture by re-training it with EERLoss, demonstrating that the proposed approach significantly outperforms the original SoTA, achieving a relative EER reduction of up to approx. 30\%. This improvement on a challenging, large-scale benchmark validates the effectiveness of EERLoss as a task-aligned training objective specifically suited for high-variance biometric traits.

中文摘要翻译: 深度学习方法在生物特征验证中通常通过优化间接目标进行训练，这导致了优化过程与主要评估指标（通常是等错误率EER）之间的不一致。本文提出EERLoss：一种用于训练深度生物特征模型的、对EER的次可微且任意精度近似函数。此外，该框架具有可扩展性，能够优化DET曲线上的任意特定工作点，从而增强其泛化能力。为验证该方法，我们在一个极具挑战性的行为生物特征模态——击键动态验证上评估EERLoss。该任务的特点是高类内变异性和低类间变异性。实验在大规模KVC-onGoing基准上进行，包含来自超过185,000名被试的数据，涵盖多种场景。综合消融研究初步证明了EERLoss相对于现有最先进损失函数的优越性。同时，相比于其他损失函数，EERLoss收敛速度显著更快，从而降低了总体训练成本。此外，通过使用EERLoss重新训练KVC获奖架构，我们将所提出的损失函数与KVC获奖架构进行了对比，结果表明所提方法显著优于原始最先进方法，实现了高达约30%的相对EER降低。这一在挑战性大规模基准上的改进验证了EERLoss作为一种任务对齐的训练目标，特别适用于高方差生物特征 traits 的有效性。

</details>


### [70] [EgoSAT: A Comprehensive Benchmark of Egocentric Streaming Interaction Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24422)
*Yijia Lei,Jinzhao Li,Yichi Zhang,Jiacheng Hua,Yin Li,Miao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoSAT是一个首个面向流式设置下第一人称视频推理的综合基准，包含1,997个视频、约4,800个问答对，统一了回顾、在线和前瞻三种推理任务。评估发现现有VLM在时态推理上表现不佳，且存在严重的置信度失校准问题，即模型会“自信地犯错”。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频推理基准多基于完整视频进行离线评估，无法反映流式场景中模型必须逐帧处理、实时理解动态视觉上下文的真实需求。缺乏一个统一基准来系统评估VLM在流式交互场景下对过去、现在和未来事件的推理能力。

Method: 构建EgoSAT基准，包含1,997个第一人称视频（共165小时）和约4,800个问答对。将问题分为三类：回顾推理（已完成事件）、在线理解（正在发生事件）和前瞻预测（未来动作）。评估时模拟流式场景，模型只能访问已观测到的帧序列，并区分答案性，进行置信度诊断分析。

Result: 现有开源和闭源VLM在流式推理中均表现不佳，尤其在回顾和前瞻任务上困难突出；模型存在严重的置信度失校准问题——置信度无法反映真实的答案性，导致大量“自信地犯错”行为。

Conclusion: 当前VLM在流式第一人称视频推理中能力不足，且置信度校准严重缺失，亟需改进模型在流式场景下的时态建模与不确定性量化能力。

Original Abstract: We introduce EgoSAT, the first comprehensive benchmark for egocentric video reasoning in streaming settings, designed to evaluate the capabilities of modern vision-language models (VLMs). The benchmark targets streaming interaction understanding, where video frames arrive sequentially and models must continuously interpret evolving visual context. EgoSAT unifies several previously distinct tasks within a single streaming framework. In this formulation, queries about completed events correspond to retrospective reasoning, queries about ongoing activities require online understanding, and queries about future actions involve prospective anticipation. This unified setting requires models to reason about the past, present, and future while operating under the constraint that only previously observed frames are available. EgoSAT contains 1,997 unique videos spanning 165 hours of egocentric footage and around 4,800 high-quality question-answer pairs, carefully designed to probe reasoning across varying temporal contexts. Using this benchmark, we evaluate a diverse set of both open-weight and closed-weight VLMs, providing a systematic assessment of their ability for streaming interaction understanding. By distinguishing answerability and conducting diagnostics on confidence of models, we find existing models not only struggle with prospective and retrospective modeling, but also exhibit severe mis-calibration: confidence often fails to track inherent answerability, leading to dangerous "confidently wrong" behaviors. Project page: https://leiyj23.github.io/EgoSAT/

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了EgoSAT，这是首个面向流式设置下第一人称视频推理的综合基准，旨在评估现代视觉语言模型（VLM）的能力。该基准聚焦于流式交互理解，即视频帧按顺序到达，模型必须持续解读不断演变的视觉上下文。EgoSAT将多个先前分散的任务统一到一个流式框架中。在此框架下，关于已发生事件的查询对应回顾推理，关于正在进行的活动的查询需要在线理解，而关于未来动作的查询则涉及前瞻预测。这种统一的设置要求模型在对过去、现在和未来进行推理的同时，受限于仅能访问先前观测到的帧。EgoSAT包含1,997个独特视频，总计165小时的第一人称 footage，以及约4,800个高质量问答对，精心设计以探测不同时间上下文下的推理能力。利用该基准，我们评估了一系列开源和闭源VLM，系统性地评测了它们在流式交互理解方面的能力。通过区分答案性并对模型置信度进行诊断分析，我们发现现有模型不仅在回顾和前瞻建模上存在困难，还表现出严重的失校准问题：置信度往往无法追踪固有的答案性，导致危险的“自信地犯错”行为。项目页面：https://leiyj23.github.io/EgoSAT/

</details>


### [71] [Transformation Behavior of Images in Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24430)
*Christian Zöllner,Mozzam Motiwala,Aysel Ahadova,Gerrit Anders,Robert Hüneburg,Jacob Nattermann,Matthias Kloor*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 研究经典图像变换对组织病理学编码器网络潜在空间的影响，发现嵌入对变换具有鲁棒性但非完全不变，且通用与专用编码器存在显著差异。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 组织病理学分类任务中，编码器网络需要对下游任务适应，编码生物/诊断内容的同时对标签无关的变换保持不变性。本文旨在探究经典图像变换对潜在空间的具体影响，以及不同编码器网络对此的鲁棒性差异。

Method: 使用Lunit Inc.、Bioptimus（病理图像专用）和Meta Research Team（通用）提供的编码器网络，对染色病理图像切片应用标准数据变换，通过比较原始与变换后图像嵌入的方差，并与随机无关嵌入进行对比分析。

Result: 原始与变换后图像的嵌入比随机嵌入更接近，表明对变换具有鲁棒性；但变换效果未被完全中和，嵌入并非完全不变，解释了变换增强可提升性能的原因；通用与病理专用编码器之间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: 编码器网络对图像变换具有一定鲁棒性但未达到完全不变性，变换增强的效用源于潜在空间中未被完全消除的变换效应；不同类型的编码器网络在变换鲁棒性上存在显著差异。

Original Abstract: Training of neural networks for histopathology classification tasks typically relies on data encoding into latent space, which reduces complexity and improves performance. There are several encoder networks available, either pretrained on general image datasets such as ImageNET, or specifically on histopathological images. Training of encoder networks should be adapted to downstream tasks, allowing encoding of biologic/diagnostic content while rendering networks invariant to label-irrelevant transformations.
  This paper investigates the effect of classical image transformation on the latent space, using networks provided by Lunit Inc. and Bioptimus, both focusing on pathological images, and by Meta Research Team. We assess variance of embeddings resulting from standard data transformations by comparing original and transformed image embeddings and by contrasting them with random, unrelated embeddings, using image tiles from hematoxylin/eosin-stained sections available in a colorectal tissue dataset and the publicly accessible TCGA dataset.
  Our findings show that embeddings of original and transformed images are closer to each other than to random embeddings, indicating robustness to transformations. However, they are not fully invariant, revealing that the encoder networks do not completely neutralize transformation effects in latent space, explaining why transformation-mediated augmentation of datasets can improve performance. Significant differences were observed between general and histopathology-specific encoder networks.

中文摘要翻译: 组织病理学分类任务的神经网络训练通常依赖于将数据编码到潜在空间，这降低了复杂度并提升了性能。存在多种编码器网络，既包括在ImageNET等通用图像数据集上预训练的，也包括专门在组织病理学图像上预训练的。编码器网络的训练应适应下游任务，使其能够编码生物学/诊断内容，同时对标签无关的变换保持不变性。
本文研究了经典图像变换对潜在空间的影响，使用了Lunit Inc.、Bioptimus（均专注于病理图像）以及Meta Research Team提供的网络。我们通过比较原始图像嵌入与变换后图像嵌入，并将其与随机的无关嵌入进行对比，评估了标准数据变换所导致嵌入的方差，使用了结直肠组织数据集和公开TCGA数据集中苏木精/伊红染色切片的图像块。
我们的发现表明，原始图像与变换后图像的嵌入彼此之间比与随机嵌入更接近，表明其对变换具有鲁棒性。然而，它们并非完全不变，揭示了编码器网络并未完全消除潜在空间中的变换效应，这解释了为何基于变换的数据集增强可以提升性能。通用编码器网络与组织病理学专用编码器网络之间观察到了显著差异。

</details>


### [72] [S1-Omni-Image: A Unified Model for Scientific Image Understanding, Generation, and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24441)
*Qingxiao Li,Zikai Wang,Qingli Wang,Nan Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: S1-Omni-Image是一个面向科学图像理解、生成和编辑的统一多模态模型，采用“先思考后生成”范式，在科学推理基座S1-VL-32B上耦合生成模块，在多个科学图像基准上取得领先性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 通用图像生成模型难以满足科学图像任务对科学语义理解、结构关系、领域知识和任务意图的严格要求，因此需要构建专门面向科学图像的统一理解、生成与编辑模型。

Method: 基于科学多模态推理基座S1-VL-32B，采用“先思考后生成”统一范式：模型首先生成面向任务的推理轨迹、文本答案和任务特殊标记，将其隐藏状态注入图像生成模块以条件化图像生成或编辑，支持科学图像理解、生成和编辑的统一框架。

Result: 在GenExam和TechImage-Bench上超越开源模型；在MSD、cigRockSEM、SynthRAD2025和IXI四个编辑基准上达到最优；同时保持科学图像理解性能的稳定性。

Conclusion: S1-Omni-Image在显著提升科学图像生成与编辑能力的同时，保留了继承自S1-VL-32B的科学图像理解能力，验证了“先思考后生成”范式在科学多模态任务中的有效性。

Original Abstract: We present S1-Omni-Image, an open-weight unified multimodal model for scientific image understanding, generation, and editing. Unlike general-purpose image generation models, scientific image tasks require not only high-fidelity synthesis, but also robust understanding of scientific semantics, structural relations, domain knowledge, and task intent. To this end, S1-Omni-Image builds on the scientific multimodal reasoning backbone S1-VL-32B and couples its understanding capability with an image generation module under a unified think-before-generate paradigm. Given a user instruction, the model first produces a task-oriented reasoning trace, a textual answer, and a task special token; their hidden states are then injected into the generation module to condition image generation or editing. S1-Omni-Image supports scientific image understanding, generation, and editing in a unified framework. For generation, it focuses on scientific illustrations and text rendering, including logical diagrams, relational comparisons, data charts, and realistic scientific visualizations. For editing, it casts segmentation and other domain-specific vision tasks as native image editing problems, enabling multi-turn illustration editing, medical and geographic image segmentation, medical image translation, and scientific image super-resolution. We construct SciGenEdit, a 314K-sample training dataset, and release the model weights, inference code, and SciGenEdit-10K. Experiments show that S1-Omni-Image substantially improves scientific image generation and editing while preserving the scientific image understanding capability inherited from S1-VL-32B. It outperforms open-source models on GenExam and TechImage-Bench, achieves state-of-the-art results on four editing benchmarks including MSD, cigRockSEM, SynthRAD2025, and IXI, and maintains stable performance on scientific image understanding evaluations.

中文摘要翻译: 我们提出了S1-Omni-Image，一个面向科学图像理解、生成与编辑的开放权重统一多模态模型。与通用图像生成模型不同，科学图像任务不仅需要高保真合成，还需要对科学语义、结构关系、领域知识和任务意图具有稳健的理解。为此，S1-Omni-Image构建于科学多模态推理基座S1-VL-32B之上，采用统一的“先思考后生成”范式，将其理解能力与图像生成模块相耦合。给定用户指令，模型首先生成面向任务的推理轨迹、文本答案和任务特殊标记；这些隐藏状态随后被注入生成模块，以条件化图像生成或编辑。S1-Omni-Image在统一框架中支持科学图像的理解、生成和编辑。在生成方面，模型聚焦于科学插图与文本渲染，包括逻辑图表、关系对比、数据图表以及逼真的科学可视化。在编辑方面，模型将分割及其他领域特定的视觉任务转化为原生图像编辑问题，支持多轮插图编辑、医学和地理图像分割、医学图像翻译以及科学图像超分辨率。我们构建了包含31.4万样本的训练数据集SciGenEdit，并发布了模型权重、推理代码以及SciGenEdit-10K。实验表明，S1-Omni-Image在显著提升科学图像生成与编辑能力的同时，保留了继承自S1-VL-32B的科学图像理解能力。该模型在GenExam和TechImage-Bench上超越了开源模型，在MSD、cigRockSEM、SynthRAD2025和IXI四个编辑基准上取得了最优结果，并在科学图像理解评估中保持了稳定的性能。

</details>


### [73] [P-MTP: Efficient Document Parsing via Multi-Token Prediction with Progressive Depth Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24447)
*Le Xiang,Chenxi Zhai,Shu Wei,Jingjing Wu,Qunyi Xie,Xiao Tan,Kunbin Chen,Wei He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: P-MTP提出渐进式多令牌预测框架，通过渐进课程损失和置信门控动态草稿机制，在文档解析领域实现高达5倍推理加速，且精度损失可忽略不计。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视觉-语言模型在文档解析中推理延迟严重，尤其对于令牌密集的文档；多令牌预测虽有望加速推理，但扩展至更深前瞻深度时存在优化不稳定性问题。

Method: 1) 渐进式课程损失（Progressive Curriculum Loss）：利用累积路径可靠性和回顾目标一致性自适应重加权不同前瞻深度，抑制长程预测中的梯度噪声，实现从易到难的优化过渡；2) 置信门控动态草稿（Confidence-Gated Dynamic Drafting）：在推理阶段自适应校准推测长度，最大化有效前瞻深度和接受率，减少计算浪费。

Result: 在多个基准和架构上，P-MTP实现高达5倍加速，且精度损失可忽略不计，首次在文档解析领域成功验证了深度前瞻多令牌预测的有效性。

Conclusion: P-MTP通过渐进式多令牌预测框架有效解决了深度前瞻MTP的优化不稳定问题，显著提升了文档解析的推理速度，为高吞吐文档解析提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have revolutionized document parsing by enabling end-to-end mapping from images to structured text, imposing a significant latency bottleneck, particularly for token-dense documents. While Multi-Token Prediction (MTP) has emerged as a promising approach for accelerating inference, its potential is constrained by optimization instability when scaling to deeper look-ahead depth. In this paper, we propose \textbf{P-MTP}, a framework that leverages \textbf{Progressive Multi-Token Prediction} with a lightweight MTP module to scale the look-ahead depth for high-throughput document parsing. Specifically, we introduce Progressive Curriculum Loss that adaptively re-weights different look-ahead depths using cumulative path reliability and retrospective target consistency. By effectively suppressing gradient noise in long-range predictions, P-MTP, facilitates an automated easy-to-hard optimization transition, enabling the model to master increasingly distant look-ahead depths. Furthermore, we propose Confidence-Gated Dynamic Drafting to maximize the effective look-ahead depth and acceptance rate by adaptively calibrating speculative length during inference, thereby minimizing computational waste and further pushing the boundaries of inference speedup. Experimental results across multiple benchmarks and architectures demonstrate that P-MTP, achieves up to a $5\times$ speedup with negligible loss in accuracy, providing the first successful validation of extensive look-ahead MTP in the document parsing domain.

中文摘要翻译: 视觉-语言模型通过实现从图像到结构化文本的端到端映射，彻底革新了文档解析技术，但对于令牌密集的文档，这一过程带来了显著的延迟瓶颈。尽管多令牌预测已成为加速推理的一种有前景的方法，但其在扩展至更深的前瞻深度时受限于优化不稳定性。本文提出P-MTP框架，该框架利用渐进式多令牌预测和轻量级MTP模块，扩展前瞻深度以实现高吞吐文档解析。具体而言，我们引入渐进式课程损失，通过累积路径可靠性和回顾目标一致性自适应地重新加权不同的前瞻深度。通过有效抑制长程预测中的梯度噪声，P-MTP促进了自动化的由易到难优化过渡，使模型能够掌握日益增加的前瞻深度。此外，我们提出置信门控动态草稿机制，通过在推理过程中自适应校准推测长度来最大化有效前瞻深度和接受率，从而减少计算浪费并进一步推动推理加速的边界。跨多个基准和架构的实验结果表明，P-MTP实现了高达5倍的加速，且精度损失可忽略不计，首次在文档解析领域成功验证了深度前瞻多令牌预测的有效性。

</details>


### [74] [SENTRY: SAM2-Enhanced Neighbor-Aware and Temporally Reasoned Memory for Visual Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24449)
*Mohamad Alansari,Yonathan Michael,Hasan AlMarzouqi,Muzammal Naseer,Naoufel Werghi,Sajid Javed*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SENTRY是一个无需训练、即插即用的“先验证再写入”模块，通过短时时间一致性校验替代置信度驱动的内存更新，有效缓解SAM2跟踪器在遮挡、快速运动和干扰下的漂移问题，在多个基准上达到零样本SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的SAM2视觉跟踪器在内存更新机制中仅依赖置信度选择掩码，导致在遮挡、快速运动和干扰物场景下容易发生漂移（drift），亟需一种不改变基础架构且无需重新训练的方法来提升跟踪的时域一致性。

Method: 提出了SENTRY——一个训练无关、即插即用的“先验证再写入”（refine-before-write）模块。它对每一帧生成多样化的分割假设，回溯为短轨迹片段（short tracklets），并通过邻域感知的循环一致性匹配（neighbor-aware cycle-consistent matching）与近期轨迹进行比对，只将满足时域和几何一致性的掩码写入内存，替代原有的纯置信度驱动写入策略。

Result: 集成到五个强基线跟踪器中，SENTRY在9个基准上取得一致性能提升，在LaSOT、LaSOT_ext、GOT-10k、VOT20、VOT22和DiDi上达到新的零样本SOTA。SAM2-L版本在A100上运行速度达32.8 FPS，额外显存开销仅0.4–0.6 GB。

Conclusion: 在写入内存时强制执行时域有效性验证能够稳定增强记忆的跟踪性能，且无需重新训练。同时首次提供了SAM2跟踪器在所有尺度上的统一评估。

Original Abstract: We revisit the memory update mechanism in SAM2-based visual object tracking and identify confidence-only mask selection as the dominant cause of drift under occlusion, rapid motion, and distractors. We introduce SENTRY, a training-free, plug-and-play, refine-before-write module that validates each memory update for short-horizon temporal consistency before committing it. SENTRY aggregates diverse segmentation hypotheses per frame, backtracks them into short tracklets, and uses neighbor-aware cycle-consistent matching against recent trajectories to favor temporally and geometrically consistent masks. It leaves the base architecture untouched, replacing confidence-driven writes with consistency-validated ones. For fair evaluation, we re-evaluate major open-source SAM2-based trackers across all available scales and datasets, filling gaps in prior reports. Integrated into five strong baselines, SENTRY delivers consistent gains across nine benchmarks, achieving new zero-shot SOTA on LaSOT, LaSOT_ext, GOT-10k, VOT20, VOT22, and DiDi. Despite these checks, the SAM2-L version runs at 32.8 FPS on an A100, and across compatible hosts adds only about 0.4--0.6 GB VRAM. Our results provide the first unified all-scale evaluation of SAM2-based trackers and show that enforcing temporal validity at write time stabilizes memory-augmented tracking without retraining.

中文摘要翻译: 我们重新审视了基于SAM2的视觉目标跟踪中的内存更新机制，并指出仅依赖置信度的掩码选择是导致遮挡、快速运动和干扰物场景下跟踪漂移的主要原因。我们提出了SENTRY——一个无需训练、即插即用、先验证后写入的模块，它在每次内存更新之前先校验短时域的时间一致性。SENTRY对每一帧聚合多样化的分割假设，将它们回溯为短轨迹片段，并利用邻域感知的循环一致性匹配与近期轨迹进行比对，从而优先选择时域和几何上一致的掩码。该方法不改变基础架构，仅用一致性验证的写入替代置信度驱动的写入。为了公平评估，我们在所有可用尺度和数据集上重新评估了主要的开源SAM2跟踪器，填补了以往报告的空白。集成到五个强基线中后，SENTRY在九个基准上均取得了一致的性能提升，并在LaSOT、LaSOT_ext、GOT-10k、VOT20、VOT22和DiDi上实现了新的零样本SOTA。尽管增加了这些校验步骤，SAM2-L版本在A100上仍能达到32.8 FPS，在兼容主机上仅增加约0.4–0.6 GB的显存。我们的结果首次提供了对SAM2跟踪器的全尺度统一评估，并表明在写入时强制执行时域有效性能够稳定增强记忆的跟踪性能，而无需重新训练。

</details>


### [75] [Lite Any Stereo V2: Faster and Stronger Efficient Zero-Shot Stereo Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24457)
*Junpeng Jing,Ronglai Zuo,Zhelun Shen,Shangchen Zhou,Rolandos Alexandros Potamias,Stefanos Zafeiriou,Krystian Mikolajczyk,Jiankang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Lite Any Stereo V2 (LAS2)，一种超快速的立体匹配模型系列，通过2D-only代价聚合架构和三阶段训练策略（合成监督、自蒸馏、真实知识蒸馏），在保持极低推理延迟的同时实现了高效的零样本泛化性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有立体匹配方法或依赖大模型与高计算量难以部署在资源受限平台，或为轻量模型但零样本泛化能力弱。本文旨在打破这一权衡，实现兼具快速推理与强零样本泛化的高效立体匹配模型。

Method: 架构上，提出仅2D的代价聚合框架，针对实际推理延迟而非理论MACs进行优化；训练上，设计三阶段策略：合成数据监督预训练、自蒸馏、真实世界知识蒸馏，并引入伪标签过滤与错误钳制操作以实现平滑的合成到真实域迁移。LAS2包含前馈变体（适配不同效率预算）和迭代变体（追求更高精度）。

Result: LAS2在高效立体匹配方法中达到SOTA精度，同时保持显著更低的延迟。LAS2-H在零样本性能上超越迭代方法Fast-FoundationStereo，在H200和Orin上推理速度分别快1.8倍和2.7倍。

Conclusion: 本文证明了轻量模型在精心设计下同样能实现强大的零样本立体匹配性能，为资源受限平台上的高精度、低延迟立体匹配提供了可行方案。

Original Abstract: Recent advances in stereo matching have achieved remarkable accuracy, but often rely on large models, heavy computation, or additional foundation-model priors, making them difficult to deploy on resource-constrained platforms. In contrast, efficient stereo models offer faster inference but are commonly considered less capable of strong zero-shot generalization. In this paper, we challenge this assumption by introducing Lite Any Stereo V2 (LAS2), an ultra-fast model series designed for efficient zero-shot stereo matching. LAS2 is developed from both architecture and training perspectives. Architecturally, we revisit efficient stereo design under practical deployment settings and propose a 2D-only cost aggregation framework, optimized for real inference latency rather than theoretical MACs alone. For training, we develop a three-stage strategy that combines synthetic supervision, self-distillation, and real-world knowledge distillation. To improve the reliability of real-world pseudo supervision, we further introduce pseudo-label filtering and an error-clamping operation, enabling smoother synthetic-to-real transfer. We instantiate LAS2 as a family of models, including feed-forward variants for different efficiency budgets and an iterative variant for higher accuracy. Extensive experiments show that LAS2 achieves state-of-the-art accuracy among efficient stereo methods while maintaining significantly lower latency. Specifically, LAS2-H achieves stronger overall zero-shot performance than the iterative method Fast-FoundationStereo, with 1.8x and 2.7x faster inference on H200 and Orin, respectively. The project page, demos, and code are available at https://tomtomtommi.github.io/LiteAnyStereoV2/.

中文摘要翻译: 近期立体匹配领域的进展取得了显著的准确性，但通常依赖于大模型、高计算量或额外的基座模型先验，使其难以部署在资源受限平台上。相比之下，高效的立体模型推理速度更快，但通常被认为零样本泛化能力较弱。在本文中，我们通过引入Lite Any Stereo V2 (LAS2) 来挑战这一假设，这是一个专为高效零样本立体匹配设计的超快速模型系列。LAS2从架构和训练两个角度进行开发。在架构上，我们重新审视了实际部署场景下的高效立体设计，提出了一个仅含2D操作的代价聚合框架，该框架针对实际推理延迟而非理论MACs进行优化。在训练方面，我们开发了一种三阶段策略，结合了合成监督、自蒸馏和真实世界知识蒸馏。为了提高真实世界伪监督的可靠性，我们进一步引入了伪标签过滤和误差钳制操作，实现了更平滑的合成到真实域迁移。我们将LAS2实例化为一个模型家族，包括适配不同效率预算的前馈变体以及追求更高精度的迭代变体。大量实验表明，LAS2在高效立体方法中达到了最先进的精度，同时保持了显著更低的延迟。具体而言，LAS2-H在零样本整体性能上优于迭代方法Fast-FoundationStereo，在H200和Orin上的推理速度分别快1.8倍和2.7倍。项目页面、演示和代码可在 https://tomtomtommi.github.io/LiteAnyStereoV2/ 获取。

</details>


### [76] [Boosting Text-Driven Video Segmentation via Geometry-Aware Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24464)
*Tianyu Zhu,Yingping Liang,Hesong Li,Ying Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出GeoLaV两阶段框架，通过单目几何预训练和几何感知蒸馏，将3D几何知识从图像迁移到视频，提升文本驱动的视频目标分割中的时空一致性与语言对齐能力，在多个基准上达到SOTA。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有文本驱动视频目标分割（RVOS）模型仅在2D图像或视频数据上用朴素分割损失训练，忽略了帧间的几何一致性，导致空间理解能力弱。

Method: 两阶段框架：第一阶段通过单目新视角合成进行单目几何预训练，在大规模单图像数据集上通过空间对齐学习几何一致的视觉表示；第二阶段引入几何感知蒸馏，利用通用3D先验模型迁移3D结构知识，在视频分割数据集上微调模型，增强3D感知能力以及时空一致性和语言对齐。

Result: 仅使用图像分割数据即可在RVOS上实现显著的零样本泛化；结合几何感知蒸馏在视频上微调后，在多个RVOS基准上达到最先进性能。

Conclusion: 通过将图像中的3D几何知识蒸馏到视频分割模型中，能有效提升文本驱动视频目标分割的时空一致性与语言理解能力，且仅用图像数据便具备零样本泛化能力。

Original Abstract: Text-driven Referring Video Object Segmentation (RVOS) aims to locate and segment target objects in videos given natural language. However, existing models are typically trained on 2D image or video datasets with naive segmentation losses, which overlooks the geometric consistency across frames and leads to weak spatial understanding. In this paper, we propose Geometry-enhanced Language-guided Video segmentation (GeoLaV), a two-stage framework that distills 3D geometric knowledge from images to enhance text-driven video segmentation. In the first stage, we perform monocular geometry pretraining with monocular novel-view synthesis, enabling the model to acquire geometry-consistent visual representations via spatial alignment on large-scale single-image datasets. In the second stage, we introduce geometry-aware distillation and fine-tune the model on video segmentation datasets, transferring 3D structural knowledge from a general 3D prior model. This process reinforces 3D awareness and improves both spatiotemporal coherence and language grounding in segmentation. Extensive experiments show that our method using only image segmentation data already provides notable zero-shot generalization in RVOS. When combined with geometry-aware distillation for fine-tuning on videos, our method achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple RVOS benchmarks. The code is available at https://github.com/Tony1882880/GeoLaV.

中文摘要翻译: 文本驱动的指代视频目标分割（Referring Video Object Segmentation, RVOS）旨在根据自然语言描述定位并分割视频中的目标对象。然而，现有模型通常在2D图像或视频数据集上使用朴素的分割损失进行训练，忽略了帧间的几何一致性，导致空间理解能力较弱。本文提出几何增强的语言引导视频分割（GeoLaV），一种两阶段框架，从图像中蒸馏3D几何知识以增强文本驱动视频分割。第一阶段，我们通过单目新视角合成进行单目几何预训练，使模型能够在大规模单图像数据集上通过空间对齐获得几何一致的视觉表示。第二阶段，我们引入几何感知蒸馏，在视频分割数据集上微调模型，将通用3D先验模型的3D结构知识迁移至分割模型。该过程增强了3D感知能力，并提升了分割的时空一致性与语言对齐能力。大量实验表明，我们的方法仅使用图像分割数据即可在RVOS上实现显著的零样本泛化。当结合几何感知蒸馏在视频上进行微调后，我们的方法在多个RVOS基准上取得了最先进的性能。代码已开源：https://github.com/Tony1882880/GeoLaV。

</details>


### [77] [video-SALMONN-R$^3$: Learning to ReWatch, ReAsk, and ReAnswer for Efficient Video Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24477)
*Yixuan Li,Guangzhi Sun,Yudong Yang,Wei Li,Zejun MA,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出video-SALMONN-R³，首个通过强化学习实现端到端“重新观看”（re-watch）机制的视频大语言模型，无需链式思维冷启动，通过重回答和重提问策略提升视频问答性能，同时显著降低计算成本。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有视频大语言模型受限于计算和内存预算，往往使用降低的帧率和空间分辨率，导致丢失关键信息。两阶段范式（先粗理解定位相关片段，再高保真重看）虽有效但依赖链式思维冷启动，需要昂贵的CoT数据标注，且CoT监督微调会损害预训练的视频理解能力。

Method: 1）采用强化学习实现端到端的re-watch机制，无需CoT冷启动；2）提出重回答（re-answer）策略：模型先在第一轮观看中给出直接答案，在re-watch后对其进行精炼，解决推理优先行为与预训练模型答案优先倾向之间的不匹配；3）提出重提问（re-ask）机制：在重新观看定位到的片段时重新注入查询，以增强问题遵循能力。

Result: video-SALMONN-R³在多个视频问答基准上持续优于基座模型和QA-SFT基线，并以显著更低的计算成本超越了先前的re-watch方法。

Conclusion: 本文验证了通过强化学习实现端到端re-watch机制的有效性，消除了对昂贵CoT数据的需求，同时提升了视频LLM的问答性能与计算效率。

Original Abstract: Video large language models (LLMs) are often constrained by computation and memory budgets, leading them to use reduced frame rates and spatial resolutions, which may cause them to miss critical information for question answering (QA). A practical and efficient solution is a two-stage paradigm: first perform coarse video understanding to localize relevant segments, and then re-watch these segments at higher temporal or spatial fidelity. In this paper, we present video-SALMONN-R$^3$, the first end-to-end video-LLM that enables re-watch through reinforcement learning without relying on chain-of-thought (CoT) cold-start. This design removes the need for costly CoT data annotations and avoids CoT-based supervised fine-tuning (SFT), which can otherwise degrade the pretrained video understanding abilities. To address the mismatch between the reasoning-first behavior induced by re-watch and the answer-first tendency of pretrained video-LLMs, we propose a re-answer strategy, in which the model first produces a direct answer in the first watch and then refines it after re-watching. Finally, to improve question adherence during re-watching, we propose a re-ask mechanism that re-injects the query when revisiting localized segments. Experimental results show that video-SALMONN-R$^3$ consistently outperforms both the base model and the QA-SFT baseline, while surpassing prior re-watch-based approaches with significantly lower computational cost. Code, models, and data will be publicly released upon acceptance.

中文摘要翻译: 视频大语言模型（LLMs）通常受计算和内存预算的限制，因而采用降低的帧率和空间分辨率，这可能导致它们遗漏问答（QA）所需的关键信息。一种实用且高效的解决方案是两阶段范式：先通过粗粒度视频理解定位相关片段，然后以更高的时间或空间保真度重新观看这些片段。本文提出了video-SALMONN-R³，这是首个通过强化学习实现端到端重新观看（re-watch）机制、且无需链式思维（CoT）冷启动的视频LLM。该设计消除了对昂贵CoT数据标注的需求，并避免了CoT监督微调（SFT）可能损害预训练视频理解能力的问题。为应对re-watch所诱导的推理优先行为与预训练视频LLM的答案优先倾向之间的不匹配，我们提出了一种重回答策略（re-answer strategy）：模型首先在第一轮观看中产生直接答案，然后在re-watch后对其进行精炼。最后，为提升re-watch过程中的问题遵循能力，我们提出了一种重提问机制（re-ask mechanism），在重新审视定位到的片段时重新注入查询。实验结果表明，video-SALMONN-R³在多个基准上持续优于基座模型和QA-SFT基线，并以显著更低的计算成本超越了先前的re-watch方法。代码、模型和数据将在论文接收后公开。

</details>


### [78] [MambaRaw: Selective State Space Modeling for Efficient 4K Raw Image Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24479)
*Peize Li,Fanhu Zeng,Tongda Xu,Xingguo Xu,Xinjie Zhang,Xingtong Ge,Haotian Zhang,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MambaRaw，一种基于状态空间模型（SSM）的高效元数据原始图像重建框架，利用JPEG预览图像作为条件信息，通过空间-能量耦合上下文建模机制（TileMambaBlock和Energy-Aware Refinement）实现高效、高质量的原始图像恢复，在三个相机数据集上达到最优性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于元数据的原始图像重建方法虽能利用JPEG预览作为辅助信息，但其上下文模型（尤其是注意力机制）随特征图尺寸二次方增长，在高分辨率（如4K）场景下计算开销过大，难以实用化。因此需要一种更高效的上下文建模方法来降低计算复杂度。

Method: 提出MambaRaw框架，核心是一个空间-能量耦合上下文建模机制，包含两个轻量化模块：(1) TileMambaBlock——仅对信息密集的图块进行Mamba风格的选择性扫描，提升效率；(2) Energy-Aware Refinement (EAR)——一个恒等初始化的残差模块，增强特征表示以匹配原始信号的长尾能量分布。

Result: 在Sony、Olympus、Samsung三个相机数据集上，MambaRaw在低元数据比特率下PSNR提升1.2-1.4 dB，端到端编码延迟降低约9%，超越了现有的元数据基线方法，达到了JPEG引导原始重建任务的最新最优水平。

Conclusion: MambaRaw通过引入状态空间模型替代传统的注意力机制，在保证高质量原始图像重建的同时显著提升了计算效率，为高分辨率原始图像的实用化重建提供了有效解决方案。

Original Abstract: In-camera JPEG previews are ubiquitous in raw image formats and provide an sRGB reference at negligible storage cost. Although existing metadata-based reconstruction frameworks can exploit this side information when recovering raw images, their context models often become computationally expensive especially at high resolution, eg, 4K raw image, given that attention mechanisms scale quadratically with feature maps, hindering its practical application. To address these limitations, we propose MambaRaw, a JPEG-conditioned metadata-based raw image reconstruction framework that uses State Space Models (SSMs) to estimate entropy parameters efficiently. Our key contribution comprises a Spatial-Energy Coupled Context Modeling mechanism with two lightweight modules: (1) TileMambaBlock, which performs Mamba-style selective scanning only on information-dense tiles to improve the efficiency; and (2) Energy-Aware Refinement (EAR), an identity-initialized residual module that enhance feature representation to match the long-tail energy distribution of raw signals. Extensive experiments on three camera datasets (Sony, Olympus, Samsung) show consistent improvements over strong metadata-based baselines and set a new state of the art for JPEG-guided raw reconstruction with great efficiency. Notably, at low metadata bitrates, MambaRaw increases PSNR by 1.2--1.4 dB and reduces end-to-end coding latency by about 9%. Code is released at https://github.com/Peizeli1/MambaRaw.

中文摘要翻译: 相机内JPEG预览在原始图像格式中无处不在，以极低的存储成本提供sRGB参考图像。尽管现有的基于元数据的重建框架可以利用这一辅助信息来恢复原始图像，但其上下文模型在高分辨率（如4K原始图像）下往往计算开销巨大，因为注意力机制随特征图尺寸呈二次方缩放，阻碍了实际应用。为解决这些局限性，我们提出MambaRaw，一种JPEG条件化的基于元数据的原始图像重建框架，利用状态空间模型（SSM）高效估计熵参数。我们的核心贡献是一个空间-能量耦合上下文建模机制，包含两个轻量化模块：(1) TileMambaBlock，仅对信息密集的图块进行Mamba风格的选择性扫描以提高效率；(2) 能量感知精化（EAR），一个恒等初始化的残差模块，增强特征表示以匹配原始信号的长尾能量分布。在三个相机数据集（Sony、Olympus、Samsung）上的大量实验表明，该方法在强元数据基线上取得了一致的改进，并在JPEG引导的原始重建任务中达到了新的最优水平，同时具有极高的效率。值得注意的是，在低元数据比特率下，MambaRaw的PSNR提升了1.2-1.4 dB，端到端编码延迟降低了约9%。代码已发布在https://github.com/Peizeli1/MambaRaw。

</details>


### [79] [Advancing WordArt-Oriented Scene Text Recognition: Datasets and Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24484)
*Xingsong Ye,Yongkun Du,Jiaxin Zhang,Haojie Zhang,Chong Sun,Chen Li,Jing Lyu,Zhineng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对艺术字（WordArt）场景文本识别（WATER）任务，从数据和模型两方面提出解决方案：构建了200万规模的合成数据集WATER-S（含SynthWordArt渲染和VL模型生成的互补子集），并设计了支持任意形状输入和自回归解码的WATERec模型，在WordArt-Bench上达到90.40%准确率，大幅超越现有方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有场景文本识别（STR）数据集和方法主要面向常规场景文本和固定模板输入，难以扩展到高度定制化的艺术字（WordArt）识别任务，且现有艺术字数据规模极小（仅千/万级），限制了该领域的发展。

Method: 数据方面：构建WATER-S数据集，包含两个互补子集——1）通过升级的渲染管线SynthWordArt生成的高精度可控合成数据；2）结合Qwen3-VL进行提示挖掘、Z-Image进行图像合成的真实多样数据。模型方面：提出WATERec，采用支持任意形状输入的视觉编码器与自回归解码器，突破固定模板STR在艺术字上的结构瓶颈。

Result: WATERec在WordArt-Bench上达到90.40%准确率，大幅超越通用视觉语言模型和OCR专用视觉语言模型，在非规则文本（如艺术字）上实现最优性能。

Conclusion: 通过大规模合成数据与适配非规则文本的模型架构设计，可有效解决艺术字识别这一更具挑战性的场景文本识别任务，为WATER任务提供了强有力的基线方案。

Original Abstract: WordArt (artistic text) features highly customized fonts, textures, and layouts, making WordArt-oriented scene TExt Recognition (WATER) substantially more challenging than general Scene Text Recognition (STR). Existing STR datasets and methods, typically built around regular scene text and fixed-template inputs, struggle to scale to WATER. Thus, we aim to advance this task from both data and model perspectives. On the data side, we construct a 2M synthetic dataset, WATER-S, with the scale improved by hundreds of times compared to existing artistic text data. WATER-S consists of two complementary subsets. One rendered by an upgraded rendering pipeline (SynthWordArt), which provides highly accurate and controllable synthetic WordArt data. The other is generated by combining Qwen3-VL for prompt mining and Z-Image for image synthesis, which improves the coverage of realistic and diverse data. On the model side, we propose WATERec. It adopts an visual encoder supporting arbitrary-shaped inputs and an autoregressive decoder to model complex layouts, structurally breaking the bottleneck of fixed-template STR on WordArt. Experiments show that this architecture outperforms prior STR methods, achieving state-of-the-art performance on irregular texts such as WordArt. Together with WATER-R, carefully reorganized from existing real STR data, our strong baseline with the new synthetic data and model design reaches 90.40% accuracy on WordArt-Bench, surpassing both general-purpose and OCR-specialized vision-language models by a large margin. Code and data are available at https://github.com/YesianRohn/WATER.

中文摘要翻译: WordArt（艺术字）具有高度定制化的字体、纹理和布局，使得面向艺术字的场景文本识别（WATER）比常规场景文本识别（STR）更具挑战性。现有的STR数据集和方法通常围绕常规场景文本和固定模板输入构建，难以扩展到WATER任务。因此，我们旨在从数据和模型两个方面推进该任务。在数据方面，我们构建了一个200万规模的合成数据集WATER-S，其规模相比现有艺术字数据提升了数百倍。WATER-S包含两个互补子集：一个由升级后的渲染管线（SynthWordArt）生成，提供高精度且可控的合成艺术字数据；另一个结合了Qwen3-VL进行提示挖掘和Z-Image进行图像合成，提升了真实多样数据的覆盖率。在模型方面，我们提出WATERec，采用支持任意形状输入的视觉编码器和自回归解码器来建模复杂布局，从结构上突破了固定模板STR在艺术字上的瓶颈。实验表明，该架构优于以往的STR方法，在艺术字等非规则文本上达到了最先进性能。结合从现有真实STR数据中精心整理的WATER-R，我们的强基线在新合成数据和模型设计下在WordArt-Bench上达到90.40%的准确率，大幅超越通用视觉语言模型和OCR专用视觉语言模型。代码和数据已在https://github.com/YesianRohn/WATER公开。

</details>


### [80] [RetiSEM: Generalising Causal Models for Fragmented Biomedical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24488)
*Inam Ullah,Imran Razzak,Shoaib Jameel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RetiSEM框架，通过领域约束的结构方程建模，在有限多模态资源下实现因果图恢复与中介分析，在合成基准和真实碎片化数据中优于无约束基线方法。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 从碎片化的生物医学数据（临床、分子、影像变量常不完整或未联合观测）中学习因果模型具有挑战性，现有方法难以有效利用有限的多模态资源进行因果发现。

Method: 提出RetiSEM框架：将变量组织为生物学信息分块，应用禁边约束，并分解路径层面效应为总效应（TE）、自然直接效应（NDE）和自然间接效应（NIE）三部分。

Result: 在十个合成基准场景中，RetiSEM相比无约束基线方法实现了更低的结构误差和更高的因果准确性；在真实数据分析中，视网膜变量主要表现为下游生物标志物样指标，存在较小但可检测的间接效应。

Conclusion: RetiSEM作为一种可解释框架，能够在资源有限的生物医学AI场景中有效检验结构化因果假设。

Original Abstract: Learning causal models from fragmented biomedical data is challenging because clinical, molecular, and imaging variables are often incomplete or not jointly observed. We propose RetiSEM, a domain-constrained structural equation modelling (SEM) framework for causal graph recovery and mediation analysis under limited multimodal resources. This proposed work organises variables into biologically informed blocks, applies forbidden-edge constraints, and decomposes pathway-level effects into TE, NDE, and NIE components. We evaluate RetiSEM across ten synthetic benchmark scenarios that vary in dimensionality, nonlinearity, causal depth, and pathway structure, together with a fragmented real-world setting that combines NHANES clinical variables with externally derived retinal representations. This approach achieves lower structural error and higher causal accuracy than unconstrained baselines across the synthetic benchmarks. In the real-data analysis, retinal variables behave mainly as downstream biomarker-like indicators, with smaller but detectable indirect effects. These findings support our strategy as an interpretable framework for testing structured causal hypotheses in limited-resource biomedical AI. The code and resources for this work are publicly available at: https://github.com/Inamullah-Colab/ReitSEM.

中文摘要翻译: 从碎片化的生物医学数据中学习因果模型具有挑战性，因为临床、分子和影像变量常常是不完整的或未被联合观测。我们提出了RetiSEM，一种在有限多模态资源下进行因果图恢复和中介分析的领域约束结构方程建模框架。该工作将变量组织为生物学信息分块，应用禁边约束，并将路径层面的效应分解为总效应（TE）、自然直接效应（NDE）和自然间接效应（NIE）三部分。我们在十个合成基准场景（涵盖不同维度、非线性程度、因果深度和路径结构）以及一个碎片化真实场景（结合NHANES临床变量与外部衍生的视网膜表征）上评估了RetiSEM。该方法在合成基准上实现了比无约束基线方法更低的结构误差和更高的因果准确性。在真实数据分析中，视网膜变量主要表现为下游生物标志物样指标，存在较小但可检测的间接效应。这些结果支持我们的策略作为在资源有限的生物医学AI中检验结构化因果假设的可解释框架。本工作的代码和资源已在https://github.com/Inamullah-Colab/ReitSEM公开提供。

</details>


### [81] [VistaRef: Boosting Visual Spatial Orientation Awareness for Pointing-to-Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24498)
*Ling Li,Zhizhen Cai,Xinkun Wu,Ziyu Zhu,Jiaqing Lyu,Bowen Liu,Zhidong Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VistaRef通过局部手部实体建模、几何射线建模和方向一致性对齐损失，显式增强空间方位感知，在指向性手势理解任务中显著提升了定位精度，相比基线取得14个点的绝对提升。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 基于Transformer的视觉模型在通用目标检测中表现优异，但其全局注意力机制容易忽略微观几何关系，导致指向任务中手指姿态隐含的指向射线捕捉不准确，出现指向漂移和定位模糊，尤其在处理远处或密集目标时问题突出。

Method: 提出VistaRef框架：(1) 局部手部实体建模(LHEM)模块，通过手部姿态嵌入增强模型对细微手指偏差的捕捉能力；(2) 几何射线建模(GRM)模块，将隐式方位信息转换为显式空间几何特征，通过注意力机制引导特征聚合与深度融合；(3) 方向一致性对齐损失(OCAL)，协同监督手部存在性和指向一致性。

Result: 实验结果表明，VistaRef在定位精度上相比基线取得14个点的绝对提升。定性分析进一步证实，该框架有效建模了从手部到目标的几何关联，弥补了传统Transformer在复杂场景中的空间感知鸿沟。

Conclusion: VistaRef通过显式增强空间方位感知，有效解决了Transformer在指向性手势理解中的微观几何关系缺失问题，为增强现实和人机协作中的空间交互提供了更精确的基础支持。

Original Abstract: Grounding deictic gestures in natural images is fundamental to AR and human-robot collaboration, providing a basis for seamless spatial interaction. While Transformer-based visual models have achieved significant progress in general object detection, their global attention mechanisms often neglect micro-geometric relationships, degrading orientation accuracy. In pointing tasks, this deficiency manifests as an inability to accurately capture the pointing ray implied by finger poses, which results in pointing drift and localization ambiguity when dealing with distant or densely packed objects. To address this, we propose VistaRef, a framework designed to explicitly enhance spatial orientation awareness. First, we develop the Local Hand Entity Modeling (LHEM) module, which incorporates hand-pose embeddings to strengthen the model's capability to capture subtle finger deviations. Second, drawing inspiration from multi-view geometry, we construct the Geometric Ray Modeling (GRM) module to transform implicit orientation information into explicit spatial geometric features, guiding feature aggregation and deep fusion via attention mechanisms. Furthermore, we introduce a novel Orientation-Consistent Alignment Loss (OCAL) to synergistically supervise hand presence and pointing consistency, ensuring that all architectural improvements collectively serve the core objective of spatial localization. Experimental results demonstrate that VistaRef significantly outperforms the baseline, achieving a 14-point absolute gain in grounding accuracy. Qualitative analysis further confirms that VistaRef effectively models the geometric correlation from hand to target, bridging the spatial perception gap inherent in traditional Transformers for complex scenarios. Code: https://github.com/lingli1724/VistaRef.

中文摘要翻译: 将指向性手势定位于自然图像中是增强现实和人机协作的基础，为无缝空间交互提供了支撑。尽管基于Transformer的视觉模型在通用目标检测方面取得了显著进展，但其全局注意力机制往往忽略微观几何关系，降低了方位估计的准确性。在指向任务中，这一缺陷表现为无法准确捕捉手指姿态所隐含的指向射线，导致在远处或密集目标场景中出现指向漂移和定位模糊。为解决这一问题，我们提出VistaRef框架，旨在显式增强空间方位感知。首先，我们设计了局部手部实体建模（LHEM）模块，通过引入手部姿态嵌入来增强模型捕捉细微手指偏差的能力。其次，受多视图几何启发，我们构建了几何射线建模（GRM）模块，将隐式方位信息转换为显式空间几何特征，通过注意力机制引导特征聚合与深度融合。此外，我们提出了一种新颖的方向一致性对齐损失（OCAL），协同监督手部存在性和指向一致性，确保所有架构改进都共同服务于空间定位这一核心目标。实验结果表明，VistaRef显著优于基线方法，在定位精度上取得了14个点的绝对提升。定性分析进一步证实，VistaRef有效建模了从手部到目标的几何关联，弥补了传统Transformer在复杂场景中空间感知的固有不足。代码：https://github.com/lingli1724/VistaRef。

</details>


### [82] [GeoIMO: Geometry-Driven Independent Motion Classification for Event Cameras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24499)
*Anil Bayram Gogebakan,Filippo Marostica,Alessio Caviglia,Alessandro Savino,Stefano Di Carlo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需标注的几何驱动框架，利用事件流中的自运动结构对物体进行静态/运动分类，无需学习或人工标注。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有汽车事件数据集依赖基于外观的帧管道标注，难以适用于运动感知的事件处理。

Method: 采用带偏航补偿的焦点膨胀模型估计全局背景运动，通过尺度不变残差量化局部运动与预测的偏差来标记运动物体，并用时间稳定性增强鲁棒性。

Result: 在MVSEC和Prophesee 1 Megapixel数据集上表现一致，偏航补偿在转弯时提升效果，简单平移局部模型在精度与效率间取得良好平衡。

Conclusion: 该方法无需学习或人工标注，可与任意输入边界框配合，有效实现事件流中物体运动状态分类。

Original Abstract: Existing automotive event datasets rely on appearance-based annotations from frame pipelines, making them poorly suited for motion-aware event perception. We present a geometry-driven, annotation-free framework that classifies detected objects as static or independently moving by exploiting ego-motion structure directly from the event stream. A Focus of Expansion model with yaw compensation estimates global background motion, while objects are labeled as moving when local motion deviates from this prediction, as quantified by a scale-invariant residual. Temporal stabilization improves robustness across consecutive event windows. The method requires no learning, no manual motion labels, and works with any input bounding boxes. Experiments on MVSEC and the Prophesee 1 Megapixel Automotive Detection dataset demonstrate consistent performance across diverse driving scenarios, with yaw compensation improving results during turns and a simple translational local model offering a favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off.

中文摘要翻译: 现有汽车事件数据集依赖基于外观的帧管道标注，难以适配运动感知的事件处理。我们提出一种几何驱动、无需标注的框架，通过直接从事件流中利用自运动结构，将检测到的物体分类为静态或独立运动。带有偏航补偿的焦点膨胀模型用于估计全局背景运动，当局部运动偏离该预测时，物体被标记为运动，偏离程度由尺度不变残差量化。时间稳定性增强了连续事件窗口的鲁棒性。该方法无需学习、无需人工运动标签，可与任意输入边界框配合。在MVSEC和Prophesee 1 Megapixel汽车检测数据集上的实验表明，该方法在多种驾驶场景下表现稳定，偏航补偿在转弯时提升效果，简单的平移局部模型在精度与效率之间提供了良好的平衡。

</details>


### [83] [What Do Flow-Based Inverse Solvers Approximate? A Posterior-Transport View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24516)
*Jian Xu,Delu Zeng,John Paisley,Qibin Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从后验传输（posterior transport）角度统一分析了基于确定性流匹配先验的逆问题求解方法（FlowDPS、FLOWER、PnP-Flow等），证明贝叶斯条件化仅需对源分布重新加权而非修正速度场，揭示了轨迹引导方法是最小动能修正场的近似，并指出现有方法存在后验偏差和模式坍塌问题，最后提出一种廉价且原理性的速度修正求解器。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有流匹配逆问题求解器（FlowDPS、FLOWER、PnP-Flow）虽经验效果良好，但其每步修正项实际近似了什么、生成样本与真实后验分布之间的距离如何，从未被理论刻画过。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 1) 理论推导：证明对确定性流先验，贝叶斯条件化完全由源分布重新加权实现，而非速度场修正；2) 将轨迹引导求解器解释为最小动能修正场，并揭示FlowDPS/FLOWER/PnP-Flow分别为零阶/高斯/近端近似；3) 提出一种便宜且原理性的速度修正求解器。

Result: 1) 2D可控实验：源重新加权在所有指标上匹配真实后验，而轨迹引导方法误差大200-800倍并导致后验模式坍塌，且与引导强度无关；2) 在AFHQ、CelebA数据集及OOD场景下，所提速度修正求解器具有竞争力，能产生多样化后验样本且不确定性可与重建误差相关。

Conclusion: 确定性流先验的贝叶斯条件化应通过对源分布重新加权实现；现有轨迹引导方法是不同近似方案，存在固有偏差；所提速度修正求解器兼顾原理性与实用性，能生成有不确定性信息的多样化后验样本。

Original Abstract: A growing family of training-free solvers -- FlowDPS, FLOWER, PnP-Flow and their diffusion ancestors (DPS, DAPS) -- repurpose a pretrained flow-matching prior to solve imaging inverse problems by adding a measurement-guidance term to the deterministic probability-flow ODE. Despite strong empirical results, what these per-step corrections actually approximate -- and how far the resulting samples are from the true posterior $p(x\mid y)$ -- has not been characterized. We give a posterior-transport account of flow-based inverse problem solving. Our starting point is a simple but consequential fact: for a \emph{deterministic} flow prior, Bayesian conditioning is realized entirely by a \emph{reweighting of the source distribution}, not by a drift correction; pushing the reweighted source through the \emph{unmodified} velocity field yields exact posterior samples. From this we show that trajectory-guidance solvers can be read as the minimum-kinetic-energy \emph{correction} field needed to morph the unconditional source into the posterior, and that FlowDPS / FLOWER / PnP-Flow correspond to distinct zeroth-order / Gaussian / proximal approximations of this single object; we bound the resulting posterior bias in Wasserstein distance. A controlled $2$D study with a closed-form posterior confirms the theory decisively: source reweighting matches the true posterior to the Monte-Carlo floor on every metric, whereas trajectory guidance incurs $200$--$800\times$ larger error and collapses posterior modes, \emph{regardless of guidance strength}. Guided by the analysis we propose a cheap, principled velocity-correction solver that is competitive across two in-domain priors (AFHQ, CelebA) and two out-of-distribution settings while, unlike point-estimate source-space optimizers, producing diverse posterior samples with uncertainty that correlates with reconstruction error.

中文摘要翻译: 一类日益增长的无训练求解器——FlowDPS、FLOWER、PnP-Flow及其扩散先辈（DPS、DAPS）——通过向确定性概率流ODE添加测量引导项，重新利用预训练的流匹配先验来求解成像逆问题。尽管经验结果出色，但这些每步修正实际近似了什么、生成样本与真实后验$p(x\mid y)$之间有多远，从未被刻画过。本文给出了基于流的逆问题求解的后验传输解释。我们的出发点是一个简单但重要的事实：对于确定性流先验，贝叶斯条件化完全由源分布的重新加权实现，而非漂移修正；将重新加权后的源通过未经修改的速度场即可得到精确的后验样本。由此我们证明，轨迹引导求解器可被解读为将无条件源分布变形为后验分布所需的最小动能修正场，而FlowDPS/FLOWER/PnP-Flow分别对应这一对象的零阶/高斯/近端近似；我们在Wasserstein距离下界定了由此产生的后验偏差。一个具有闭式后验的可控2D研究决定性地验证了理论：源重新加权在每一项指标上都以蒙特卡洛精度匹配真实后验，而轨迹引导无论引导强度如何都会产生200-800倍更大的误差并导致后验模式坍塌。基于该分析，我们提出了一种廉价且原理性的速度修正求解器，在两个域内先验（AFHQ、CelebA）和两个分布外场景中均具有竞争力，且与点估计的源空间优化器不同，它能产生多样化的后验样本，其不确定性与重建误差相关联。

</details>


### [84] [VisCritic: Visual State Comparison as Process Reward for GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24525)
*Jiachen Qian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VisCritic是一个视觉过程奖励框架，通过孪生视觉Transformer比较操作前后的截图来验证GUI代理动作，无需额外人工标注即可提升长时序任务性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有过程奖励模型仅通过文本推理验证动作，忽略了GUI状态变化的视觉特性，导致长时序场景中频繁失败。

Method: 采用孪生视觉Transformer提取变化感知表示，结合动作感知评论头联合评估动作成功性、任务进度和错误类型；设计评论训练数据构建管道，从现有轨迹生成弱监督样本。

Result: 在五个基准上进行的实验和离线分析表明，VisCritic可作为即插即用增强模块融入多种GUI代理，普遍提升基准指标并提供视觉诊断线索。

Conclusion: VisCritic通过直接比较操作前后截图的可视化特征空间来验证代理动作，有效弥补了纯文本推理的不足，为GUI代理提供了轻量级且有效的视觉过程验证方案。

Original Abstract: GUI agents powered by vision-language models show strong potential for automating digital tasks, yet frequently fail in long-horizon scenarios due to the absence of step-level verification. Existing process reward models verify actions through textual reasoning alone, missing the visual nature of GUI state changes. We introduce VisCritic, a visual process reward framework that verifies agent actions by directly comparing pre-action and post-action screenshots in visual feature space. VisCritic employs a Siamese vision transformer to extract change-aware representations, coupled with an Action-Aware Critic Head that jointly evaluates action success, task progress, and error type. A critic-training data construction pipeline generates weakly supervised samples from existing trajectories without additional human labels for critic training. Experiments and offline analyses across five benchmarks demonstrate that VisCritic serves as a plug-and-play enhancement for diverse GUI agents, generally improving benchmark metrics while providing visual diagnostic cues.

中文摘要翻译: 由视觉语言模型驱动的图形用户界面代理在自动化数字任务方面展现出巨大潜力，但由于缺乏步骤级验证，在长时序场景中频繁失败。现有的过程奖励模型仅通过文本推理来验证动作，忽略了图形用户界面状态变化的视觉本质。我们提出VisCritic，一种视觉过程奖励框架，它通过在视觉特征空间中直接比较操作前和操作后的截图来验证代理动作。VisCritic采用孪生视觉Transformer提取变化感知表示，并配备一个动作感知评论头，该评论头联合评估动作成功性、任务进度和错误类型。我们还设计了一条评论训练数据构建管道，从现有轨迹中生成弱监督样本，无需为评论训练额外的人工标注。在五个基准上的实验和离线分析表明，VisCritic可作为即插即用增强模块融入多种图形用户界面代理，普遍提升基准指标，同时提供视觉诊断线索。

</details>


### [85] [ForensicsTok: Forensics-Guided Tokenized Modeling for Image Tampering Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24538)
*Lei Xu,Haowei Wang,Shen Chen,Taiping Yao,Bin Li,Changsheng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ForensicsTok通过将图像篡改定位任务重新定义为自回归序列生成任务，并引入Token Splatting解码器和分层专家融合模块，在不依赖外部分割器的情况下实现了精准的篡改定位，在六个基准上显著优于现有MLLM方法，并略优于强专家基线。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于多模态大语言模型(MLLM)的取证方法依赖外部分割解码器，这种拼接式流水线在反向传播中引入信息瓶颈，稀释了空间信号，且受限于分割器的语义先验，导致定位效果次优。

Method: 将图像篡改定位重新定义为自回归序列生成任务，提出：(1) Token Splatting Decoder (TSD)，通过码本感知的代码平滑将token映射为二值掩码，缓解确定性去token化器带来的尖锐梯度；(2) Hierarchical Expert Fusion (HEF)模块，从取证专家模型中注入多尺度特征，弥补标准MLLM中取证先验的缺失。

Result: 在六个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，ForensicsTok相比现有基于MLLM的基线方法有显著提升，相比强取证专家基线也有小幅提升，同时对扰动表现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过统一的自回归生成架构取代传统的拼接式流水线，能够有效克服信息瓶颈和语义先验限制，实现更精准、更鲁棒的图像篡改定位。

Original Abstract: Multi-modal Large Language Models (MLLMs) offer powerful reasoning for forensic tasks, yet existing approaches utilizing exogenous segmentation decoders often suffer from suboptimal localization. The reliance on stitched pipelines introduces information bottlenecks during backpropagation, which dilutes spatial signals and is limited by semantic priors of the segmentor. To address these limitations, we propose ForensicsTok, which reformulates image manipulation localization as an autoregressive sequence generation task. ForensicsTok directly generates spatially grounded token sequences, enabling precise mask prediction without intermediary supervision. Specifically, we introduce a Token Splatting Decoder (TSD) to map tokens to binary masks via codebook-aware code smoothing, which mitigates sharp gradients from deterministic detokenizers. Furthermore, to capture diverse tampering clues, we propose a Hierarchical Expert Fusion (HEF) module that injects multi-scale features from a forensic expert model. This unified architecture effectively compensates for the lack of forensic priors in standard MLLMs. Extensive experiments on six benchmarks show that ForensicsTok substantially improves over existing MLLM-based baselines and slightly improves over strong forensic expert baselines, while exhibiting stronger robustness to perturbations.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）为取证任务提供了强大的推理能力，然而现有利用外部分割解码器的方法往往存在定位效果次优的问题。这种依赖拼接式流水线的方法在反向传播过程中引入了信息瓶颈，稀释了空间信号，并受限于分割器的语义先验。为解决这些局限性，我们提出ForensicsTok，将图像篡改定位重新定义为一个自回归序列生成任务。ForensicsTok直接生成空间对齐的token序列，无需中间监督即可实现精确的掩码预测。具体而言，我们引入了一个Token Splatting解码器（TSD），通过码本感知的代码平滑将token映射为二值掩码，缓解了确定性去token化器带来的尖锐梯度问题。此外，为了捕捉多样化的篡改线索，我们提出了一个分层专家融合（HEF）模块，从取证专家模型中注入多尺度特征。这一统一架构有效弥补了标准MLLM中缺乏取证先验的不足。在六个基准上的大量实验表明，ForensicsTok相比现有基于MLLM的基线方法有显著提升，相比强取证专家基线也有小幅提升，同时对扰动表现出更强的鲁棒性。

</details>


### [86] [PointVG-R: Internalizing Geometric Reasoning in MLLMs for Precise Pointing Localization via Visual Chain of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24539)
*Ling Li,Bowen Liu,Zinuo Zhan,Jianhui Zhong,Ziyu Zhu,Bingcai Wei,Kenglun Chang,Zhidong Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 PointVG-R，一种基于推理引导的多模态大语言模型，通过几何感知推理、强化学习和冷启动数据，显著提升指向性视觉定位的性能。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统方法将输入图像编码为静态特征表示并在语言域进行推理，忽略了图像中丰富的感知线索和显式空间几何信息，导致模型在解释手势空间关系时存在认知脆弱性。

Method: (1) 设计几何推理流水线，模拟人类解读指向手势的迭代认知过程；(2) 构建 EgoPoint-CoT 高质量视觉思维链数据集，通过 SFT 和 RL 引导模型；(3) 提出基于组方差的自适应重要性加权策略，动态调整奖励信号以优化学习过程。

Result: 在 mIoU 指标上超越基线 15.86 个点，达到 SOTA 性能；消融实验验证了各模块的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过几何感知推理与强化学习的有机结合，PointVG-R 有效缓解了模型在指向性视觉定位中的认知脆弱性，显著提升了定位精度。

Original Abstract: Pointing-based visual grounding requires models to precisely locate target objects by deciphering complex spatial relationships between the visual scene and pointing gestures. Traditional methods typically encode input images into static feature representations and perform reasoning primarily within the linguistic domain, often overlooking the rich perceptual cues and explicit spatial geometry inherent in images. In this study, we aim to mitigate the cognitive vulnerability of models in interpreting gestural spatial relations by proposing PointVG-R, a reasoning-guided Multi-modal Large Language Model (MLLM). PointVG-R introduces geometric-aware reasoning for pointing-based grounding, enabling the model to think with images through the strategic integration of Reinforcement Learning (RL) and cold-start data. Specifically, we design a novel geometric reasoning pipeline that simulates the iterative cognitive process humans employ when interpreting pointing gestures. Furthermore, we construct EgoPoint-CoT, a high-quality visual Chain-of-Thought (CoT) dataset featuring detailed reasoning trajectories to guide the model via Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and RL. To address the varying quality of learning signals encountered during training, we further propose an Adaptive Importance Weighting strategy based on Group Variance, which dynamically adjusts reward signals to optimize the learning process. Experimental results demonstrate that PointVG-R achieves SOTA performance, outperforming the baseline by $\textbf{15.86}$ points in mIoU. Extensive ablation studies further validate the efficacy of our proposed modules. Code: https://github.com/lingli1724/PointVG-R.

中文摘要翻译: 基于指向的视觉定位要求模型能够通过解读视觉场景与指向手势之间的复杂空间关系，精确定位目标物体。传统方法通常将输入图像编码为静态特征表示，并主要在语言域中进行推理，往往忽略了图像中丰富的感知线索和显式的空间几何信息。在本研究中，我们旨在缓解模型在解释手势空间关系时的认知脆弱性，提出了一种基于推理引导的多模态大语言模型 PointVG-R。PointVG-R 引入了面向指向性定位的几何感知推理能力，通过强化学习与冷启动数据的策略性集成，使模型能够结合图像进行思考。具体而言，我们设计了一种新颖的几何推理流水线，模拟人类在解读指向手势时所采用的迭代认知过程。此外，我们构建了 EgoPoint-CoT，一个包含详细推理轨迹的高质量视觉思维链数据集，通过监督微调和强化学习来引导模型。为了解决训练过程中遇到的不同质量的学习信号问题，我们进一步提出了一种基于组方差的自适应重要性加权策略，该策略动态调整奖励信号以优化学习过程。实验结果表明，PointVG-R 达到了最先进的性能，在 mIoU 上超越基线 15.86 个点。大量的消融研究进一步验证了我们所提出模块的有效性。代码：https://github.com/lingli1724/PointVG-R。

</details>


### [87] [Are Text-to-Image Models Inductivist Turkeys? A Counterfactual Benchmark for Causal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24548)
*Jiayi Lei,Yuandong Pu,Xingyu Han,Rongpeng Zhu,Jing Xu,Jinyao Wang,Zijian Zhou,Bin Fu,Yuewen Cao,Yihao Liu,Yongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出反事实基准CF-World，通过三层次反事实生成任务测试文本到图像模型是否具备因果推理能力。实验发现所有模型在反事实设置下表现急剧下降，原因是模型将世界知识与视觉外观耦合为紧密模式，过度依赖训练数据中的视觉共现，因而在反事实任务中退化到常识先验。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前文本到图像生成模型在生成逼真图像方面取得了显著进展，但其成功是源于真正的因果理解，还是仅仅是视觉-文本相关性的复杂模式匹配，这一问题尚不清楚。受罗素归纳主义火鸡的启发，作者希望探究T2I模型能否在系统性违背现实世界先验的规则下生成图像。
