Result: NanoGen在ImageNet上匹配SOTA DiT基线；T2I训练计算量与ImageNet相当。ImageNet与T2I的三种指标间的Pearson相关系数在-0.377到-0.580之间，表明两者无强相关性——一个方法在ImageNet FID上提升，可能在T2I上无对应改进甚至倒退。

Conclusion: 仅用ImageNet评估DiT方法不能反映真实进展，必须在ImageNet和T2I两项任务上同时评估。建议使用DiffusionBench（综合ImageNet和T2I结果）替代仅ImageNet评估，提升改进的方法更可能代表生成建模的广泛进步。

Original Abstract: Diffusion transformer (DiT) research on image generation has converged to a single evaluation setup: class-conditional generation on ImageNet. While methods improve the FID and related metrics, it is increasingly unclear whether they reflect real progress in generative modeling. The natural alternative, i.e., text-to-image (T2I) generation, is perceived as too costly or inconvenient to train and evaluate and is often skipped. We argue that this perception no longer holds. We introduce NanoGen, a unified DiT training and evaluation framework. NanoGen matches state-of-the-art DiT baselines on ImageNet and, with 12 lines of configuration change, also trains competitive text-to-image models. It currently supports RAE, VAE, pixel-space, and MeanFlow diffusion methods under both ImageNet and T2I setups. Under NanoGen, training T2I requires comparable compute to ImageNet. After training 21 latent diffusion models with NanoGen, we observe that method ranking shows no strong correlation between ImageNet and T2I generation: Pearson correlation is between -0.377 and -0.580 across three metrics. This suggests that a method which improves class-conditional ImageNet FID may show no corresponding improvement on T2I, clearly indicating the necessity of evaluating DiTs on both tasks. To this end, we summarize ImageNet and text-to-image results, which yields DiffusionBench, a holistic benchmark for DiT research. We recommend reporting DiffusionBench in place of ImageNet alone: methods that improve DiffusionBench are more likely to reflect broader progress.

中文摘要翻译: 关于图像生成的扩散Transformer（DiT）研究已收敛到一个单一的评估设置：在ImageNet上进行类条件生成。虽然各种方法改进了FID及相关指标，但这些指标是否能反映生成建模的真实进展变得越来越不确定。自然的替代方案——文生图（T2I）生成——被认为训练和评估成本过高或不方便，因此常被跳过。我们认为这一看法已不再成立。本文引入NanoGen，一个统一的DiT训练与评估框架。NanoGen在ImageNet上匹配最先进的DiT基线，并且通过12行配置更改，也能训练出具有竞争力的文生图模型。它目前支持RAE、VAE、像素空间和MeanFlow四种扩散方法，涵盖ImageNet和T2I两种设置。在NanoGen框架下，训练T2I所需的计算量与ImageNet相当。在用NanoGen训练了21个潜扩散模型后，我们观察到方法排名在ImageNet和T2I生成之间没有强相关性：三个指标上的Pearson相关系数在-0.377到-0.580之间。这表明，一个在类条件ImageNet FID上有所改进的方法，在T2I上可能没有相应的提升，清楚地说明有必要在两个任务上同时评估DiT。为此，我们汇总了ImageNet和文生图的结果，形成了DiffusionBench，一个面向DiT研究的整体基准。我们建议用DiffusionBench替代仅用ImageNet进行评估：那些能改进DiffusionBench的方法更可能反映更广泛的进步。

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [SpaCE: Rethinking Spatial Capacity and Generalization in Multi-Frame Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23717)
*Mariana Costa,Camila Ferreira,Alberlucia Rafael Soarez,Alejandro Torres*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出SpaCE理论框架，首次严格刻画了多帧输入下多模态大语言模型的空间推理能力、样本复杂度和泛化保证，并在多个基准上验证了理论边界的紧致性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在空间理解任务上取得了显著经验进展，但多帧空间推理的理论基础仍是空白，缺乏对其能力边界和样本效率的严格理论分析。

Method: 构建SpaCE理论框架，包含四项主要结果：(1)基于互信息的信息论上界；(2)样本复杂度界Θ(d_eff·K_max/(ε²·δ))；(3)PAC-Bayes分布偏移泛化界；(4)显式3D表示与隐式推理之间的偏差-方差权衡及范式选择条件。

Result: 在MultiSPA、CA-VQA和SpatialRGPT基准上验证了理论边界具有经验紧致性，并发现帧互补性是多帧空间推理能力的关键驱动因素。

Conclusion: 该框架为理解多帧空间推理何时、为何以及如何成功提供了首个严格的理论基础。

Original Abstract: Multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable empirical progress in spatial understanding through large-scale training on spatial visual question answering datasets. However, the theoretical foundations of multi-frame spatial reasoning remain entirely unexplored. We present SpaCE, a rigorous theoretical framework that characterizes the spatial reasoning capacity, sample complexity, and generalization guarantees of MLLMs operating on multi-frame inputs. We establish four main results. First, we prove an information-theoretic upper bound on spatial reasoning accuracy in terms of the mutual information between multi-frame observations and spatial targets. Second, we derive a sample complexity bound of order $Θ(d_{\mathrm{eff}} \cdot K_{\max} / (\varepsilon^2 \cdot δ))$, where $d_{\mathrm{eff}}$ is the effective spatial dimension and $K_{\max}$ bounds the KL divergence of the learned posterior. Third, we provide a PAC-Bayes generalization bound for multi-frame spatial reasoning under distribution shift. Fourth, we formally characterize the bias-variance trade-off between explicit 3D representations and implicit reasoning approaches, identifying the crossover conditions under which each paradigm is provably preferable. We validate our theoretical predictions on the MultiSPA, CA-VQA, and SpatialRGPT benchmarks, demonstrating that our bounds are empirically tight and that frame complementarity is the key driver of multi-frame spatial capacity. Our framework provides the first principled theoretical foundation for understanding when, why, and how multi-frame spatial reasoning in MLLs succeeds.

中文摘要翻译: 多模态大语言模型通过在空间视觉问答数据集上进行大规模训练，在空间理解方面取得了显著的经验进展。然而，多帧空间推理的理论基础仍然完全未被探索。我们提出了SpaCE，一个严格的理论框架，用于刻画基于多帧输入的多模态大语言模型的空间推理能力、样本复杂度和泛化保证。我们建立了四项主要结果。第一，我们证明了关于多帧观测与空间目标之间互信息的空间推理准确率的信息论上界。第二，我们推导了阶为Θ(d_eff·K_max/(ε²·δ))的样本复杂度界，其中d_eff是有效空间维度，K_max是学习后验的KL散度的上界。第三，我们提供了分布偏移下多帧空间推理的PAC-Bayes泛化界。第四，我们正式刻画了显式3D表示与隐式推理方法之间的偏差-方差权衡，识别了每种范式在何种交叉条件下被证明是更优的。我们在MultiSPA、CA-VQA和SpatialRGPT基准上验证了我们的理论预测，表明我们的边界在经验上是紧致的，且帧互补性是多帧空间能力的关键驱动因素。我们的框架为理解多帧空间推理在多模态语言模型中何时、为何以及如何成功提供了首个原则性的理论基础。

</details>


### [118] [An Empirical Study of Entropy-Conserving Binarization in H.264/AVC CABAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23753)
*Vinamra Singh*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文首次将熵保持二值化（ECB）集成到CABAC生产级二进制算术编码器中，与UEG、单上下文Huffman及逐位位置上下文Huffman（HuffmanPos）进行基准对比。实验表明：在低熵源上，码率差距主要由上下文分配驱动而非二值化的符号位计数；ECB在大字母表上带来7-10倍解码延迟，论文提出了一种交错单遍变体以解决该问题。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: ECB理论上能保持m元数据到m-1位二进制串的熵映射，但从未在实际生产级二进制算术编码器（如CABAC）中评估过，其实际压缩效率和计算成本尚不清楚。

Method: 将ECB集成到从头实现的CABAC框架中，与UEG、单上下文Huffman和逐位位置上下文Huffman（HuffmanPos）共享同一个M-coder后端，在合成源、程序生成图像的DCT残差以及完整Kodak 24图集上进行基准测试（共2,480次往返试验，逐位精确验证）。

Result: 在程序图像上，Q=8时ECB超过单上下文Huffman，Q=32时低27个百分点；Kodak图集上ECB在所有量化级别均优于单上下文Huffman，差距从0.031增长到0.113 bits/symbol；HuffmanPos在15个源单元中的12个上超过ECB，其余3个最多落后0.56个百分点；ECB在大字母表上解码延迟增加7-10倍。

Conclusion: 低源熵下码率差距主要由上下文分配驱动而非二值化的每符号位数；ECB的率效率以解码延迟为代价；所提出的交错单遍变体可以消除该延迟差距。

Original Abstract: CABAC, the entropy coder of H.264/AVC and the basis for HEVC and VVC, decomposes multi-symbol values into bins via a binarization scheme before a binary arithmetic coder. H.264 uses Truncated Unary plus k-th order Exp-Golomb (UEG); alternatives include canonical Huffman and the entropy-conserving binarization (ECB), which provably preserves entropy mapping m-ary data to m-1 binary strings but has not been evaluated inside a production binary arithmetic coder. We integrate ECB into a from-scratch CABAC implementation alongside UEG, single-context Huffman, and a Huffman variant with per-bin-position contexts (HuffmanPos), all sharing one M-coder backend. We benchmark all four on synthetic sources, DCT residuals from a procedural image, and the full 24-image Kodak suite (2,480 round-trip trials, bit-exact verified). On the procedural image, a sparsity-driven crossover at Q=8 lets ECB overtake single-context Huffman, reaching 27 percentage points below at Q=32. On Kodak the crossover shifts below the tested range and ECB beats single-context Huffman at every Q, the gap growing from 0.031 to 0.113 bits per symbol. HuffmanPos, sharing Huffman's codewords but allocating one context per bin position, beats ECB on 12 of 15 source cells and loses by at most 0.56 percentage points on the other three, despite the same per-symbol bin count as single-context Huffman. This isolates the dominant mechanism: at low source entropy the rate gap is driven primarily by context allocation over the bin stream, not the binarization's per-symbol bin count. ECB's rate efficiency costs 7 to 10x in decoder latency on large alphabets, traced to an O(N*m) decoder; we sketch an interleaved single-pass variant that would close this gap. Code, benchmarks, and raw data are open source.

中文摘要翻译: CABAC是H.264/AVC的熵编码器，也是HEVC和VVC的基础，它通过二值化方案将多符号值分解为二进制算术编码器之前的比特位（bin）。H.264采用截断一元码加k阶指数哥伦布码（UEG）；替代方案包括经典哈夫曼编码和熵保持二值化（ECB），后者理论上能保持m元数据到m-1位二进制字符串的熵映射，但从未在实际生产级二进制算术编码器中评估过。我们将ECB集成到一个从零实现的CABAC框架中，与UEG、单上下文哈夫曼以及一种按位位置分配上下文的哈夫曼变体（HuffmanPos）并列，所有方案共享同一个M-coder后端。我们在合成源、程序生成图像的DCT残差以及完整24张Kodak图像集上对四种方案进行基准测试（共2,480次往返试验，逐位精确验证）。在程序生成图像上，量化参数Q=8时出现稀疏驱动的交叉点，ECB超过单上下文哈夫曼，并在Q=32时达到低于后者27个百分点的水平。在Kodak图像集上，交叉点移至测试范围以下，ECB在所有Q值下均优于单上下文哈夫曼，差距从0.031增加到0.113比特/符号。HuffmanPos共享哈夫曼的码字但为每个位位置分配一个上下文，在15个测试源单元中的12个上超过ECB，在其余3个上最多落后0.56个百分点，尽管其每符号比特数与单上下文哈夫曼相同。这隔离出主导机制：在低源熵条件下，码率差距主要由二进制流上的上下文分配驱动，而非二值化的每符号位数。ECB的率效率在大字母表上以7到10倍解码延迟为代价，可追溯至O(N*m)的解码器；我们勾勒出一种交错单遍变体，可消除这一差距。代码、基准测试和原始数据均已开源。

</details>


### [119] [Promise and challenges of heart chamber segmentation from non-contrast CT scans using contrastive unpaired image translation: a feasibility study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23879)
*Jing Wang,Tong Yu,Hao-En Lu,Zixue Zeng,Joseph K. Leader,Xin Meng,Jianbing Zhu,Jiantao Pu*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出ChameleonNet框架，利用对比非配对翻译（CUT）网络和豪斯多夫距离损失增强的nnU-Net，实现从非增强CT扫描中进行无手动标注的心腔分割，在合成和真实非增强CT上取得较好性能，但心室的体积误差仍需改进。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 非增强CT扫描中心腔分割通常需要大量手动标注的非增强CT数据，标注成本高且耗时。本文旨在探索无需非增强CT手动标注即可实现心腔分割的可行性，利用对比非配对图像翻译技术将增强CT转化为非增强CT，从而利用已有的增强CT标注训练分割模型。

Method: 提出ChameleonNet框架，包含两个阶段：（1）使用带解耦对比学习（DCL）损失的对比非配对翻译（CUT）网络，将增强CT合成非增强CT；（2）在合成非增强图像上，使用豪斯多夫距离损失增强的nnU-Net训练四个心腔（左心房、左心室、右心房、右心室）的分割模型。

Result: 在合成非增强CT上，四个心腔的DSC为0.91-0.94，HD95为3.63-5.74 mm。在真实非增强CT上，体积Pearson相关系数为0.82-0.93（均p<0.001），MAPE为9.22%-20.79%，MPE为-12.52%至4.67%。

Conclusion: ChameleonNet证明了无需非增强CT手动标注即可进行心腔分割的可行性，但左心室和右心室的体积误差表明在临床应用前仍需进一步改进和验证。

Original Abstract: Purpose: To evaluate the feasibility and challenges of heart chamber segmentation from non-contrast CT scans using contrastive unpaired image translation and deep learning-based segmentation. Approach: We developed ChameleonNet, a framework utilizing the Contrastive Unpaired Translation (CUT) network with decoupled contrastive learning (DCL) loss to synthesize non-contrast CT from contrast CT scans. Using annotations of four heart chambers (left atrium (LA), left ventricle (LV), right atrium (RA), and right ventricle (RV)) from contrast scans, we trained a Hausdorff distance loss-enhanced nnU-Net on synthesized non-contrast images. The translation model was trained with 35,538 contrast-enhanced and 37,197 non-contrast CT slices. The segmentation model was trained with 292 synthesized non-contrast scans. Performance was evaluated using Dice similarity coefficient (DSC) and 95th Hausdorff distance (HD95) on 36 synthesized non-contrast scans, and volume agreement on 36 real non-contrast CT scans was assessed using Pearson correlation, mean absolute percentage error (MAPE), and mean percentage error (MPE). Results: The segmentation model achieved DSC of 0.94 (0.01), 0.91 (0.04), 0.92 (0.03), 0.93 (0.02), and HD95 of 3.63 (1.49), 5.74 (4.08), 5.18 (1.77), 5.51 (3.21) mm on synthesized non-contrast images for LA, LV, RA, and RV, respectively. On real non-contrast CT scans, Pearson correlations were 0.93, 0.82, 0.87, and 0.89 (all p<0.001), with MAPE ranging from 9.22% to 20.79%, and MPE ranging from -12.52% to 4.67%. Conclusions: ChameleonNet demonstrated feasibility for heart chamber segmentation from non-contrast CT without manual non-contrast annotations. However, volume errors, particularly for LV and RV, indicate that further refinement and validation are needed before clinical use.

中文摘要翻译: 目的：评估使用对比非配对图像翻译和基于深度学习的分割方法从非增强CT扫描中进行心腔分割的可行性与挑战。方法：我们开发了ChameleonNet框架，该框架利用带有解耦对比学习（DCL）损失的对比非配对翻译（CUT）网络，从增强CT扫描合成非增强CT。利用增强CT扫描中四个心腔（左心房（LA）、左心室（LV）、右心房（RA）、右心室（RV））的标注，我们在合成的非增强图像上训练了豪斯多夫距离损失增强的nnU-Net。翻译模型使用35,538张增强CT切片和37,197张非增强CT切片进行训练。分割模型使用292张合成非增强扫描进行训练。性能评估使用Dice相似系数（DSC）和95%豪斯多夫距离（HD95）在36张合成非增强扫描上进行，并在36张真实非增强CT扫描上使用Pearson相关性、平均绝对百分比误差（MAPE）和平均百分比误差（MPE）评估体积一致性。结果：分割模型在合成非增强图像上，对LA、LV、RA、RV的DSC分别为0.94（0.01）、0.91（0.04）、0.92（0.03）、0.93（0.02），HD95分别为3.63（1.49）、5.74（4.08）、5.18（1.77）、5.51（3.21）mm。在真实非增强CT扫描上，Pearson相关系数为0.93、0.82、0.87和0.89（均p<0.001），MAPE范围为9.22%至20.79%，MPE范围为-12.52%至4.67%。结论：ChameleonNet证明了在没有非增强CT手动标注的情况下进行心腔分割的可行性。然而，体积误差，尤其是左心室和右心室的误差，表明在临床应用前仍需进一步改进和验证。

</details>


### [120] [E-MRL: Cross-view Aligned Evidence-driven Multimodal Reinforcement Learning for Reliable 3D Tumor Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23888)
*Sijing Li,Zhongwei Qiu,Zhuoya Wang,Boxiang Yun,Zhenyu Yi,Jianwei Xu,Wenqiao Zhang,Yingda Xia,Ling Zhang*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出交叉视图对齐的证据驱动多模态强化学习框架(E-MRL)，通过将报告生成建模为“诊断-定位-验证”的马尔可夫决策过程，并引入关键证据切片定位与跨视图一致性奖励，显著减少3D CT肿瘤报告生成中的视觉幻觉，提升诊断准确性与临床可解释性。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有的视觉语言模型在三维医学报告生成中存在严重的视觉幻觉，缺乏对3D CT数据的视觉锚定；现有的监督微调和强化学习策略仅优化文本保真度，实质上奖励的是基于语言先验的正确诊断而非真实的视觉感知，导致模型缺乏对视觉证据的真正利用。

Method: 提出E-MRL框架，将报告生成过程建模为“诊断-定位-验证”的马尔可夫决策过程；模型在生成全局诊断报告的同时，明确训练定位一个“关键证据切片”；引入跨视图一致性奖励，通过重新查询所选关键切片的局部视觉特征，验证其与金标准报告的语义对齐程度，从而为正确定位的推理提供额外奖励。

Result: 在大规模3D CT肿瘤数据集上的实验表明，E-MRL相比SFT和RL基线方法显著减少了视觉幻觉，提高了诊断准确性。

Conclusion: E-MRL提供了一种临床可解释的、基于视觉证据的肿瘤分析解决方案，有效实现了视觉锚定的报告生成。

Original Abstract: While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) show great promise in volumetric medical report generation, they frequently suffer from visual hallucinations and a lack of grounding in 3D CT data. Current Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and Reinforcement Learning (RL) strategies typically optimize text fidelity alone, essentially rewarding correct diagnoses derived from language priors rather than genuine visual perception. To address this, we propose cross-view aligned Evidence-driven Multimodal Reinforcement Learning (Evidence-MRL, noted as E-MRL), a reliable RL reasoning framework that formulates the generation process as a Markov Decision Process of "diagnosis-localization-verification". Unlike standard approaches, our model is explicitly trained to identify a "key evidence slice" alongside the global diagnostic report, grounding its findings in verifiable visual evidence. Crucially, we introduce a novel cross-view consistency reward, which validates the semantic alignment between the golden-standard report and a local visual re-query of the selected key slice, providing additional rewards for correctly-localized reasoning. Experiments on large-scale 3D CT tumor datasets demonstrate that E-MRL significantly reduces hallucinations and improves diagnostic accuracy compared to SFT and RL baselines, offering a clinically interpretable solution for visually-grounded and tumor analysis.

中文摘要翻译: 尽管视觉语言模型在三维医学报告生成中展现出巨大潜力，但它们经常遭受视觉幻觉的困扰，且缺乏对3D CT数据的视觉锚定。当前的监督微调和强化学习策略通常仅优化文本保真度，实质上奖励的是基于语言先验的正确诊断，而非真正的视觉感知。为了解决这一问题，我们提出了交叉视图对齐的证据驱动多模态强化学习框架（E-MRL），这是一种可靠的强化学习推理框架，将生成过程建模为“诊断-定位-验证”的马尔可夫决策过程。与标准方法不同，我们的模型被明确训练为在生成全局诊断报告的同时定位一个“关键证据切片”，将其发现锚定在可验证的视觉证据上。关键的是，我们引入了一种新颖的跨视图一致性奖励，用于验证金标准报告与所选关键切片局部视觉重新查询之间的语义对齐程度，从而为正确定位的推理提供额外奖励。在大规模3D CT肿瘤数据集上的实验表明，与SFT和RL基线方法相比，E-MRL显著减少了幻觉并提高了诊断准确性，为视觉锚定的肿瘤分析提供了一种临床可解释的解决方案。

</details>


### [121] [A Dual Edge Spatial Jacobian Image Graph for Interpretable Diabetic Retinopathy Grading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24168)
*Inam Ullah,Imran Razzak,Shoaib Jameel*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 提出了一种双边缘空间-雅可比图像图（dual-edge spatial-Jacobian image graph），用于可解释的糖尿病视网膜病变分级，通过将眼底图像表示为包含四条证据流、两条边缘分支和轻量级注意力融合的图结构，在APTOS数据集上取得了良好性能，并定位为可解释表示学习工具而非临床部署分类器。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 临床糖尿病视网膜病变分级不仅需要图像级别的标签，还需要理解病变证据如何在视网膜血管周围分布，以及这些证据与定量血管生物标志物之间的关系。现有方法缺乏对这种血管-病变几何关系和生物标志物敏感性的建模能力。

Method: 将每张眼底图像建模为一个图节点，包含四条对齐的证据流：AutoMorph血管信息、DR-XAI风格病变证据图、128维基于病变的对比图像嵌入、AutoMorph形态计量生物标志物。空间边缘分支编码血管-病变几何关系，雅可比边缘分支建模嵌入-生物标志物敏感性，通过轻量级双token注意力融合两类边缘形成最终图像图。

Result: 在2,910张匹配的非增强APTOS图像上，完整图模型达到0.8076准确率、0.8312二次加权kappa、0.5915宏F1、0.9330相邻级别准确率；可转诊DR达到0.9055准确率和0.9711 AUROC。

Conclusion: 该框架被定位为一种可解释的表示学习工具，用于病变-生物标志物假设生成，而非可直接部署的临床分类器。

Original Abstract: Automated diabetic retinopathy (DR) grading from colour fundus photographs can achieve strong predictive performance, but clinical interpretation requires more than an image-level label. It requires understanding how lesion evidence is distributed around retinal vessels and how this evidence relates to quantitative vascular biomarkers. We present a dual-edge spatial-Jacobian image graph for interpretable DR grading. Each fundus image is represented as a graph node with four aligned evidence streams: AutoMorph vessel information ($X_1$), DR-XAI-style lesion evidence maps ($X_2$), a 128-dimensional lesion-based contrastive image embedding ($X_3$), and AutoMorph morphometric biomarkers ($X_4$). The spatial edge branch ($X_{12}$) encodes vessel-lesion geometry, while the Jacobian branch ($X_{34}$) models embedding-biomarker sensitivity. Lightweight two-token attention fuses both edge families into a final image graph. On 2,910 matched non-augmented APTOS images, the full graph achieves 0.8076 accuracy, 0.8312 quadratic weighted kappa, 0.5915 macro-F1, and 0.9330 adjacent-grade accuracy; referable DR reaches 0.9055 accuracy and 0.9711 AUROC. The framework is positioned as an explainable representation-learning tool for lesion-biomarker hypothesis generation, rather than as a deployment-ready clinical classifier. The code is available at https://github.com/Inamullah-Colab/dual-edge-dr-graph-xai.

中文摘要翻译: 从彩色眼底照片进行自动糖尿病视网膜病变（DR）分级可以获得强大的预测性能，但临床解读需要的不仅仅是图像级别的标签。它需要理解病变证据如何在视网膜血管周围分布，以及这些证据如何与定量的血管生物标志物相关联。我们提出了一种双边缘空间-雅可比图像图用于可解释的DR分级。每张眼底图像被表示为一个图节点，包含四条对齐的证据流：AutoMorph血管信息（$X_1$）、DR-XAI风格的病变证据图（$X_2$）、128维基于病变的对比图像嵌入（$X_3$）以及AutoMorph形态计量生物标志物（$X_4$）。空间边缘分支（$X_{12}$）编码血管-病变几何关系，而雅可比边缘分支（$X_{34}$）建模嵌入-生物标志物敏感性。轻量级双token注意力融合两类边缘家族，形成最终的图像图。在2,910张匹配的非增强APTOS图像上，完整图模型达到0.8076准确率、0.8312二次加权kappa、0.5915宏F1和0.9330相邻级别准确率；可转诊DR达到0.9055准确率和0.9711 AUROC。该框架被定位为一种可解释的表示学习工具，用于病变-生物标志物假设生成，而非可直接部署的临床分类器。代码可在https://github.com/Inamullah-Colab/dual-edge-dr-graph-xai获取。

</details>


### [122] [High Resolution Sediment-Specific Surface Soil Moisture Retrieval Using Sentinel-1 Time Series and Auxiliary Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24364)
*Alireza Hamedianfar,Oleg Antropov,Matthieu Molinier,Ulla Salmela,Hanna Kukkula,Lauri Seitsonen,Pauliina Liwata-Kenttälä,Maarit Middleton*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本研究利用原位土壤湿度传感器与多传感器SAR影像（Sentinel-1/2）结合机器学习方法，对芬兰某石灰岩矿区的表层土壤水分进行高精度反演。结果表明，融合多源特征（SAR后向散射、时序湿度指数、光学数据、地形和温度）的树集成方法（LightGBM、RF、XGBoost）表现最佳，RMSE低至0.037-0.050 m³/m³，R²达0.90，且加入沉积物类型信息可进一步提升精度。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于SAR的土壤水分反演方法在矿区等复杂地表环境下精度有限，缺乏针对特定沉积物类型的校准模型，且对多源传感器融合的潜力挖掘不足。因此，本文旨在探索结合原位传感器与多传感器SAR影像，通过沉积物特异性校准模型提升不同天气条件下土壤水分反演的空间分辨率和准确性。

Method: 以芬兰东南部石灰岩矿区为研究区，使用Copernicus Sentinel-1 C波段SAR影像和Sentinel-2光学影像，辅以IoT电容式湿度传感器获取原位参考数据。采用XGBoost、LightGBM、随机森林（RF）、线性回归和k近邻回归五种机器学习方法，构建包含Sentinel-1后向散射、时序土壤湿度指数、Sentinel-2光学特征、地形和温度预测因子的多源特征集，评估不同特征组合和沉积物类型对反演精度的影响。

Result: 最佳配置下RMSE为0.037-0.050 m³/m³（3.7-5.0 vol%），R²达0.90；树集成方法（LightGBM、RF、XGBoost）表现最优且最稳定；粘土和有机土误差最低，浮选砂和砾石误差较高；加入沉积物信息使仅用Sentinel-1的反演提升超过2 vol%，但在多源特征集下增益有限。

Conclusion: 沉积物特异性校准的树集成模型可显著提高矿区土壤水分反演精度，多源传感器融合策略优于单一SAR数据源，但沉积物信息的附加价值在特征丰富时边际递减。该方法可为矿区环境监测提供高分辨率、空间显式的土壤水分估算。

Original Abstract: In this study, we examine the potential of continuous ground moisture monitoring over a mining site using a combination of in-situ soil moisture sensors and multi-sensor SAR images. We focus on assessing and improving methodologies for retrieval of surface soil moisture, i.e. ground moisture, from SAR measurements focusing on detailed in situ reference observations for several key geomaterials, i.e. sediments, typical in the study site. The mining site represents a limestone quarry locate in the southeastern Finland. Our hypothesis is that sediment-specific well-calibrated models can be instrumental in improving soil moisture retrieval under different weather conditions to produce spatially explicit soil moisture estimates at high resolution compared to baseline approaches. Studied SAR data are represented by Copernicus Sentinel-1 C-band images, while auxiliary datasets include optical Sentinel-2 data. Reference data were collected using IoT enabled capacitance sensors. The examined machine learning methods include Xgboost, LightGBM, RFs, linear regression and k-nearest neighbors regression. The best performance was achieved with the most comprehensive feature set which combines Sentinel-1 backscatter, time-series based soil moisture indices, Sentinel-2 optical, topographic, and temperature predictors. In the best sediment-area-level configurations, RMSE decreased to 0.037-0.050 m^3 m^(-3) (3.7-5.0 volumetric % points), with R^2 values reaching 0.90. Tree-based ensemble methods, especially LightGBM, RF, and XGBoost, provided the most accurate and stable predictions. Accuracy varied by sediment texture, with the lowest errors for clay and organic soil and higher errors for flotation sand and gravel. Adding sediment information improved Sentinel-1-only retrievals by more than 2 vol-%, but provided little additional benefit when richer multi-source feature sets were used.

中文摘要翻译: 在本研究中，我们探讨了利用原位土壤湿度传感器与多传感器SAR影像相结合，对矿区进行连续地表水分监测的潜力。我们重点评估并改进了基于SAR测量反演表层土壤水分（即地表湿度）的方法，聚焦于研究区内几种关键地质材料（即沉积物）的详细原位参考观测。该矿区位于芬兰东南部的一个石灰岩采石场。我们的假设是，针对特定沉积物进行良好校准的模型，有助于在不同天气条件下改善土壤水分反演，从而相较于基线方法生成高分辨率的空间显式土壤水分估算。研究的SAR数据为哥白尼Sentinel-1 C波段影像，辅助数据集包括光学Sentinel-2数据。参考数据通过物联网电容式传感器采集。所考察的机器学习方法包括XGBoost、LightGBM、随机森林、线性回归和k近邻回归。性能最佳的是特征集最全面的配置，该特征集融合了Sentinel-1后向散射、基于时间序列的土壤水分指数、Sentinel-2光学数据、地形和温度预测因子。在最优的沉积物区域级配置下，RMSE降至0.037-0.050 m³/m³（3.7-5.0体积百分比），R²达到0.90。基于树的集成方法，尤其是LightGBM、随机森林和XGBoost，提供了最准确且最稳定的预测。精度因沉积物纹理而异，粘土和有机土的误差最低，浮选砂和砾石的误差较高。加入沉积物信息使仅基于Sentinel-1的反演提升了超过2 vol%，但在使用更丰富的多源特征集时，附加增益有限。

</details>


### [123] [Female-RHINO: A Real-Time Scanner-Integrated Framework for Automated Quantitative Uterine MRI Analysis and Structured Reporting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24390)
*Deepak Bhatia,Saad Ahmad,Smiti Tripathy,Maria Camila Bustos Vivas,Lieselotte Kratzsch,Anika Knupfer,Jordina Aviles Verdera,Susanne Schulz-Heise,Matthias May,Jana Hutter*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出Female-RHINO，一种与MRI扫描仪实时集成的AI辅助框架，可在图像采集过程中自动进行子宫MRI定量分析和结构化报告，无需人工干预。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 子宫MRI的标准化评估因解剖变异、观察者依赖性以及缺乏工作流集成的自动分析工具而面临挑战。

Method: 端到端系统，集成了与MRI扫描仪的在线通信和基于深度学习的分析，包括分割和 anatomical landmark 检测模型（在500+多中心数据集上训练），执行体积测量、检测常见偶然发现（如子宫肌瘤和纳博特囊肿），并提取六个解剖标志点用于生物测量评估。

Result: 子宫Dice系数0.82，子宫肌瘤0.80； landmark检测平均径向误差3.7 mm；端到端处理<70秒；在前瞻性部署中实现了即时的标准化和可重复分析。

Conclusion: 该系统实现了实时扫描仪集成AI的自动化子宫MRI分析和报告，有望提高盆腔成像的标准化、效率和临床工作流。

Original Abstract: Standardized assessment of uterine MRI remains challenging due to anatomical variability, observer dependence, and the lack of workflow-integrated automated analysis tools. This work presents Female-RHINO: (R)eproductive (H)ealth (I)maging A(N)alysis T(O)ol, a real-time AI-assisted framework for automated quantitative uterine MRI analysis and structured reporting during image acquisition. We present an end-to-end system that integrates inline communication with the MRI scanner and deep learning-based analysis to derive quantitative uterine biomarkers from sagittal T2-weighted pelvic MRI. The framework combines segmentation and anatomical landmark detection models trained and evaluated on more than 500 multi-center datasets spanning diverse protocols, vendors, and patient populations. It performs volumetry, detects and quantifies common incidental findings such as fibroids and Nabothian cysts, and extracts six anatomical landmarks for biometric assessment. Results are compiled into a structured clinician-oriented report with integrated visualizations, without manual interaction. Evaluation on independent retrospective and prospective cohorts demonstrated robust performance across varying acquisition settings. Mean Dice similarity coefficients were 0.82 for the uterus and 0.80 for fibroids, with lower but consistent agreement for Nabothian cysts. Landmark detection achieved a mean radial error of 3.7 mm. End-to-end processing was completed in under 70 seconds, enabling availability of results during the ongoing scan. Prospective deployment yielded immediate, standardized, and reproducible analyses supported by inter-observer agreement. The proposed system enables real-time scanner-integrated AI for automated uterine MRI analysis and reporting, with potential to improve standardization, efficiency, and clinical workflow in pelvic imaging.

中文摘要翻译: 由于解剖变异、观察者依赖性以及缺乏工作流集成的自动化分析工具，子宫MRI的标准化评估仍然面临挑战。本文提出Female-RHINO——女性生殖健康影像分析工具，一种在图像采集过程中进行实时AI辅助自动化定量子宫MRI分析和结构化报告的框架。我们提出一个端到端系统，该系统集成了与MRI扫描仪的在线通信和基于深度学习的分析，从矢状T2加权盆腔MRI中推导出定量子宫生物标志物。该框架结合了分割和 anatomical landmark 检测模型，这些模型在超过500个多中心数据集上训练和评估，涵盖了多种协议、供应商和患者群体。该系统执行体积测量，检测和量化常见的偶然发现（如子宫肌瘤和纳博特囊肿），并提取六个解剖标志点用于生物测量评估。结果被编译成结构化的、面向临床医生的报告，并集成可视化，无需手动交互。在独立的回顾性和前瞻性队列上的评估表明，系统在不同采集设置下均具有稳健性能。子宫的平均Dice相似系数为0.82，子宫肌瘤为0.80，纳博特囊肿的一致性较低但稳定。Landmark检测的平均径向误差为3.7毫米。端到端处理在70秒内完成，使得结果可在持续扫描期间获得。前瞻性部署产生了即时的、标准化的和可重复的分析，并得到观察者间一致性的支持。所提出的系统实现了实时扫描仪集成AI用于自动化子宫MRI分析和报告，有望提高盆腔成像的标准化、效率和临床工作流。

</details>

### [124] [Identifying structural design principles shaping the computational abilities of recurrent neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23874)
*Tom Talpir,Elad Schneidman*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 通过系统性构建循环神经网络目录，发现局部2-环和3-环结构显著增强网络计算能力，并揭示小世界连接模式可作为连接神经结构与计算能力的设计原则。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 尽管已知特定电路架构与计算功能相关，且存在网络表达能力的理论界限，但缺乏连接有限网络结构与其计算能力之间的一般性原理。

Method: 训练大量不同连接的循环神经网络计算大量布尔函数，为小网络构建完整的"网络-功能性能目录"，分析网络结构统计量与性能的关系，并扩展到大网络及引入生物启发的稀疏连接中间神经元。

Result: 1) 计算能力在不同架构间差异很大，大部分网络性能差，大多数函数难以计算；2) 局部2-环和3-环能显著增强计算能力；3) 少量结构统计量可准确预测网络性能；4) 大网络中典型网络甚至无法近似随机函数；5) 添加少量稀疏连接的生物启发式中间神经元可大幅提升计算能力；6) 短环能提升网络容量，优于无环或仅可达性匹配的对照网络。

Conclusion: 局部环路（短环）是连接神经连通性与计算能力的关键设计原则，为探索计算网络中的结构-功能关系提供了通用框架。

Original Abstract: Understanding how the architecture of neural networks shapes the computations they carry is a central challenge in neuroscience and machine learning. While specific circuit architectures have been linked to particular network computations and theoretical bounds on expressivity of broad classes of networks have been found, we are still missing general principles connecting the structure of finite networks to their computational capabilities. Here, we characterize the computational abilities of recurrent neural networks as a function of their connectivity by training a large collection of different networks to compute a large set of Boolean functions. For small networks, we constructed the complete ``catalogs'' of network-function performance, which revealed that computational capacity varies widely across architectures and that most networks show poor performance, and most functions are hard to compute. However, we show that having local 2- and 3-cycles in a network strongly enhances its computational ability, and networks with such cycles are often the minimal architectures that can solve particular functions. We further show that a small set of structural statistics accurately predict networks' performance. Extending our analysis to large networks showed that typical networks fail even to approximate a randomly selected function. Surprisingly, adding a small number of sparsely connected biologically-inspired interneurons to the network dramatically increases computational capacity. As in small networks, adding short cycles improved networks' capacity, outperforming acyclic or reachability-matched controls. Thus, our results identify local cycles as design principles linking neural connectivity to computational power, and offer a general framework to explore structure-function relations in computing networks.

中文摘要翻译: 理解神经网络架构如何塑造其执行的计算，是神经科学和机器学习领域的一项核心挑战。尽管已有研究将特定电路架构与特定网络计算联系起来，并找到了广泛网络类别在表达能力上的理论界限，但我们仍然缺乏连接有限网络结构与其计算能力之间的一般性原理。本文通过训练大量不同连接的循环神经网络来计算大量布尔函数，系统地表征了循环神经网络的计算能力与其连通性之间的关系。对于小网络，我们构建了完整的"网络-功能性能目录"，揭示了计算能力在不同架构间存在广泛差异，大多数网络表现出较差的性能，且大多数函数难以计算。然而，我们发现网络中具有局部2-环和3-环会显著增强其计算能力，拥有这些环路的网络常常是能够解决特定函数的最小架构。我们进一步证明，少量结构统计量即可准确预测网络的性能。将分析扩展到大规模网络后发现，典型网络甚至无法近似一个随机选择的函数。令人惊讶的是，向网络中添加少量稀疏连接的生物启发性中间神经元，能够大幅提升计算能力。与小网络类似，添加短环提升了网络的容量，其表现优于无环或仅可达性匹配的对照网络。因此，我们的结果将局部环路确定为连接神经连通性与计算能力的设计原则，并为探索计算网络中的结构-功能关系提供了通用框架。

</details>


### [125] [The Morality Game: An online multiplayer platform to standardize, expedite, and expand research on cooperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24037)
*Gregory N. Stanley,Alan Yang,Liam Tsimhoni,Roujia Wang,Ekdatha Arramreddy,Rohan Desai,Shulin Pan,Michael Ahn,Vijairam G. Moorthy,William Mathias,Mai Xu,James Kessler,Yahan Xing,Yang Li,Duoming Bian*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文介绍了Morality Game平台，这是一个基于博弈论实验、旨在标准化和加速合作与道德研究的平台。它支持零编码定制在线多人实验、自动化数据收集与分析，并利用动态自校正博弈树模拟道德困境。平台致力于成为涵盖全研究生命周期的集成生态系统，以提升合作研究的可复制性、数据可访问性和协作效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 当前合作与道德研究领域缺乏标准化平台，实验方法不统一、数据分散、结果难以比较和复现，研究流程效率低下。因此，作者开发了Morality Game平台，旨在通过标准化实验方法、统一数据格式、加速研究流程来推动该领域的发展。

Method: 通过构建一个基于博弈树的零代码实验配置平台，支持在线多人互动实验；自动化参与者支付、数据收集与分析流程；利用动态自校正博弈树生成可控的抽象实验场景；提供响应式用户界面和研究者仪表盘；支持嵌套信念表示和可定制人工智能体。

Result: 平台已具备从实验配置、数据收集到分析的全流程能力，支持多种道德困境模拟，具备标准化研究方法、统一数据格式、快速聚合和比较结果的功能。未来计划包括集成社交网络功能、增强情感表达能力，以及扩展至偏远小规模社会以检验生态效度。

Conclusion: Morality Game平台通过提供标准化、自动化的研究工具，有望促进合作研究领域的协作、提升数据可访问性，并最终增加合作行为。该平台展示了推动合作研究向前发展的巨大潜力。

Original Abstract: This paper presents the Morality Game, a platform designed to standardize and accelerate research on cooperation and morality through game theory-based experiments. The Morality Game functions as a video game for science, a hub for economic game research, an open-access data repository, and a tool for expediting the research process. It allows researchers to launch customized online multiplayer experiments with zero coding, using game trees to simulate moral dilemmas. The platform automates participant payments, data collection, and analysis, promoting replication and transparency. This paper details the platform's architecture, emphasizing its capabilities for standardizing research methods, unifying data, and enabling rapid aggregation and comparison of results. The Morality Game leverages dynamic, self-correcting game trees to generate well-controlled, abstract experiments that can represent any social scenario. Participants interact through a responsive user interface, making the experiments intuitive and engaging. Researchers can configure experiments through a user-friendly dashboard, specifying various parameters and utilizing pre-created or auto-generated game trees. The platform supports nested belief representation and incorporates artificial agents with customizable traits. Plans include integrating social networking features, enhancing emotional expression capabilities, and expanding the platform's reach to remote small-scale societies to test the ecological validity of findings. By evolving into an integrated ecosystem that supports the entire research lifecycle, the Morality Game aims to foster collaboration, enhance data accessibility, and ultimately increase cooperation. This paper outlines the platform's current features, architectural details, and future directions, demonstrating its potential to advance cooperation research.

中文摘要翻译: 本文介绍了Morality Game，这是一个旨在通过基于博弈论的实验来标准化和加速合作与道德研究的平台。Morality Game的功能包括：作为科学研究的视频游戏、经济类游戏的研究中心、开放获取的数据存储库，以及加速研究流程的工具。它允许研究人员在零编码的情况下启动自定义的在线多人实验，利用博弈树来模拟道德困境。该平台自动化了参与者支付、数据收集和分析流程，促进了研究的可复制性和透明度。本文详细阐述了该平台的架构，重点介绍了其在标准化研究方法、统一数据格式以及实现结果快速聚合与比较方面的能力。Morality Game利用动态、自校正的博弈树来生成高度可控的抽象实验，这些实验可以表征任何社会场景。参与者通过响应式用户界面进行交互，使实验过程直观且富有参与感。研究人员可通过用户友好的仪表盘配置实验，指定各种参数，并利用预先创建或自动生成的博弈树。该平台支持嵌套信念表征，并纳入了具有可定制特征的人工智能体。未来计划包括集成社交网络功能、增强情感表达能力，以及将平台扩展至偏远的小规模社会，以检验研究结果的生态效度。通过演变成一个支持整个研究生命周期的集成生态系统，Morality Game旨在促进协作、增强数据可访问性，并最终提升合作水平。本文概述了该平台的当前功能、架构细节及未来方向，展示了其在推进合作研究方面的潜力。

</details>


### [126] [Graph-based analysis of inflammatory profiles in New Onset Refractory Status Epilepticus (NORSE)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24351)
*Linon Denis,Martin Guillemaud,Vincent Navarro,Aurélie Hanin,Mario Chavez*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文针对不明原因新发难治性癫痫持续状态（cNORSE）中细胞因子谱高度异质性的问题，提出了一种基于图聚类和统计建模的框架，用于将患者归入生物学验证的炎症亚群，并开发了临床友好型界面，以支持个性化免疫治疗决策。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: cNORSE患者的炎症特征存在显著异质性，导致难以选择精准的靶向免疫疗法；现有工具无法有效辅助临床医生解读复杂的细胞因子检测结果，因此亟需开发可解释、可量化的分析工具来支持个性化免疫治疗策略。

Method: 基于62名cNORSE患者的96种细胞因子血清谱数据，采用图聚类方法识别不同的炎症患者分组；在此基础上构建新模型，量化每个患者归属于生物学验证聚类（炎症亚群）的概率；使用蒙特卡洛模拟和自定义参数检验对最优模型进行统计评估；最终将框架部署为临床友好型界面。

Result: 该方法能够快速处理多个细胞因子谱，输出最可能的炎症聚类归属、相应的归属概率及统计置信度；对于纵向数据，还可追踪炎症轨迹的时程演变。

Conclusion: 对cNORSE炎症异质性进行系统性统计表征需要开发临床可操作的支持工具；本研究提出的基于聚类细胞因子谱分析框架，有望为cNORSE患者制定个性化免疫调节策略提供支撑。

Original Abstract: Background and Objectives: Cryptogenic new-onset refractory status epilepticus (cNORSE) represents one of the most severe forms of status epilepticus, occurring in patients without prior neurological disease, and remaining of unknown aetiology despite extensive diagnostic evaluation. Emerging evidence supports a role for immune dysregulation in cNORSE; however, marked heterogeneity in inflammatory signatures has been reported, complicating the selection of targeted immunotherapies. Therefore, a critical need for tools facilitating the interpretation of cytokine panels exists. Methods: Building on the identification of distinct inflammatory groups of cNORSE patients using a graph clustering approach applied to a cohort of 62 patients with serum profiling of 96 cytokines, we tailored new models to quantify attribution probability to biologically validated clusters. Statistical assessment of the most informative model involved Monte-Carlo simulations and custom-developed parametric tests. Ultimately, we applied our framework to the implementation of a clinician-friendly interface for inflammatory profiling. Results: Our approach enables quick processing of several cytokine profiles, providing the most likely inflammatory cluster, associated attribution probability, and statistical confidence. For longitudinal assessments, the proposed method may also allow tracking the evolution of inflammatory trajectories over time. Conclusion: Systematic statistical characterization of the inflammatory heterogeneity in cNORSE requires the development of clinically actionable support tools. Our study offers a framework that may support personalized immunomodulatory strategies in cNORSE patients through clustering-based cytokine profiling.

中文摘要翻译: 背景与目的：不明原因新发难治性癫痫持续状态（cryptogenic new-onset refractory status epilepticus, cNORSE）是癫痫持续状态中最严重的形式之一，发生于既往无神经系统疾病的患者，且虽经广泛诊断评估仍病因不明。新近证据支持免疫失调在cNORSE中发挥作用，但文献中报告了显著的炎症特征异质性，使得靶向免疫治疗的选择复杂化。因此，亟需开发有助于解读细胞因子检测结果的工具。方法：基于图聚类方法对62名患者的队列进行分析，通过96种细胞因子的血清谱识别出cNORSE患者的不同炎症分组，我们据此定制了新模型以量化归属至生物学验证聚类的概率。对最优模型的统计评估涉及蒙特卡洛模拟和自定义参数检验。最终，我们将该框架应用于开发临床友好型界面，以实现炎症谱分析。结果：我们的方法能够快速处理多个细胞因子谱，提供最可能的炎症聚类归属、相应的归属概率和统计置信度。对于纵向评估，所提出的方法还可追踪炎症轨迹随时间的演变。结论：对cNORSE炎症异质性的系统性统计表征需要开发临床可操作的支持工具。本研究提供了一个框架，通过基于聚类的细胞因子谱分析，可能支持cNORSE患者的个性化免疫调节策略。

</details>


### [127] [EEG Interpretation Across Chant Listening: A Single-Subject Pilot Investigation Using Spectral and Functional Connectivity Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24406)
*Prerna Singh,Aishwarya Ghosh,Neelam Sinha,Deepti Navaratna*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该技术报告基于一名5岁健康儿童的EEG数据，研究了五种听觉条件（静息态、Shiv Tandav Stotra、Mahasudarshan Mantra、Aum吟唱和Tanpura聆听）下的神经活动。通过频谱功率估计和加权相位滞后指数（wPLI）功能连接分析发现，不同听觉条件诱发了不同的神经振荡调制和网络组织模式，其中STS聆听表现出最强的广泛连接和β频段功率增强。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 探究不同文化相关听觉干预（如咒语吟唱、器乐聆听）对儿童神经活动的影响机制，为认知发展、神经教育和儿童中心神经科学研究建立方法学框架。

Method: 对一名5岁健康参与者进行EEG记录，采集五种听觉条件（RS、STS、MM、Aum吟唱、Tanpura聆听）下的脑电数据；采用频谱功率估计分析神经振荡活动；使用加权相位滞后指数（wPLI）进行功能连接分析。

Result: 频谱分析显示STS聆听在多个频段（尤其是β频段）产生最高相对功率；功能连接分析显示STS聆听具有最强且最广泛的连接模式，表现为额叶、颞叶、顶叶和枕叶之间的长程交互；Tanpura聆听产生密集且平衡的连接网络；Aum聆听表现出中等分布连接；MM和静息态连接较弱且更局部化。

Conclusion: 不同吟唱聆听条件通过皮层激活和大尺度神经同步化，调动了不同的神经机制。该研究为未来探索文化相关听觉干预在认知发展、神经教育和儿童神经科学中的应用奠定了方法学基础。

Original Abstract: This technical report presents an EEG-based investigation of neural activity across five auditory conditions: Resting State (RS), Shiv Tandav Stotra (STS), Mahasudarshan Mantra (MM), Aum Chant, and Tanpura Listening. EEG recordings acquired from a healthy 5-year-old participant were analyzed using spectral power estimation and functional connectivity measures based on the weighted Phase Lag Index (wPLI). Spectral analysis revealed condition-specific modulation of neural oscillatory activity, with STS listening producing the highest relative power across multiple frequency bands, particularly within the beta range. Functional connectivity analysis demonstrated distinct network organizations across conditions. STS listening exhibited the strongest and most widespread connectivity pattern, characterized by prominent long-range interactions among frontal, temporal, parietal, and occipital regions. Tanpura listening generated a dense yet balanced connectivity network, while Aum listening showed moderate distributed connectivity. In contrast, MM and resting-state conditions displayed comparatively weaker and more localized network organization. These preliminary findings suggest that different chant-listening conditions engage distinct neural mechanisms involving both cortical activation and large-scale neural synchronization. The study establishes a methodological framework for future investigations examining the role of culturally relevant auditory interventions in cognitive development, neuroeducation, and child-centered neuroscience research.

中文摘要翻译: 本技术报告基于脑电图（EEG）对五种听觉条件（静息态RS、Shiv Tandav Stotra STS、Mahasudarshan Mantra MM、Aum吟唱和Tanpura聆听）下的神经活动进行了研究。对一名健康的5岁参与者的EEG记录数据，采用频谱功率估计和基于加权相位滞后指数（wPLI）的功能连接测量进行分析。频谱分析揭示了条件特异性的神经振荡活动调制，其中STS聆听在多个频段（尤其是β频段）产生最高的相对功率。功能连接分析显示不同条件下存在不同的网络组织模式。STS聆听表现出最强且最广泛的连接模式，其特征是额叶、颞叶、顶叶和枕叶区域之间显著的长程交互作用。Tanpura聆听产生了密集但平衡的连接网络，而Aum聆听则表现出中等程度的分布式连接。相比之下，MM和静息态条件下的网络组织较弱且更为局部化。这些初步发现表明，不同的吟唱聆听条件通过涉及皮层激活和大尺度神经同步化的不同神经机制发挥作用。该研究为未来探讨文化相关听觉干预在认知发展、神经教育和儿童中心神经科学研究中的作用建立了方法学框架。

</details>


### [128] [A pilot study examining transcranial photobiomodulation therapy intervention in college students with insomnia](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24668)
*Jiangshan He,Lianghua Zhang,Dan Liang,Xiaoyu Wang,Tianyi Luo,Haoda Wang,Ziqi Ren,Mingzhe Jiang,Lei Zheng,Qiyuan Cheng,Hui Xie,Xueli Chen*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文探讨了大学生失眠问题及其神经机制，重点关注前额叶皮层功能低下在失眠中的关键作用，以及经颅光生物调节（tPBM）疗法对失眠的潜在治疗效果，指出其神经机制尚待阐明。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 大学生失眠问题普遍（约30%符合失眠标准），严重影响身心健康并造成社会经济负担。现有研究已揭示前额叶功能低下是失眠的关键神经基础，而靶向前额叶的tPBM疗法在伴失眠的神经精神疾病中显示出疗效，但其治疗失眠的神经机制尚不明确，亟需深入探究。

Method: 本文为综述性研究，系统梳理了失眠的过度觉醒模型理论、前额叶功能低下的神经影像学证据，以及tPBM疗法在神经精神疾病伴失眠中的临床应用研究。

Result: 研究发现：约30%大学生符合失眠标准；失眠的过度觉醒模型强调认知、情绪和生理层面的相互强化；前额叶功能低下是失眠的关键神经基础，表现为对边缘系统和脑干觉醒核团的自上而下调控失效；tPBM靶向治疗前额叶在伴失眠的神经精神疾病中显示出疗效。

Conclusion: 前额叶功能低下是失眠的重要神经机制，tPBM疗法可能通过改善前额叶功能发挥治疗失眠的作用，但其具体的神经机制仍需进一步阐明。

Original Abstract: College students commonly report insufficient sleep and poor sleep quality, with ~30% meeting insomnia criteria, posing significant threats to their physical growth, cognitive development, and overall well-being, as well as imposing a substantial economic burden on society [1]. The hyperarousal model of insomnia [2] emphasizes that hyperarousal across cognitive, emotional, and physiological domains mutually reinforces one another. Neuroimaging studies have further identified prefrontal hypoactivity as a key neural substrate underlying these dysfunctional cognitions and elevated arousal, reflecting a failure of top-down modulatory control over both limbic reactivity [3] and brainstem arousal nuclei [4]. Moreover, transcranial photobiomodulation (tPBM) therapy targeting the prefrontal cortex has demonstrated therapeutic efficacy across neuropsychiatric disorders with insomnia comorbidities [5,6], providing preliminary support for its application in insomnia. However, the neuro mechanisms underlying tPBM's therapeutic effects on insomnia remain to be elucidated.

中文摘要翻译: 大学生普遍报告睡眠不足和睡眠质量差，其中约30%符合失眠诊断标准，这对其身体发育、认知发展和整体健康构成严重威胁，同时也给社会带来沉重的经济负担[1]。失眠的过度觉醒模型[2]强调认知、情绪和生理层面的过度觉醒相互强化。神经影像学研究进一步发现，前额叶功能低下是这些功能失调性认知和觉醒水平升高的关键神经基础，反映出自上而下的调控机制对边缘系统反应性[3]和脑干觉醒核团[4]的调节失败。此外，靶向前额叶皮层的经颅光生物调节（tPBM）疗法已在伴失眠共病的神经精神疾病中显示出治疗效果[5,6]，为tPBM在失眠中的应用提供了初步支持。然而，tPBM治疗失眠的神经机制仍有待阐明。

</details>

### [129] [FEM-Based Dispersion and Mode Analysis of Rectangular, Circular, and Ridge Waveguide Geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23703)
*Sabrina Saima*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于二维有限元法（FEM）的求解器，用于计算空心金属波导的模场分布和色散特性，验证了其在空矩形和圆形波导中的高精度，并将其应用于单脊和双脊波导分析，证明脊结构能有效降低主模截止频率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 复杂波导几何结构（如脊波导）不存在精确解析解，因此需要一种鲁棒且自适应的数值计算工具来求解其模场分布和色散特性。

Method: 将无源频域麦克斯韦方程组简化为横向截面上的标量亥姆霍兹特征值问题，通过施加理想电导体（PEC）边界条件，利用二维有限元法（FEM）求解横电（TE）和横磁（TM）模族。

Result: 在空矩形和圆形波导上，该方法在截止波数、色散曲线和场图方面与解析基准高度吻合；在单脊和双脊波导中，金属脊成功重新分布了模场，并显著降低了主模的截止频率。

Conclusion: 广义特征值FEM公式是一种鲁棒且自适应的工具，适用于分析缺乏解析解的复杂波导几何结构。

Original Abstract: This paper presents a two-dimensional finite element method (FEM) solver for computing modal field distributions and dispersion characteristics of hollow metallic waveguides. To solve the waveguide problem, the source-free frequency-domain Maxwell equations are reduced to scalar Helmholtz eigenvalue formulations evaluated over the waveguide's transverse cross section. The computational method determines both transverse electric (TE) and transverse magnetic (TM) mode families by enforcing perfectly electrically conducting (PEC) boundary conditions. The framework is initially validated against analytical benchmarks using empty rectangular and circular waveguides, demonstrating high accuracy in computing cutoff wavenumbers, dispersion curves, and field maps for the first three unique modes. After validation, the solver is applied to analyze single-ridged and double-ridged waveguides. The numerical results demonstrate that introducing metallic ridges successfully redistributes the modal fields and significantly lowers the cutoff frequency of the dominant mode relative to empty rectangular guides. Ultimately, this work confirms that the generalized eigenvalue FEM formulation is a robust and adaptable tool for analyzing complex waveguide geometries where exact analytical solutions are unavailable.

中文摘要翻译: 本文提出了一种二维有限元法（FEM）求解器，用于计算空心金属波导的模场分布和色散特性。为求解波导问题，将无源频域麦克斯韦方程组简化为在波导横截面上求解的标量亥姆霍兹特征值公式。该计算方法通过施加理想电导体（PEC）边界条件，同时确定横电（TE）和横磁（TM）模族。该框架首先以空矩形和圆形波导为基准，与解析基准进行了验证，结果表明在计算前三阶独特模式的截止波数、色散曲线和场图方面具有高精度。验证之后，将求解器应用于单脊和双脊波导的分析。数值结果表明，引入金属脊能够成功重新分布模场，并且相对于空矩形波导，显著降低了主模的截止频率。最终，本研究证实了广义特征值FEM公式是一种鲁棒且自适应的工具，适用于分析缺乏精确解析解的复杂波导几何结构。

</details>


### [130] [Zero-Shot Neural Priors for Generalizable Cross-Subject and Cross-Task EEG Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23706)
*Baimam Boukar Jean Jacques,Brandone Fonya,Nchofon Tagha Ghogomu,Pauline Nyaboe,Kipngeno Koech*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种零样本跨被试脑电解码框架，通过在大型数据集上微调Transformer并采用渐进解冻策略，在未见被试上实现了优于基线模型的回归性能，推动了无校准、可泛化的脑电解码发展。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 传统脑电解码模型因个体间差异大和神经信号非平稳性，导致跨被试和跨任务泛化能力差，制约了脑机接口和神经生物标记物的实际应用。

Method: 在大规模Healthy Brain Network数据集上，采用零样本跨被试解码框架，对比了CNN基线、混合LSTM和基于Transformer的基座模型；针对回归任务，提出了一种渐进解冻（progressive unfreezing）微调策略以避免灾难性遗忘。

Result: CNN基线模型的nRMSE为0.9991，而经过微调的Transformer在未见被试上达到了0.9799，显著提升了跨被试解码性能。

Conclusion: 该工作推动了面向计算精神病学和行为预测的可扩展、免校准脑电解码的发展。

Original Abstract: The development of generalizable electroencephalography (EEG) decoding models is essential for robust brain-computer interfaces (BCI) and objective neural biomarkers in mental health. Conventional approaches have been hindered by poor cross-subject and cross-task generalization, owing to high inter-subject variability and non-stationary neural signals. We address this challenge with a zero-shot cross-subject decoding framework on the large-scale Healthy Brain Network dataset, benchmarking a convolutional neural network baseline, a hybrid LSTM, and a Transformer-based foundation model. To adapt the Transformer for regression while averting catastrophic forgetting, we propose a novel progressive unfreezing strategy. The baseline yielded an nRMSE of 0.9991, whereas our fine-tuned Transformer achieved 0.9799 on unseen subjects. This work advances scalable, calibration-free EEG decoding for computational psychiatry and behavioral prediction.

中文摘要翻译: 开发具有泛化能力的脑电图（EEG）解码模型对于构建鲁棒的脑机接口（BCI）以及心理健康领域中的客观神经生物标记物至关重要。由于个体间变异性高和神经信号的非平稳性，传统方法在跨被试和跨任务泛化方面一直受到阻碍。本文在大规模Healthy Brain Network数据集上提出了一种零样本跨被试解码框架，对卷积神经网络基线、混合LSTM和基于Transformer的基座模型进行了基准测试。为了使Transformer适应回归任务同时避免灾难性遗忘，我们提出了一种新颖的渐进解冻策略。基线的nRMSE为0.9991，而我们微调后的Transformer在未见被试上达到了0.9799。该工作为计算精神病学和行为预测中的可扩展、免校准脑电解码提供了进展。

</details>


### [131] [Coordinate-Queryable Neural Field Reconstruction for EEG Spatial Super-Resolution with Unseen-Electrode Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23707)
*Hongjun Liu,Leyu Zhou,Zijianghao Yang,Chao Yao*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于条件隐式神经表示的位置引导编码器-解码器框架，将EEG空间超分辨率重构为从部分观测通道学习共享条件头皮场，并引入保真通道损坏训练策略，实现对随机缺失和未见电极信号的稳健重建。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有EEG空间超分辨率方法学习固定的低-高通道映射，对测试时通道随机缺失鲁棒性差；实际部署中通道缺失、电极质量不稳定等问题普遍存在，亟需一种能适应可变可见通道模式的方法。

Method: （1）位置引导编码器将观测到的EEG通道及其坐标编码为潜在条件；（2）条件隐式神经表示解码器通过在目标电极坐标处查询该条件重建信号；（3）引入保真通道损坏训练策略，在混合电极状态下对编码器潜在表示施加更强约束，构建与观测通道一致的头皮场。

Result: 在多个EEG数据集上，对随机缺失通道重建和严格未见电极信号生成均有效。在AAD数据集的严格留出电极设置下，NMSE降低37.5%，SNR提升2.12 dB，优于最强基线。

Conclusion: 该框架通过条件隐式神经表示将EEG超分辨率问题转化为从部分观测学习共享头皮场，结合保真通道损坏训练，显著提升了模型对通道缺失和未见电极的泛化能力。

Original Abstract: EEG spatial super-resolution (EEGSR) in real deployments is challenged by random channel missingness, unstable electrode quality, and changing visible-channel patterns caused by bad contacts or device variability. Most existing EEGSR methods learn a fixed low-to-high channel mapping under pre-defined input-output layouts, which makes them brittle when missing channels vary at test time. In this paper, we reformulate EEGSR as learning a shared conditional scalp field from partially observed support channels. Specifically, a position-guided encoder summarizes the observed EEG channels and their coordinates into a latent condition, and a conditional implicit neural representation decoder reconstructs target EEG signals by querying this condition at desired electrode coordinates. During inference, the model directly reconstructs unseen electrode signals from the available EEG support and the queried coordinates. To strengthen the constraint of the encoded latent representation on the decoder and thereby construct a more stable scalp field consistent with the observed channels, we further introduce a fidelity-preserving channel corruption training strategy under mixed electrode states. Extensive experiments across multiple EEG datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of our framework for both random missing-channel reconstruction and strict unseen-electrode signal generation. Notably, under the strict held-out-electrode setting on AAD, our method reduces NMSE by 37.5\% and improves SNR by 2.12 dB over the strongest baseline, showing its ability to synthesize signals at electrode locations never exposed during training.

中文摘要翻译: 实际部署中的EEG空间超分辨率面临随机通道缺失、电极质量不稳定以及因接触不良或设备差异导致的可见通道模式变化等挑战。大多数现有EEG空间超分辨率方法学习的是预定义输入-输出布局下的固定低-高通道映射，这使得它们在测试时缺失通道变化时表现脆弱。本文重新将EEG空间超分辨率定义为从部分观测的支持通道中学习共享的条件头皮场。具体而言，一个位置引导编码器将观测到的EEG通道及其坐标总结为潜在条件，而一个条件隐式神经表示解码器通过在目标电极坐标处查询该条件来重建目标EEG信号。在推理阶段，模型直接从可用的EEG支持通道和查询坐标重建未见电极信号。为增强编码潜在表示对解码器的约束力，从而构建与观测通道一致的更稳定头皮场，我们进一步引入了混合电极状态下的保真通道损坏训练策略。在多个EEG数据集上的广泛实验表明，本框架在随机缺失通道重建和严格未见电极信号生成任务上均有效。值得注意的是，在AAD数据集上的严格留出电极设置下，我们的方法相比最强基线将归一化均方误差降低了37.5%，信噪比提升了2.12 dB，展示了其在训练中从未出现的电极位置上合成信号的能力。

</details>


### [132] [RadioRange: An Open-Source Digital Twin-based Ranging Simulator for UWB, Wi-Fi, and 5G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23708)
*Zhen Lyu,Yidi Chen,Li-Ta Hsu,Guohao Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: RadioRange 是一个开源、定位优先的数字孪生平台，统一了UWB、Wi-Fi和5G NR协议，在相同的射线追踪物理信道上模拟硬件损伤，支持可重现的RF测距算法比较与基准测试。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 目前缺乏一个开放的、可公平重现的平台来比较不同协议下考虑真实硬件损伤的RF测距算法，现有模拟器仅关注通信层指标，缺少测距算法、损伤模型和定位专用评估。

Method: 构建了定位优先的数字孪生平台RadioRange，在相同的射线追踪物理信道上统一UWB、Wi-Fi和5G NR三种协议；设计了三阶段共11个可独立切换的硬件损伤模型（天线级偏移、RF电路非理想性、补偿后CSI残差）；提供5种首径检测器和3种多径识别算法，支持蒙特卡洛基准测试和消融研究。

Result: 经过真实UWB和Wi-Fi测量数据验证，该平台的信道模型能够捕获几何相关的多径偏差，证明了仿真与实测的一致性。

Conclusion: RadioRange填补了RF测距领域缺乏开放、可重现基准平台的空白，为定位感知无线系统的研究与开发提供了标准化评估工具。

Original Abstract: Accurate RF-based ranging is critical for location-aware wireless systems, yet no open platform exists for fair, reproducible comparison across protocols under realistic hardware impairments. Existing simulators target communication-layer metrics and lack ranging algorithms, impairment models, and positioning-specific evaluation. We present RadioRange, an open-source, positioning-first digital twin that unifies UWB, Wi-Fi, and 5G NR on identical ray-traced physical channels. The platform models eleven independently toggleable hardware impairments across three injection stages, spanning antenna-level offsets, RF circuit non-idealities, and post-compensation CSI residuals, each with documented physical models and protocol-specific defaults. Five first-path ranging detectors and three multipath identification algorithms are provided within a protocol-specific evaluation framework, enabling controlled Monte Carlo benchmarking and systematic ablation studies. The simulator is validated against real-world UWB and Wi-Fi measurements, demonstrating that the channel model captures geometry-dependent multipath bias. RadioRange-Sim is publicly available at https://github.com/Togure/RadioRange.

中文摘要翻译: 精确的RF测距对于位置感知无线系统至关重要，然而目前还没有一个开放平台能够在真实硬件损伤条件下，跨协议进行公平且可重现的比较。现有模拟器仅关注通信层指标，缺乏测距算法、损伤模型以及面向定位的专用评估。我们提出了RadioRange，一个开源、定位优先的数字孪生平台，它在相同的射线追踪物理信道上统一了UWB、Wi-Fi和5G NR三种协议。该平台在三个注入阶段建模了11个可独立切换的硬件损伤，涵盖天线级偏移、RF电路非理想性以及补偿后的CSI残差，每个损伤模型均具有文档化的物理模型和协议特定的默认参数。平台提供了5种首径检测器和3种多径识别算法，并嵌入协议特定的评估框架，支持受控的蒙特卡洛基准测试和系统性的消融研究。该模拟器经过真实UWB和Wi-Fi测量数据的验证，结果表明其信道模型能够捕获几何相关的多径偏差。RadioRange-Sim已在https://github.com/Togure/RadioRange 公开提供。

</details>


### [133] [WiFi-Based People Counting Using Beam-Steerable Antennas: A Test-bed Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23710)
*Riccardo Bersan,Anay Ajit Deshpande,Sanaz Kianoush,Daniele Piazza,Stefano Savazzi*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文综述了利用射频信号（特别是Wi-Fi和蜂窝网络）实现泛在感知的技术，重点介绍了Wi-Fi 7标准（IEEE 802.11be）在信道状态信息（CSI）提取和人体运动感知方面的最新进展，以及利用Wi-Fi信号进行人体重识别的潜力。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 泛在感知技术是未来智能服务（如智能生活、远程医疗、自动化物流和自由空间手势交互）的关键机遇，而Wi-Fi和蜂窝网络的广泛部署为开发创新感知工具提供了有前景的平台。

Method: 利用现有Wi-Fi芯片的MIMO天线配置和OFDM子载波进行CSI提取，结合IEEE 802.11be标准提供的更宽子载波带宽（160MHz，最高320MHz），获取至少120个可用于CSI或CIR估计的导频子载波。同时利用Wi-Fi信号变化在不同人群间的差异进行人体重识别。

Result: Wi-Fi 7提供了更宽的子载波带宽（160MHz，最高320MHz），至少120个可用导频子载波用于CSI或CIR估计；Wi-Fi信号可用于追踪日常人体运动和行为，信号变化在不同个体间存在差异，可用于人体重识别。

Conclusion: 基于Wi-Fi和蜂窝网络的RF信号感知技术具有广阔前景，未来标准将引入专用感知功能（如使路由器作为FMCW雷达），当前芯片设计已支持CSI提取，Wi-Fi信号在人体运动追踪和身份重识别方面展现出重要应用价值。

Original Abstract: Ubiquitous perception through RF signals is a pivotal opportunity for future technology: it enables personalized services such as smart living, remote healthcare, automated logistics or interaction through free-space gestures. The ubiquity of Wi-Fi and cellular networks presents a promising platform for the development of innovative sensing tools. Future standards will also introduce dedicated sensing features which, for example, will allow routers to work as frequency modulated continuous wave radios targeting radar applications. Most of the current chip designs support ad-hoc firmware for CSI extraction with MIMO arrangements of the transmitter (TX) and receiver (RX) antennas and OFDM subcarriers. The CSI describes the phase shift and amplitude attenuation of multiple propagation paths on each subcarrier. The latest IEEE 802.11be standard (Wi-Fi 7) offers a wider subcarrier bandwidth of 160MHz (up to 320MHz), providing at least 120 usable pilot subcarriers for CSI or CIR estimation. Additionally, Wi-Fi signals have been recently exploited to track daily human movements and behaviors, while Wi-Fi signal variations have been shown to differ between different people and can consequently be used for their re-identification.

中文摘要翻译: 通过射频信号实现泛在感知是未来技术的关键机遇：它能支持个性化服务，例如智能生活、远程医疗、自动化物流或通过自由空间手势进行交互。Wi-Fi和蜂窝网络的普及为开发创新感知工具提供了有前景的平台。未来标准还将引入专用感知功能，例如，使路由器能够作为调频连续波雷达用于雷达应用。目前大多数芯片设计支持通过发射（TX）和接收（RX）天线的MIMO配置以及OFDM子载波进行专用的CSI提取。CSI描述了每个子载波上多条传播路径的相位偏移和幅度衰减。最新的IEEE 802.11be标准（Wi-Fi 7）提供了更宽的子载波带宽，达160MHz（最高320MHz），至少提供120个可用于CSI或CIR估计的导频子载波。此外，近期研究已利用Wi-Fi信号追踪日常人体运动和行为，同时发现Wi-Fi信号变化在不同人群间存在差异，因此可用于人体重识别。

</details>


### [134] [Optical Ground Stations for Space Communications:Systems Engineering, Availability, and Service Economics Through 2030](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23711)
*Slava G. Turyshev*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文对截至2026年6月的光学地面站（OGS）领域进行了系统工程与服务经济学评估，发现瓶颈已从峰值线路速率转向天气、捕获、调度和运维约束下的可重复服务。在标准化规划下，固定成本约为3000-4000美元/TB，且2030年可行基线为光学与射频混合方案。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 光学地面站正成为高速星地通信的网络化基础设施，但其应用受服务可用性和利用率制约，而非仅取决于光学线路速率。本文旨在从系统工程和服务经济学角度对OGS领域进行系统性评估。

Method: 结合公开飞行演示和运行记录，采用标量链路预算、可用性和成本归一化模型进行分析。参考了从星际25 Mbps到近地轨道200 Gbps等多个公开基准数据，并建立10 Gbps近地站点的标准化规划模型进行成本敏感性分析。

Result: 瓶颈已从峰值线路速率转向天气、捕获、调度和运维约束。在标准化规划下（10 Gbps近地站点，年化成本200万美元，每日计划0.5小时天气前光学接触时间，天气综合效率η=0.7），固定成本约3000-4000美元/TB。关键控制变量为占空比、有效天气多样性、共享网络负载和服务等级分配。

Conclusion: 公共产业证据应被解释为分层价值链而非单一供应商排名。2030年可行的基线方案是光学与射频混合：光学负责吞吐量、中继和频谱缓解；射频负责连续性、应急保障和确信指令路径。

Original Abstract: Optical ground stations (OGSs) are becoming networked infrastructure for high-rate space-to-Earth communications, but their adoption is governed by service availability and utilization as much as by optical line rate. This paper develops a systems-engineering and service-economics assessment of the OGS sector as of June~2026. The analysis combines public flight demonstrations and operational records with scalar link-budget, availability, and cost-normalization models. Public benchmarks span 25 Mbps from interplanetary range, 260 Mbps-class lunar links, 1.2 Gbps-class ISS relay, 1.8 Gbps operational GEO relay, 120 Gbps-class direct-to-ground demonstrations in China, and 200 Gbps from LEO in NASA's TBIRD mission. The resulting conclusion is that the bottleneck has shifted from peak line rate to repeatable service under weather, acquisition, scheduling, and operations constraints. Under one explicit planning normalization -- a 10 Gbps near-Earth station, annualized cost of $2 million/year, scheduled pre-weather optical contact time of 0.5 h/day, and weather-inclusive combined efficiency $η=0.7$ -- the fixed-cost component is of order $3\times10^{3}$--$4\times10^{3}$ USD/TB. This number is a sensitivity anchor, not a tariff forecast; the controlling variables are duty factor, effective weather diversity, shared-network loading, and service-level allocation. The public industrial evidence is best interpreted as a stratified value chain, not as a single vendor ranking. The defensible 2030 baseline is hybrid optical+radio-frequency (RF): optical for throughput, relay, and spectrum relief; RF for continuity, contingency, and assured command paths.

中文摘要翻译: 光学地面站（OGSs）正成为高速星地通信的网络化基础设施，但其应用受服务可用性和利用率制约，这与光学线路速率同等重要。本文对截至2026年6月的光学地面站领域进行了系统工程与服务经济学评估。该分析将公开飞行演示和运行记录与标量链路预算、可用性和成本归一化模型相结合。公开基准涵盖：来自星际距离的25 Mbps、260 Mbps级月球链路、1.2 Gbps级国际空间站中继、1.8 Gbps运行中地球同步轨道中继、中国120 Gbps级直接对地演示，以及NASA TBIRD任务中来自近地轨道的200 Gbps。得出的结论是，瓶颈已从峰值线路速率转向天气、捕获、调度和运维约束下的可重复服务。在一个明确的规划归一化条件下——10 Gbps近地站，年化成本200万美元，每日计划天气前光学接触时间0.5小时，包含天气的综合效率η=0.7——固定成本分量约为3×10³–4×10³美元/TB。该数字是敏感性锚点而非费率预测；其控制变量是占空比、有效天气多样性、共享网络负载和服务等级分配。公开的产业证据应被解释为分层价值链，而非单一供应商排名。2030年可防御的基线方案是光学与射频（RF）混合：光学负责吞吐量、中继和频谱缓解；射频负责连续性、应急保障和确信指令路径。

</details>


### [135] [Audio-visual Contrastive Alignment for Diffusion-based Visual-conditioned Speech Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23712)
*Colombe Mboungou,Mostafa Sadeghi,Jean-Eudes Ayilo,Romain Serizel*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出在扩散模型训练目标中添加对比性视听损失，以增强视听模态对齐，从而在不改变后验采样框架的前提下提升语音增强性能，尤其在低信噪比场景下效果显著。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于扩散的无监督视听语音增强方法虽优于纯音频版本，但交叉注意力融合中显式强制跨模态对齐的影响尚不明确，作者旨在探究并增强这种对齐以提升性能。

Method: 在扩散训练目标中引入对比性视听损失（contrastive audio-visual loss），鼓励模型更充分地利用视觉信息，同时保持原有的后验采样框架不变。

Result: 在匹配与非匹配测试数据上，该方法在干扰抑制、信号重建和感知质量方面均实现一致提升，低信噪比条件下增益最大。

Conclusion: 显式增强跨模态对齐能有效提升视听语音增强性能，尤其有助于在噪声严重的场景下更充分地利用视觉信息。

Original Abstract: Audio-visual speech enhancement (AVSE) exploits visual cues such as lip movements to recover speech in noisy environments. Recent work introduced diffusion-based unsupervised AVSE, where a speech diffusion model conditioned on visual features via cross-attention is trained and used as a data-driven prior for posterior sampling-based speech enhancement. Despite promising performance over its audio-only counterpart, the impact of explicitly enforcing cross-modal alignment in the fusion remains unclear. In this work, we propose to augment the diffusion training objective with a contrastive audio-visual loss to encourage stronger use of visual information while keeping the posterior sampling framework unchanged. Experiments across matched and mismatched test data show consistent improvements in interference suppression, signal reconstruction, and perceptual quality, with the largest gains at low SNRs. Code is available at https://github.com/ cexauce/AV-CA-DiffUSE

中文摘要翻译: 视听语音增强（AVSE）利用唇部运动等视觉线索在噪声环境中恢复语音。近期工作引入了基于扩散的无监督AVSE方法，该方法训练一个通过交叉注意力以视觉特征为条件的语音扩散模型，并将其作为基于后验采样的语音增强的数据驱动先验。尽管该方法相比纯音频版本表现出更优的性能，但在融合中显式强制跨模态对齐的影响仍不明确。本文提出在扩散训练目标中增加对比性视听损失，以鼓励模型更充分地利用视觉信息，同时保持后验采样框架不变。在匹配与非匹配测试数据上的实验表明，该方法在干扰抑制、信号重建和感知质量方面均实现了一致的提升，其中低信噪比条件下的增益最大。代码已发布于 https://github.com/cexauce/AV-CA-DiffUSE。

</details>


### [136] [Unifying Adaptive Fourier and Möbius-Based Models for Efficient and Interpretable Biomedical Signal Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23713)
*Christian Canedo,Rocío Carratalá-Sáez,Cristina Rueda*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文建立了自适应傅里叶分解（AFD）与调频莫比乌斯（FMM）模型之间的数学等价性，证明两者在有限阶情况下本质相同，且在高斯噪声假设下对应相同的优化问题。该等价性使得AFD的计算效率与FMM参数的可解释性得以统一，可用于生物医学振荡信号（如ECG、EEG）的高效解析。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 生物医学振荡信号（如心电图、脑电图）的分解方法需要在计算效率和可解释性之间取得平衡。AFD基于正交Takenaka-Malmquist展开，计算高效但缺乏生理参数的可解释性；FMM基于莫比乌斯变换，参数具有形态学意义但计算复杂。两者独立发展已久，缺乏统一的数学框架，本文旨在建立两者之间的形式化联系。

Method: 通过严格的数学推导，证明有限阶AFD与FMM分解在数学上等价；在独立高斯噪声假设下，证明两者的估计过程求解同一优化问题；将结果扩展至多通道信号场景；通过EEG分解逼近行为实验和ECG五分量分解对比实验进行验证。

Result: 理论上证明了有限阶AFD与FMM的数学等价性，以及在高斯噪声下两者优化目标的一致性；实验上，EEG示例展示了随分量数增加AFD与FMM的逼近行为关系，ECG示例对比了无标签AFD分量与生理学标识的FMM分量在典型心拍上的分解表现。

Conclusion: 本文建立的等价关系为生物医学信号分析提供了一个原则性框架，使得研究者可以同时利用AFD的计算优势（包括基于FFT的高效实现）和FMM的参数可解释性，实现振荡信号的高效且可解释的分解。

Original Abstract: Oscillatory biomedical signals such as electrocardiograms (ECG) and electroencephalograms (EEG) call for decompositions that are both computationally efficient and interpretable. This paper establishes a formal connection between two finite-order frameworks that have largely evolved independently: Adaptive Fourier Decomposition (AFD), based on orthonormal Takenaka-Malmquist expansions, and the Frequency-Modulated Mobius (FMM) model, a parametric decomposition built on Mobius transforms with morphologically meaningful parameters. We prove that finite-order AFD and FMM decompositions are mathematically equivalent. Under mild regularity assumptions, we further show that their associated estimation procedures solve the same underlying optimization problem when FMM is formulated with independent Gaussian noise. The results are extended to multi-channel signals, which are central in multilead bioelectric recordings. Practically, the equivalence clarifies how fast AFD approximations, including FFT-based implementations, relate to FMM-style parametrization and component interpretability. We illustrate these implications with an EEG example evaluating approximation behavior as the number of components increases, and with an ECG use case comparing five-component decompositions on representative beats, contrasting unlabeled AFD components with physiologically identified FMM components. Overall, the proposed equivalence provides a principled basis to leverage the computational advantages of AFD alongside the interpretability of FMM in biomedical signal analysis.

中文摘要翻译: 诸如心电图（ECG）和脑电图（EEG）等振荡生物医学信号，需要的分解方法既要计算高效又要具备可解释性。本文建立了两种长期独立发展的有限阶框架之间的形式化联系：基于正交Takenaka-Malmquist展开的自适应傅里叶分解（AFD），以及基于莫比乌斯变换、参数具有形态学意义的调频莫比乌斯（FMM）模型。我们证明了有限阶AFD与FMM分解在数学上是等价的。在温和的正则性假设下，我们进一步证明了当FMM在独立高斯噪声假设下建模时，两者的估计过程求解同一底层优化问题。该结果被扩展至多通道信号场景，而多通道信号是多导联生物电记录的核心。在实际意义上，该等价性阐明了快速AFD近似（包括基于FFT的实现）与FMM式参数化及分量可解释性之间的关系。我们通过一个EEG示例展示了随分量数量增加时的逼近行为，并通过一个ECG用例在典型心拍上对比了五分量分解，将无标签的AFD分量与生理学标识的FMM分量进行对照。总体而言，本文提出的等价性为在生物医学信号分析中兼顾AFD的计算优势与FMM的可解释性提供了原则性基础。

</details>


### [137] [Physics-Informed Path-Parametric Learning for Efficient and Lightweight CSI Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23730)
*Chunyu Ling,Jiajia Guo,Yiming Cui,Chao-Kai Wen,Shi Jin,Shuangfeng Han,Xiaoyun Wang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出HS-PINNnet，一种基于分层感知机制的物理信息神经网络用于CSI反馈，通过将多径信道模型融入网络，将高维CSI重建转化为低维多径参数估计，显著降低计算复杂度和延迟。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的CSI反馈方法将CSI视为视觉图像处理，缺乏可解释性且与多径传播原理不一致；高维CSI带来的反馈开销问题亟需解决。

Method: HS-PINNnet包含：分层感知编码器生成紧凑的多径表示；异构解码器通过专用分支估计不同参数（幅度、角度等）；PCD模块自适应估计主导路径数量；子信道共享编码与并行解码策略将高维CSI处理分解为低维子信道任务。

Result: HS-PINNnet在不同配置下均优于现有方法，FLOPs降低92.8%，FPGA仿真延迟降低两个数量级。

Conclusion: 将物理模型融入深度学习网络可提升CSI反馈的可解释性与效率，HS-PINNnet在性能和效率上均实现显著提升，尤其适用于未来XL-MIMO系统。

Original Abstract: Channel State Information (CSI) feedback is vital for high spectral efficiency in wireless systems, yet high-dimensional CSI introduce significant feedback overhead. Recent deep learning (DL) approaches alleviate this issue by treating CSI as a visual image, but such "black-box" designs often lack interpretability, producing CSI that is not consistent with multipath propagation principles. To address these limitations, this paper proposes HS-PINNnet, a Hierarchical Sensing mechanism assisted Physics-Informed Neural Network for CSI Feedback. Unlike vision-inspired methods, HS-PINNnet integrates a multipath channel model into the network, reformulating high-dimensional CSI reconstruction as low-dimensional multipath parameter estimation (e.g., amplitude, angle). HS-PINNnet features a hierarchical sensing encoder to produce a compact multipath representation, and a heterogeneous decoder for parameter-specific CSI reconstruction, with dedicated branches to estimate different parameters. Moreover, a PCD module adaptively estimates the number of dominant paths in each CSI sample to enhance generalization across diverse environments. A subchannel-wise shared encoding and parallel decoding strategy is further designed to decompose high-dimensional CSI processing into low-dimensional subchannel tasks, reducing training difficulty and improving scalability of HS-PINNnet for future extremely large-scale multiple-input multiple-output (XL-MIMO) systems. Simulation results show that HS-PINNnet outperforms the state-of-the-art under different configurations, achieving a 92.8% reduction in FLOPs and exhibiting two orders of magnitude lower FPGA simulation latency.

中文摘要翻译: 信道状态信息（CSI）反馈对于无线系统中的高频谱效率至关重要，然而高维CSI带来了显著的反馈开销。最近的深度学习方法通过将CSI视为视觉图像来缓解这一问题，但这种"黑箱"设计通常缺乏可解释性，产生的CSI与多径传播原理不一致。为解决这些局限，本文提出HS-PINNnet——一种基于分层感知机制的物理信息神经网络用于CSI反馈。与视觉启发方法不同，HS-PINNnet将多径信道模型融入网络，将高维CSI重建重新表述为低维多径参数估计（如幅度、角度）。HS-PINNnet采用分层感知编码器生成紧凑的多径表示，以及用于参数特定CSI重建的异构解码器，通过专用分支估计不同参数。此外，PCD模块自适应估计每个CSI样本中的主导路径数量，以增强在多样化环境中的泛化能力。进一步设计了子信道共享编码与并行解码策略，将高维CSI处理分解为低维子信道任务，降低训练难度并提升HS-PINNnet在未来超大规模多输入多输出（XL-MIMO）系统中的可扩展性。仿真结果表明，HS-PINNnet在不同配置下均优于现有方法，FLOPs降低92.8%，FPGA仿真延迟降低两个数量级。

</details>


### [138] [Integrated Sensing and Communications for Real-time Avatar Control in XR over 5G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23771)
*Nabeel Nisar Bhat,Javad Sameri,Rreze Halili,Rafael Berkvens,Maria Torres Vega,Jeroen Famaey*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合5G毫米波ISAC和表面肌电信号(sEMG)的多模态感知架构，用于扩展现实(XR)中的全身与手指级手势识别，实现无需手持控制器的沉浸式交互。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有XR交互方案依赖手持控制器和摄像头，难以捕捉全身姿态、限制手部自由使用，且需要良好的视距条件。为克服这些局限，需要一种无需视线约束、支持自然手部动作的多模态感知方案。

Method: 提出多模态感知架构：(1) 利用5G毫米波ISAC同时完成内容传输和粗粒度全身姿态估计，基于功率每波束对(PPBP)从标准波束管理过程中提取信号特征；(2) 结合轻量级sEMG传感器捕捉前臂肌肉活动，实现细粒度手指级手势识别。两种模态互补，实现多尺度手势识别。

Result: (1) PPBP-based人体级感知在未见过的测试用户上达到82.2±5.9%的平均准确率；(2) sEMG在不同运动设置下均表现出强判别性信息，持续取得有前景的性能。

Conclusion: 结合5G毫米波ISAC和sEMG两种模态，可以在身体级别利用现有5G信号、在手指级别利用轻量级sEMG传感器实现多尺度手势识别，构建完整的XR交互框架。

Original Abstract: Extended Reality (XR) presents a challenging use case for 5G and 6G networks, requiring high data-rates and lowlatency communication to deliver a truly immersive experience. Moreover, in order to seamlessly translate physical actions to the virtual world, accurate gesture recognition and pose estimation are required. Current XR interaction solutions based on handheld controllers and cameras cannot easily capture full-body poses, inhibit the free use of hands, and require good visibility and a clear line of sight. In this work, we propose a multimodal sensing architecture for XR that combines 5G MillimeterWave (mmWave) Integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) and surface electromyography (sEMG) signals. 5G mmWave ISAC cannot only be used to deliver content wirelessly to the Head-mounted display (HMD), but also the same communication signals can be used to derive coarse body-level gestures and poses of the user, to support real-time avatar control. For fine-grained finger-level gestures, our architecture leverages lightweight sEMG sensors that capture forearm muscle activity. To illustrate the need of both modalities, we present evaluations of both sensing technologies. At the body level (5G), our architecture relies on power-per-beam-pair (PPBP), which can be computed from standard beam management or beam sweeping procedures of the 5G NR standard. PPBP-based sensing achieves 82.2$\pm$5.9% average accuracy when evaluated on users not seen during training. For fine-grained finger-level interactions, we show that surface electromyography (sEMG) carries strong discriminative information achieving consistent promising performance across different movement settings. Thus, combining the two modalities enables multi-scale gesture recognition, at the body level via existing 5G signals and finger level via lightweight sEMG sensors, forming a complete XR framework.

中文摘要翻译: 扩展现实（XR）对5G和6G网络提出了严峻的挑战，需要高数据速率和低延迟通信来提供真正沉浸式的体验。此外，为了将物理动作无缝地映射到虚拟世界，需要精确的手势识别和姿态估计。当前基于手持控制器和摄像头的XR交互解决方案难以轻松捕捉全身姿态，限制了手的自由使用，并且需要良好的可见性和清晰的视线。在这项工作中，我们提出了一种用于XR的多模态感知架构，该架构结合了5G毫米波（mmWave）集成感知与通信（ISAC）和表面肌电信号（sEMG）。5G毫米波ISAC不仅可以用于将内容无线传输到头戴式显示器（HMD），而且相同的通信信号可用于推导用户粗粒度的身体级手势和姿态，以支持实时化身控制。对于细粒度的手指级手势，我们的架构利用轻量级sEMG传感器来捕捉前臂肌肉活动。为了说明两种模态的必要性，我们对两种感知技术进行了评估。在身体级别（5G），我们的架构依赖于每波束对功率（PPBP），该指标可从5G NR标准的标准波束管理或波束扫描过程中计算得到。基于PPBP的感知在训练期间未见的用户上达到了82.2±5.9%的平均准确率。对于细粒度的手指级交互，我们表明表面肌电信号（sEMG）携带了强判别性信息，在不同运动设置下均持续取得了有前景的性能。因此，结合两种模态可以实现多尺度手势识别——通过现有5G信号实现身体级别，通过轻量级sEMG传感器实现手指级别——从而形成一个完整的XR框架。

</details>


### [139] [Generative Modeling for Physiological Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23864)
*Xinqi Bao,Ernest Kamavuako,Saikat Chatterjee*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了生成模型在生理信号（心血管、神经、肌肉、外周等）中的应用，涵盖GANs、VAEs、扩散模型、自回归序列模型等主流方法，并提出了一个分层评估框架（信号级、分布级、生理有效性、任务效用、泛化鲁棒性），为生成模型在生理信号研究中的使用与评估提供结构化指导。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 生理信号应用受限于标签数据稀缺、类别不平衡、噪声干扰、采集条件异构及隐私限制等问题，生成模型为克服这些障碍提供了潜在途径。本文旨在系统梳理生成模型在各类生理信号中的研究进展，并建立统一的评估框架，以指导未来的研究与实践。

Method: 综述方法：系统梳理心血管、神经、肌肉、外周及专用生理信号上的生成模型研究，覆盖GANs、自编码器/变分自编码器、扩散模型、自回归序列模型及混合架构；并构建一个分层评估框架，包含信号级相似性、数据集级分布、生理有效性、任务导向效用、泛化性与鲁棒性五个层级。

Result: 本文为综述性论文，主要结果是提出了一个结构化的生成模型分类体系和五层评估框架，并总结了不同信号类型与生成模型之间的匹配关系及现有评估实践的不足。

Conclusion: 通过将信号特定约束、生成角色、模型家族与评估证据相链接，本文为未来在生理信号研究中应用和评估生成模型提供了结构化指导。

Original Abstract: Physiological signals support clinical diagnosis, health monitoring, rehabilitation, wearable sensing, and human--machine interaction. However, their applications are often constrained by limited labeled data, class imbalance, noisy or incomplete recordings, heterogeneous acquisition settings, and privacy restrictions. Generative modeling has therefore attracted increasing attention as a means of addressing some of these barriers. Recent studies have used generative models to augment scarce datasets, restore degraded recordings, translate between modalities, and synthesize conditional physiological waveforms. This review summarizes recent work on generative modeling for cardiovascular, neural, muscular, peripheral, and specialized physiological signals. Major model families are covered, including generative adversarial networks (GANs), autoencoders and variational autoencoders (AEs/VAEs), diffusion models, autoregressive sequence models, and hybrid architectures. In addition, it organizes existing evaluation practices into a hierarchical framework spanning signal-level similarity, dataset-level distribution, physiological validity, task-oriented utility, and assessments of generalization and robustness. By linking signal-specific constraints, generative roles, model families, and evaluation evidence, this review provides structured guidance for the future use and evaluation of generative models in physiological-signal research.

中文摘要翻译: 生理信号支持临床诊断、健康监测、康复、可穿戴传感与人机交互。然而，其应用常受限于标注数据稀缺、类别不平衡、噪声或记录不完整、异构采集条件以及隐私限制。因此，生成建模作为克服这些障碍的手段日益受到关注。近期研究利用生成模型来扩充稀缺数据集、恢复退化记录、实现模态间转换以及合成条件生理波形。本综述总结了心血管、神经、肌肉、外周及专用生理信号上生成模型的最新工作。涵盖的主要模型家族包括生成对抗网络（GANs）、自编码器与变分自编码器（AEs/VAEs）、扩散模型、自回归序列模型以及混合架构。此外，本文将现有评估实践组织成一个分层框架，涵盖信号级相似性、数据集级分布、生理有效性、任务导向效用以及泛化性与鲁棒性评估。通过将信号特定约束、生成角色、模型家族与评估证据相链接，本综述为未来在生理信号研究中应用和评估生成模型提供了结构化指导。

</details>


### [140] [Unlocking Realism and Interpretability in Wireless Channel Synthesis: A Physics-Guided Generative Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23868)
*Satyavrat Wagle,Akshay Malhotra,Shahab Hamidi-Rad,Aditya Sant,David J. Love,Christopher G. Brinton*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种将参数化物理几何信道（PPGC）模型与生成方法相结合的框架，通过线性化重构和张量分解解决非凸优化、物理不可解释性和标签依赖问题，生成真实且可解释的信道矩阵。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有机器学习方法用于无线通信需要大量高保真信道数据，但空中采样成本极高且无法准确反映真实信道多样性；生成模型虽被提出但存在输出不满足物理可行性、无法提供环境洞察、训练需要标注数据等问题。

Method: 集成参数化物理几何信道（PPGC）模型与生成方法；通过线性化重构PPGC模型以解决非凸优化问题，确保训练中梯度平滑流动；引入张量分解框架以灵活处理无线信道参数数量；将线性化重构与参数提取任务兼容。

Result: 通过与先前基线模型对比，评估生成的场景特定样本与真实信道在相似性及下游压缩任务中的效用。

Conclusion: 所提方法能生成物理可行的信道矩阵，提供可解释的参数域表示，且无需标注数据，同时克服了非凸优化带来的训练困难。

Original Abstract: In recent years, machine learning (ML) methods have become increasingly popular for wireless communication systems. These require large amounts of data reflecting the behavior of realistic channels with high fidelity. However, sampling over-the-air (OTA) channel data is an extremely resource-intensive process which cannot accurately represent the variety of real world channels. This results in the need for realistic training data for ML systems. To this end, generative models have been proposed to synthesize channel data. However,(i) the outputs produced by such methods may not correspond to physically viable channels, (ii) the outputs may not provide insights into the associated environment, and (iii) training the generative model may need labeled data, requiring resource intensive data annotation. Through this work, we address these issues by integrating a parametric, physics-based geometric channel (PPGC) modeling framework derived from planar wave propagation equations, with generative methods to produce realistic channel matrices with interpretable representations in the parameter domain. To overcome the limitations of the resulting non-convex optimization landscape, we propose a linearized reformulation of the PPGC model to ensure smooth gradient flow during training, while also providing insights into the underlying physical environment. We incorporate a tensor decomposition framework into the linearized reformulation to allow for flexibility in the number of wireless channel parameters. We also show the compatibility of this reformulation with parameter extraction tasks. We evaluate our model against prior baselines by comparing generated, scenario-specific samples to true channels in terms of their similarity and through their utility in downstream compression tasks.

中文摘要翻译: 近年来，机器学习方法在无线通信系统中日益流行。这些方法需要大量反映真实信道行为的高保真数据。然而，空中信道数据的采样是一个极其资源密集的过程，无法准确代表真实世界信道的多样性。这导致机器学习系统需要真实的训练数据。为此，生成模型被提出用于合成信道数据。然而，（i）这些方法产生的输出可能不对应于物理可行的信道，（ii）输出可能无法提供对相关环境的洞察，以及（iii）训练生成模型可能需要标注数据，从而需要资源密集型的数据标注。通过这项工作，我们通过将基于平面波传播方程导出的参数化物理几何信道（PPGC）建模框架与生成方法相结合，解决了这些问题，以生成具有参数域中可解释表示的真实信道矩阵。为了克服由此产生的非凸优化景观的局限性，我们提出了PPGC模型的线性化重构，以确保训练过程中平滑的梯度流动，同时提供对底层物理环境的洞察。我们将张量分解框架纳入线性化重构中，以允许无线信道参数数量的灵活性。我们还展示了该重构与参数提取任务的兼容性。我们通过与先前基线模型对比，评估生成的场景特定样本与真实信道在相似性以及在下游压缩任务中的效用。

</details>


### [141] [A Kalman Filter-Based Tracking Loop Design for Real-Time Aerospace GNSS Applications with Minimum Pull-Out Probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23925)
*J. Fermín Llorente,Javier A. Smidt,Pedro A. Roncagliolo,Ramón López La Valle*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种修正卡尔曼滤波（mKF）方法，解决硬件相关器处理延迟导致KF跟踪环路最优性丧失的问题，并建立了与单延迟数字锁相环（DPLL）的等效关系，在高动态 aerospace GNSS 应用中实现了更优的鲁棒性与噪声效率。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有卡尔曼滤波跟踪环路在理论上的最优性因实时系统中硬件相关器的处理延迟而受到损害，但已有KF公式通常忽略这一延迟，导致实际性能下降。

Method: 1. 提出修正卡尔曼滤波（mKF），重新设计量测更新以与处理延迟一致；2. 基于拉出概率（POP）的解析表达式，给出过程噪声协方差矩阵和相关时间的系统化调参方法；3. 建立mKF与单延迟数字锁相环（DPLL）在纯数字域的直接等价关系。

Result: 1. 通过蒙特卡洛仿真验证了POP解析表达式的正确性；2. 在GNSS信号模拟器以及实时GPS接收机硬件平台上完成了验证；3. 在相同噪声带宽下，mKF的相位误差方差与DPLL相当，但高阶状态误差更低；4. mKF在最优单延迟DPLL无法锁定的大带宽下仍能保持锁定。

Conclusion: mKF作为一种稳健且低噪声的解决方案，适合用于高动态 aerospace GNSS应用。

Original Abstract: Kalman filter-based (KF-based) tracking loops are a powerful alternative to traditional phase-locked loops (PLLs) for Global Navigation Satellite Systems (GNSS) signal tracking. The primary advantage of the KF is its ability to incorporate high-fidelity models for receiver dynamics and clock errors, allowing the loop to adapt optimally to signal conditions. However, this theoretical optimality is often compromised in practice by the processing delays inherent in real-time systems with hardware correlators, which existing KF formulations typically neglect. This paper introduces a Modified Kalman filter (mKF) that overcomes this limitation specifically for hardware-based architectures. By reformulating the measurement update to be consistent with the processing delays, the proposed mKF maintains optimality in a practical implementation. We further present a systematic method for tuning both the process noise covariance matrix and the correlation time, based on an analytical expression for the pull-out probability (POP), which is validated through Monte Carlo simulation. The mKF is then validated with a GNSS signal simulator, both by post-processing baseband samples and on a real-time GPS receiver with hardware correlators. A direct equivalence between the mKF and a one-delay Digital PLL (DPLL) is established entirely in the digital domain. At equal noise bandwidth, the mKF matches the DPLL's phase error variance while achieving lower error in the higher-order states. Moreover, the mKF sustains lock at bandwidths inaccessible to the optimal one-delay DPLL under the same dynamic stress, positioning the proposed architecture as a robust and noise-efficient solution for high-dynamic aerospace GNSS applications.

中文摘要翻译: 基于卡尔曼滤波（KF）的跟踪环路是全球导航卫星系统（GNSS）信号跟踪中传统锁相环（PLL）的有力替代方案。KF的主要优势在于能够融入高保真的接收机动态和时钟误差模型，从而使环路能够根据信号条件实现最优自适应。然而，在具有硬件相关器的实时系统中，这种理论上的最优性常常因固有的处理延迟而受到损害，而现有的KF公式通常忽略了这一延迟。本文针对硬件架构，提出了一种克服该局限性的修正卡尔曼滤波（mKF）。通过重新设计量测更新以与处理延迟保持一致，所提出的mKF在实际实现中保持了最优性。我们进一步给出了一种系统化的调参方法，基于拉出概率（POP）的解析表达式对过程噪声协方差矩阵和相关时间进行整定，该方法通过蒙特卡洛仿真得到了验证。随后，通过后处理基带样本以及在带有硬件相关器的实时GPS接收机上运行的GNSS信号模拟器，对mKF进行了验证。在纯数字域中，建立了mKF与单延迟数字锁相环（DPLL）的直接等价关系。在相同噪声带宽下，mKF的相位误差方差与DPLL相当，同时高阶状态误差更低。此外，在相同动态应力条件下，mKF能够以最优单延迟DPLL无法达到的带宽维持锁定，这使得所提出的架构成为高动态 aerospace GNSS应用中一种稳健且低噪声的解决方案。

</details>


### [142] [Low-rank Updates in Slowly Time-varying Graphs for Spatial-Temporal Signal Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24011)
*Saghar Bagheri,Gene Cheung,Tim Eadie,Antonio Ortega*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出一种联合优化方法，通过低秩矩阵建模时变图结构的变化，交替插值图信号并估计演化后的邻接矩阵，并利用可展开神经网络进行参数调优，在信号插值任务上优于现有模型。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 对于时空数据中节点间相似性随时间演变的问题，静态空间图无法充分表征这种动态关系，因此需要一种能够捕捉缓慢时变成对关系的图模型。

Method: 将连续两个时刻邻接矩阵的差异矩阵建模为低秩矩阵。给定初始邻接矩阵 $W^{(1)}$，交替优化：固定 $W^{(2)}$ 时通过解线性系统插值信号 $x_2$；固定 $x_2$ 时通过近端梯度下降更新 $W^{(2)}$，其中秩项的近端映射利用快速正交匹配追踪算法在字典上选取稀疏原子组合来近似。将迭代过程展开为轻量级神经网络层以实现有限数据下的参数调优。

Result: 实验表明，所提出的联合优化方法在信号插值任务上优于现有的时变图模型。

Conclusion: 通过低秩先验建模图结构的缓慢时变并联合优化信号插值与图估计，能够有效提升时空图信号处理的性能。

Original Abstract: A crucial assumption in graph signal processing (GSP) is the existence of an underlying graph that captures the pairwise similarities between nodes, allowing filters to be designed based on this graph for tasks such as denoising. For spatial-temporal data in which node-to-node similarities evolve over time, a static spatial graph is insufficient. In this paper, to represent slowly time-varying pairwise relationships, we model the graph changes in two consecutive adjacency matrices $P = W^{(2)} - W^{(1)}$ across time as a low-rank matrix. % Specifically, given an initial adjacency matrix $W^{(1)}$ at time $t=1$, we jointly interpolate a signal $x_2$ and estimate $W^{(2)}$ at $t=2$ using both a graph signal smoothness prior for $x_2$ and a low-rank prior on $¶$. We alternate optimization steps. With $W^{(2)}$ fixed, $x_2$ is interpolated by solving a linear system. Alternatively, holding $x_2$ fixed, $W^{(2)}$ is updated via proximal gradient descent (PGD). The proximal mapping of the rank term $Gamma(W^{(2)} - W^{(1)})$ is approximated in linear time using a fast orthogonal matching pursuit (OMP) algorithm that selects a sparse combination of atoms from a dictionary $cR$ formed by the outer products of $W^{(1)}$'s eigenvectors. We unroll iterations of our algorithm into layers to build a lightweight neural network for limited data-driven parameter tuning. Experiments show that our joint optimization achieves better signal interpolation compared to existing time-varying graph models.

中文摘要翻译: 图信号处理中的一个关键假设是存在一个潜在图来捕捉节点间的成对相似性，从而可基于该图设计用于去噪等任务的滤波器。对于节点间相似性随时间演变的时空数据，静态空间图是不够的。本文为表征缓慢时变的成对关系，将连续两个时刻邻接矩阵的变化 $P = W^{(2)} - W^{(1)}$ 建模为低秩矩阵。具体地，给定时刻 $t=1$ 的初始邻接矩阵 $W^{(1)}$，我们联合插值信号 $x_2$ 并估计时刻 $t=2$ 的 $W^{(2)}$，同时利用 $x_2$ 的图信号平滑先验和 $P$ 的低秩先验。我们采用交替优化步骤：固定 $W^{(2)}$ 时通过解线性系统插值 $x_2$；固定 $x_2$ 时通过近端梯度下降更新 $W^{(2)}$。秩项 $\Gamma(W^{(2)} - W^{(1)})$ 的近端映射通过快速正交匹配追踪算法在由 $W^{(1)}$ 特征向量外积构成的字典上选取稀疏原子组合来近似，计算时间为线性复杂度。我们将算法迭代展开为层，构建一个轻量级神经网络，用于在有限数据下进行参数调优。实验表明，与现有时变图模型相比，我们的联合优化方法实现了更好的信号插值。

</details>


### [143] [A Conditional Timing Protection Level: Holdover-Limited Undetected Time Error Under GNSS Spoofing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.24210)
*Chakshu Baweja*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文针对GNSS授时接收机在欺骗攻击下的时间误差检测问题，提出了三个递进式贡献：现场测量显示欺骗攻击可导致1.01ms时间误差而接收机仅报告51ns（近20000倍差距）；证明了在单自参考时钟辅助监测下不存在有限的无条件时间误差界；提出了时序保护水平（TPL）这一条件性误差界，通过模型无关监测器与跨卫星一致性校验实现。

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 现有GNSS接收机在位置域RAIM（接收机自主完好性监测）无法有效检测欺骗攻击导致的时间误差，因为欺骗攻击产生的慢速共模拉偏不会触发标称几何故障报警，接收机自身的时间精度标志也无法揭示这一威胁。

Method: 通过现场测量记录真实欺骗攻击数据；理论分析证明无条件误差界的不可能性；提出时序保护水平（TPL）作为条件性误差界，由模型无关监测器的静态可检测性下限加上振荡器在检测延迟期间的时间漂移组成，通过开源的Kshana仿真器验证。

Result: 现场测量显示欺骗攻击导致1.01ms时间误差而接收机仅报告51ns（差距约20000倍）；TPL预算在1秒恢复时为114ns，60秒恢复时为458ns，远低于接受的1.01ms；时钟辅助时序检测对慢速斜坡几乎无效（仅在~1ms捕获时报警），而模型无关监测器在斜坡期间即可报警。

Conclusion: 提出了可复现的条件性时间误差界（TPL），校准结果数千倍低于实际攻击误差，但明确该界为校准而非现场验证值，不携带完整性风险预算，且长恢复时间下以区间形式报告。

Original Abstract: A GNSS timing receiver under spoofing has no nominal-geometry fault for position-domain RAIM to bound: the threat is a slow, common-mode pull of served clock time that the receiver's own time-accuracy flag need not reveal. We make three graded contributions. First, a field measurement: solving the receiver clock trajectory from raw L1 pseudoranges and broadcast ephemeris, we show a recorded over-the-air spoof from the public JammerTest 2024 campaign pulled a u-blox ZED-F9P by about 1.01 ms of served time while it reported at most 51 ns, a gap near 20,000x.
